# apps/factory-api — the factory API

The factory's Fastify 5 API: a separate product from Commerce, with its own database, login and Railway projects.
Today the API core: context, contracts, route manifest, security baseline, `GET /health` and `GET /ready`, and
`command()` (`src/core/command.ts`): the ONLY way to write — one transaction for the change, its audit rows, its events
and the idempotency receipt; the outbox (events → BullMQ → handlers and live screens); and the worker and scheduler
(`src/background.ts worker|scheduler`); office and floor sign-in, roles and money grains (`src/platform/auth/`),
one-off links (`src/platform/links/`), the integration platform (gateway, sealed secrets, inbound ledger), files on
R2 (`src/platform/files/`), record threads with the record timeline (`src/platform/threads/`), imports with a dry
run (`src/platform/imports/`), documents and printing (`src/platform/{documents,print}/`), email out through SES
(`src/platform/email-out/`) and email in with "mail to sort" (`src/platform/email-in/`), portal sign-in
(`src/platform/auth/portal/`) and the abuse guard of public routes (`src/platform/abuse/`), below.
Modules: `modules/compliance/certificates` (certificate register and gate `certificateGateFor`; cron `compliance.certificateExpiry`; the notified body's PDF only through `POST /compliance/certificates/:certificateId/pdf` + `…/pdf/:fileId/complete`, pdf.ts).
`modules/compliance/public-doc` (B-W2b-166; PRD-294, PRD-356; factory-domain `compliance/public-doc/{code,rules,contracts}`): the public DoC at `/doc/<code>` on the web — the code is the last 40 bits of a work order's (a released batch) or a style's id, 8 Crockford symbols, no column (`publicDocCodeOf`, `readPublicDocCode`, `publicDocAddress`); PUBLIC `POST /public/doc/lookup` {code, n?, proof?} (abuse guard `public.lookup` per network, no subject; every ACTIVE tenant read in its system scope, `$queryRaw` on the id's last 10 hex digits; one 404 for every miss) and `GET /public/doc/:code/files/:fileId[?download=1]` (only the page's PDFs, `servedFileIds`; streamed, kept in-process); office `GET /compliance/public-doc/address?styleId=|workOrderId=` (compliance.certificates.view). Reads only: no audit, no event.
`modules/compliance/{renewals,type-changes}` (B-W2a-66; QHS-105, PRD-283, PRD-285): the Renewals tab `GET /compliance/certificates/:id/renewals` (the CERTIFICATE_EXPIRING rules as the 180 / 90 / 30-day stages with their timers and alerts, the 12-to-6-month window, the renewal = audit rows `compliance.renewals.start|applied|link` of the Certificate) and `POST …/renewals/start` (platform/tasks createTask, then a command on the certificate's version), `…/applied`, `…/successor` (Certificate.successorId once, a VALID successor of the same holder; under the CERTIFICATE_EXPIRING kind lock: its timers cancelled, its alerts cleared; the evaluator leaves renewed certificates out); `stages.ts` clustered cron `compliance.renewalStages` (hourly) makes the 180- and 90-day rules once per tenant (audit marker); type changes: `detect.ts openTypeChangeReviewsInCommand` = THE hook of `product/bom` releaseRevision (the approved base: the previous RELEASED revision, or its holding review's base), `/compliance/type-changes…` (list, one, notify, not-needed, answer with its PDF through `…/answer-file` only: MODULE_OWNED_FILES TypeChangeReview), `GET /compliance/type-changes/styles/:styleId` (the style tab, a draft compared live), `reviews.ts classLabelHoldFor` = the garment label's hold (compliance/labels preview.ts readCell). compliance.certificates.view | manage.
`modules/inventory/{locations,ledger,stock}`: every stock move goes through `postMoves(tools, drafts, { key })` (ledger/post-moves.ts) inside the caller's command (locks, 409 before below zero, values office/system moves; worker handler `inventory.valueFloorMoves` values floor moves); locations import `inventory.locations`. The warehouse screens' reads (stock/reads-contract.ts, office, inventory.stock.view, read only): `GET /inventory/hides[/:id]` (every hide with its stock, place and `left`; one hide with its cut log, the worker only where the site names workers), `GET /inventory/lots[/:id]` (every lot with stock, documents, work orders; one lot's stock per place and trace: supplier, PO line, receipt, DDT ← → work orders, shipments, customers), `GET /inventory/ledger[/counts|/:id]` (the Movements grid: names, document, both clocks, posted by (a floor move by its device; the worker only in `/:id` where the site names workers), value by the cost grain, `closing` for the running balance), `GET /inventory/stock/explain` (a figure summed by reason; adds up or the gap).
`modules/inventory/locations-depth` (B-W2b-289; SCM-177, SCM-178, SCM-179, SCM-181; factory-domain `inventory/locations-depth/{kinds,labels,blocks,defaults,tree}`, events `inventory-locations/events.ts`): the location TYPES (`GET /inventory/locations/kinds`: group, what each holds ANY | MATERIALS | FINISHED, party, transfer-only, default purposes, labelled, blockable, at the top, container, fixed; W1's create/retype/move and the import apply them: a CUSTOMER place names a CUSTOMER or BRAND party of the company and sits at the top, nothing sits inside an outside/transit place or a pick face, an outside/transit place never retypes); ledger `postMoves` refuses a move INTO a place that does not hold the item's kind (400) and a move in or out of a blocked place but a COUNT (409 `warehouse/LOCATION_BLOCKED`, the database's words, on the database's clock) before the database; BIN LABELS (`PUT /inventory/locations/:id/label-code` suggested `<site>-<code>` or typed, upper-cased, unique in the tenant, `printed` = labels of the old code; `GET …/:id/label` the label + print history; `POST /inventory/locations/labels/print` one LOCATION print job per place (platform/print/layouts/bin.ts, MODULE_LABEL_KINDS + LOCATION), a place without a code given its suggested one first; `POST …/labels/pdf`; `GET /inventory/locations/scan?code=` the place a scan names, office and floor); BLOCKS (`POST …/:id/block|unblock`, `GET /inventory/locations/blocked`; a count of a place, nothing narrowing it, blocks the place and its subtree with reason COUNT and the mark "Count <number> is running here." at start and send-back, lifts only its own at submit and cancel: `blockForCount`/`releaseForCount` in counts.ts); DEFAULT LOCATIONS (`GET|PUT /inventory/locations/defaults`, `GET …/defaults/resolve`; `defaultPlaceFor(client, {siteId, purpose, itemKinds})` read by receipts (RECEIVING), holds ask/quarantine and customer returns (QUALITY), issue (ISSUE first) and return to stock (RETURN when `toLocationId` is left out)). Office writes need inventory.locations.manage; printing print.labels.
`modules/inventory/{bin-moves,putaway,tasks}` (B-W2b-292; SCM-218, SCM-202, SCM-274; WT1:01, WT1:02; factory-domain `inventory/{bin-moves,putaway,tasks}`, events `inventory/tasks/events.ts` context inventory-tasks): moves by two scans for the office and a floor device at its site (inventory.transfer): `GET /inventory/bin-moves/scan?code=` (In your hands: a hide's, a remnant's, a roll's tag or a lot number, tenant-unique, found in that order; where it lies, quality RELEASED|QUARANTINE|BLOCKED|REJECTED|ON_HOLD, whole, the open tasks naming it), `POST /inventory/bin-moves` and `POST /inventory/putaway` {code, placeCode (bin label, else the place's code, of the stock's site only), fromLocationId? (when it lies in several; left out: the task's place, or for a put-away its one dock or quarantine place), quantity? (base unit; a hide, a remnant, a roll and a lot tracked hide by hide move whole), taskId?} = ONE command (`bin-moves/move.ts moveByScan`: refusals in words first — NAME_THE_PLACE, SAME_PLACE, ARCHIVED, QUARANTINE_ONLY (stock not released or on hold only into a QUARANTINE place), WHOLE_ONLY, TOO_MUCH, a frozen count line in the database's words — then TRANSFERs through postMoves (blocked place, kind), then `tasks/lifecycle.ts settleMoveTasksInCommand`: the open PUT_AWAY/MOVE tasks of what moved, from that place, DONE once nothing is left there, else TAKEN by the worker); a roll moves through rolls' `moveRoll` and its tasks in a second command; `POST /inventory/bin-moves/:moveId/undo` (the poster's own scan move, same device and worker or office user, within 2 minutes, once: mirror reversals; the tasks it did are asked again as new OPEN rows). `GET /inventory/putaway/scan` adds the suggestion (`putaway/suggest.ts`: not released → the QUALITY default or the only quarantine place; else the PUT_AWAY default of the kind; else where the lot lies most). The task inbox: floor (floor.work) `GET /inventory/tasks/inbox` (for me, my live WorkerRole roles, anyone; OPEN or taken by me; only kinds whose act I hold, TASK_ACTS; next first; counts per kind), `POST /inventory/tasks/:id/take|give-back|done` {version?} (a QUARANTINE task only with {placeCode} of an active QUARANTINE place: THE act of `quality/holds/depth.ts moveIntoQuarantineInCommand`, shared with the office's confirm, which closes the hold's QUARANTINE tasks in the same command; PUT_AWAY/MOVE by a tap only when nothing is left at from); office `GET /inventory/tasks[?siteId&status&kind&sourceType&sourceId]`, `GET /inventory/tasks/:id` (who took/did it behind `workerGateFor`), `POST /inventory/tasks`, `PATCH …/:id`, `POST …/:id/cancel` (inventory.tasks.manage, the Warehouse template). Sources (`tasks/sources/`, index.ts a merge point; `tasks/handlers.ts` handler `inventory.warehouseTaskSources` + cron `inventory.warehouseTaskTimed` :17 in background.ts): receipts (receipt_posted onto a RECEIVING place → PUT_AWAY per lot; the PO's RECEIVE tasks DONE by the poster; receipt_reversed cancels; lot.released and a MOVED hold released → PUT_AWAY from quarantine), holds (ASKED → one QUARANTINE task to the asked place; follows; released/closed/not asked → cancelled), pickLists (a pick list's reservations → one PICK per work order; done/cancelled when none is ACTIVE), countSchedules (due → one COUNT per schedule; not due → cancelled). Floor-sync kinds inventory.putAway | binMove | task.take | task.giveBack | task.done. Event inventory.warehouse_task_changed (live inventory.stock.view, floor floor.work; never a person: forPerson). PG tests: `tasks/moves-on-the-floor.pg.vitest.test.ts` (the whole seed).
`modules/inventory/transfers` (B-W2b-326; SCM-219, SCM-220; factory-domain `inventory/transfers/{rules,contracts,ddt,permissions}`, events `inventory-transfers/events.ts`; the DDT's PDF `modules/shipping/ddt/transfer.ts`): transfer orders between two sites (or two companies of the tenant), office only, `/inventory/transfer-orders…` (W1's `POST /inventory/transfers`, a move inside one site, is unchanged). Reads (inventory.stock.view at EITHER end): the list + tiles IN_TRANSIT / ARRIVING_TODAY / WITH_DIFFERENCES, `/options` (sites, transit and other places), one transfer (lines with `available` at the pick place while a draft, `inTransit` = Σ the line's moves into its transit place − out of it, the DDT, `actions` held with `refusal` codes), `/:id/stock` (the picker), `GET /:id/ddt/pdf` (presigned). Writes (inventory.transfers.manage; refusals 409 `refusal/<CODE>`): at the SENDING site create (number from its TRANSFER series; transit place = the site's only TRANSIT place, or the one whose code names the to-site, else 400), PATCH (draft: places, days, note; after the ship: expected day, receiving place, tracking — either end), lines add (a hide whole, its place found; never more than the place holds), PATCH a line on ITS version (autosave per cell), remove, cancel, `POST /:id/ship` (ONE command: a TRANSFER per line onto the transit place naming `transferLineId`, shippedQuantity, the DDT number from the company's DDT series (allocateNumber, record TransferOrder), header SHIPPED with the whole DDT; the layout checked BEFORE the number; then the PDF, FISCAL_ARCHIVE file of the transfer shared with the sending site's floor, named once in `ddtFileId`), `POST /:id/ddt/pdf` again; at the RECEIVING site `POST /:id/receive` (per line: the to-site lot (the receiving company's LOT series, quality and Cr VI copied) and a re-tagged hide named on the line first, TRANSFER_OUT off the transit place and TRANSFER_IN at the to-site valued at the same unit cost (LOT_COST), receivedQuantity + a DISCREPANCY reason for a short line; RECEIVED once every line is complete or has its reason, else PARTLY_RECEIVED) and `POST /:id/lines/:lineId/settle` (ADJUSTMENT | CARRIER_CLAIM: an ADJUSTMENT off the transit place naming the line, valued; SENT_BACK: the ship's TRANSFER reversed, only while nothing of the line arrived). Event inventory.transfer_changed (live inventory.stock.view). postMoves' MoveDraft carries `transferLineId`.
`modules/inventory/{pick-lists,substitutions}` (B-W2b-298; SCM-212, MES-139; follow-up PK1:01; factory-domain `inventory/{pick-lists/walk,substitutions/rules}`, events `inventory-substitutions/events.ts`): the PICK LIST BY BIN (office + floor, inventory.issue, read only): `GET /inventory/pick-lists/work-orders/:id` and `GET /inventory/pick-lists/walk?workOrderIds=` (1-20, one walk for the shift's work orders): stops in the site's bin order (`binWalkOrder`: the location tree depth first, places inside one place by natural code order; each stop `turn`, `location` {path, rank, labelCode, blocked}), RESERVED (the work order's ACTIVE reservations where their lot/hide lies, a held lot said in words, not issuable) → SUBSTITUTE (an APPROVED substitute's lot) → PROPOSED (W2a's proposal up to `toPick` = toIssue − approved/issued substitutes − reserved); per work order its lines (`substituted`, `toPick`, `substitutes`), kit place (B-W2b-15) and open PICK task; the PICK task of B-W2b-292's pickLists source names the walk's first bin (`fromLocationId`), the kit's place (`toLocationId`) and "N places by bin path, first X" (`walkStartOf`). SUBSTITUTES WITH A YES (MaterialSubstitution): `GET /inventory/substitutions/options?workOrderId&bomLineId` (office + floor, inventory.issue: the line's need/short/standing/open, each alternate's `alternateUse` answer with the work order's facts, the covering certificate (quality/lab `certificateCovering`), stock lot by lot with quality words, `max`, the approval route as a dry run of the switched-on MATERIAL_SUBSTITUTION rules), `POST /inventory/substitutions` (office + floor, inventory.issue; a floor ask runs as the tenant's system caller naming the worker) = ONE command under the work order's issue lock: refusals `refusal/NOT_OPEN|NOT_ON_THE_LINE|LOT_NOT_USABLE|ONLY_WHEN_SHORT|OUTSIDE_CERTIFICATE|TOO_MUCH|NO_APPROVER` in words, the row WAITING with `ruleAnswer`, then `askApproval` (kind MATERIAL_SUBSTITUTION, platform/approvals/kinds/material-substitution.ts): asked → approvalTaskId; not asked → approved at once when nobody's yes is needed (ALWAYS, WHEN_SHORT while short), else NO_APPROVER (another lot of the own material and WITH_APPROVAL alternates need a person); the yes/no/withdraw/stale of the engine set APPROVED (decidedById = the approver) / REFUSED (the no's code and words) / WITHDRAWN; `POST …/:id/withdraw` (office, inventory.issue, {version, reason}) withdraws it and its pending request; `POST …/:id/issue` (office + floor, inventory.issue) = W1's `issueInCommand` per piece with `plannedAs` (what the substitution allows) then ISSUED with its moveIds; `GET /inventory/substitutions[?siteId&workOrderId&status]` (tiles waiting/approved) and `GET …/:id` (inventory.stock.view). Floor-sync kinds inventory.pick.issue (W1's issue) and inventory.substitute.issue. Event inventory.substitution_changed (live inventory.stock.view, floor floor.work). No new permission. PG test: `pick-lists/pick-lists-and-substitutes.pg.vitest.test.ts` (the whole seed).
`modules/inventory/kits` (B-W2b-15; B-W2b-310, follow-up FT2:01): `POST /inventory/kits/:workOrderId/{stage,complete}` serve a floor device at the work order's site too (inventory.issue; the floor takes the work order's ISSUE lock, it may not lock the WorkOrder row); reading and reopening stay the office's; the walk's `kit.version` is the version the tablet sends.
`modules/inventory/issue` (B-W1-39): `/inventory/picking/…` pick list, issue (a hide whole; a lot by quantity; over-issue only office + inventory.issue.over), return, undo of one's own last issue (office and floor, inventory.issue), office reversal (inventory.reverse; the ledger's generic reverse refuses ISSUE/RETURN); lock order work-order issue lock → `factory.hide-cut` → stock locks (work orders' complete/close/cancel take the issue lock before the row, so an issue never lands on a cancelled one; the DB holds the same rule: …_inventory_issue_work_order_guards); `issueInCommand` for cutting; handler `inventory.startWorkOrderOnIssue` starts a RELEASED work order (production `startOnFirstWork`).
`modules/inventory/{unplanned,reception-report}` (B-W2b-300; SCM-122, SCM-119; factory-domain `inventory/{unplanned,reception-report}/rules`, events `inventory-receiving/events.ts`; perm inventory.unplanned.decide: buyer, warehouse): goods without a PO — floor `GET /inventory/receiving/unplanned/start|items`, `POST /inventory/receiving/unplanned` (inventory.receive at the device's site; ONE floor command: GoodsReceipt kind UNPLANNED, OPEN, no PO, in the holding place (the suggestion = where the last ones waited, else the label scanned), its lines, NO move; then the buyer's Task on the receipt in a system command (`buyer.ts askBuyerAfterFloor`, key derived from the floor's; assignee = the supplier's last PO maker, else the first person with the decide permission), `GET …/unplanned/:id`; the DDT photo through the receipt's ddt-photos route); office `GET /inventory/unplanned/:id` (the supplier's open orders that fit), `POST …/:id/order` (names the PO and posts through `recordReceiptInCommand` with `options.unplanned`: the same receipt, its waiting lines replaced, moves into the holding place; buyer's task DONE; the put-away source treats the holding place as a dock), `…/:id/return` (CANCELLED with why), `…/:id/buyer`; the reception report `GET /inventory/receipts/:id/reception-report` (the site's latest DONE run's pegging of the receipt's PO lines, then open work orders that plan the material; free per lot; `proposeServes`), `POST …/reception-report/reserve` (several demands in ONE command through `reservations/reserve.ts reserveInCommand`; `tellBuyer`) and `…/send` (an internal note on the PO's thread mentioning its maker). PG tests: `unplanned/unplanned.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
`modules/inventory/reservations` (B-W2a-11; SCM-205/209/210): office `GET /inventory/reservations[/availability|/:id]` (inventory.stock.view; `stale` = ACTIVE of a done demand) and `POST /inventory/reservations` (one work order of the site, open incl. ON_HOLD, or one order line; lines = a hide whole (HIDE), an area of a lot or any lot (AREA), a quantity (QUANTITY); only from available = on hand − held − open reservations, for the item+colour AND the lot; refusals `body/lines/<i>/refusal/<CODE>`), `POST /inventory/reservations/release` ({id, version}[] + reason) (inventory.reserve); lock `factory.reserve|tenant|site|item|colour` (store.ts `lockReservations`) taken by reserve, release, write-off and the issue path, after the work order's issue lock and a hide's cut lock, before the stock locks; `consume.ts` is what modules/inventory/issue calls: an issue never takes stock another demand holds (409 `refusal/RESERVED_FOR_OTHER`) and counts against its work order's own (hide → lot → any lot; ISSUED when all issued), its undo/reversal gives back to the ACTIVE ones. Write-off (ledger/write-off.ts, SCM-193): `POST /inventory/write-offs` (inventory.scrap; a WRITE_OFF ReasonCode, note `<CODE>: <note>`, key `inventory.writeOff:<key>:<n>`, a hide whole → SCRAPPED, never a reserved hide), `GET /inventory/write-offs/:id` (id = first move's), photos `POST …/:id/photos` + `…/photos/:fileId/complete` (PHOTO files of record `StockWriteOff`; + files.upload). Stock as of a date: `GET /inventory/stock?asOf=` and `/inventory/stock/explain?asOf=` (moves with occurredAt ≤ asOf; held/reserved null). The Movements grid: `GET /inventory/ledger?q=&writeOffs=` with `rowCount` on the first page.
`modules/inventory/receipts` (B-W1-32): office `/inventory/receipts…` (list (`postedFrom`, `waiting`), `/counts` (the screen's tiles), record, `…/lots` with every hide, `/expected` open PO lines; inventory.stock.view | receive | reverse; a reversal's blockers name their lots/hides and place); record = ONE command: the PO row's version bump → purchasing `receiving.ts` lock `factory.purchase-order-receiving` (purchasing's cancel takes it too and refuses while an OPEN/POSTED receipt names the order) → GOODS_RECEIPT and LOT numbers → hide tags = the tenant's HIDE scan ids (`allocateScanIds`, one ONLINE block) → receipt, lines, lots (QUARANTINE for a Cr VI test, a PPE certificate or the receiver's hold; else RELEASED) and hides → postMoves RECEIPT moves valued at the PO line's price per base unit → POSTED → PO PARTLY_RECEIVED | RECEIVED; beyond the line's tolerance over needs `differenceReason`; reverse = receipt CAS → PO locks → stock locks → refused in words while its stock is issued, moved, scrapped or corrected → mirror moves, lots REJECTED, PO progress read again (rules factory-domain `inventory/receipts/rules`; numbers through `numbers.ts` (`receiptNumber`, `lotNumber`, and `numberReceipt` for B-W1-33's floor command: GR + LOT numbers, office or floor, lots before any hide tag); `recordReceiptInCommand(tools, body, key, options)` is also the floor's work, below).
`modules/inventory/receipts` W2a (B-W2a-31; SCM-115/118/123/248): the supplier's delivery note photo `POST /inventory/receipts/:id/ddt-photos` (+ `/:fileId/complete`; office with files.upload, and the floor at the device's site through the tenant's system caller holding files.upload + files.manage over the receipt's company and site: OFFICE + FLOOR file); discrepancies `…/:id/discrepancies` (GET stock.view; POST, PATCH and DELETE while OPEN, `…/:id/status` AGREED|CREDITED|CLOSED, photos: inventory.discrepancies.manage; the DISCREPANCY reason code in ReceiptDiscrepancy.reasonCode (B-W2b-14; one recorded before W2b still reads its note prefix `<CODE>: …`); lock `factory.receipt-discrepancy|company|receipt`); the report to the supplier `…/discrepancy-report` (GET the PDF preview, GET `/check`, POST: inventory.discrepancies.report; a PDF without prices, a DOCUMENT of the GoodsReceipt shared with its party, mailed on the receipt's thread through email out; OPEN → SENT); the supplier's unit at receipt (lineInput receivedQuantity/receivedUom/receivedFactor, office default = the approved source's unit, the floor names it; moves post in the base unit; the line's quantity in the order line's unit); the tannery hide list = importer `inventory.hideList` (one sheet, one order, ONE receipt: its apply is one batch whose check keeps the rows it passed and the first write records them all through recordReceiptInCommand) + `GET /inventory/receipts/hide-lists/:importId` (totals against the order; the receipt it made).
`modules/inventory/receipts` "Request from supplier" (B-W2a-F4; Owner answer 1A: a narrow route, NOT email.send for the warehouse): `GET|POST /inventory/receipts/:id/documents/request` (inventory.receipts.documents.request: warehouse, buyer, owner; `document-request.ts`): what the lots still wait for (factory-domain `inventory/receipts/document-request` wantedDocuments: a quarantined lot's pending Cr VI report, a PPE component's certificate, a DDT neither numbered nor photographed), recipients ONLY the supplier's active contacts with an e-mail by id (no address, no cc), the text = documentRequestMail in the supplier's language + an optional note (500), no attachment; ONE command `inventory.requestReceiptDocuments` → email out `sendEmail` on the receipt's thread (QUEUED, the worker delivers) + audit `inventory.receiptDocumentsRequested`; refused in words: reversed/cancelled (409 invalid_state), nothing missing / no contact with an e-mail (409, paths `documents` / `contactIds`), a contact not the supplier's or a document not missing (400 `body/contactIds/i`, `body/documents/i`).
`modules/inventory/receiving` (B-W1-33; SCR-092): floor only (inventory.receive at the device's site; the operator and team_leader floor templates hold it) `GET /inventory/receiving/orders[?code=]` (what the site expects; a scanned number finds its order, `found` in any state), `…/orders/:orderId` (open lines with upTo/holds/area units, the dock, the hold place, the station's label printer; never a price), `POST …/orders/:orderId/receipts` {version, lines, printerId?, language?} and `GET|POST /inventory/receiving/receipts/:receiptId[/tags]` (a reprint logs `inventory.hideTagReprinted` with the reason); three transactions: (1) a FLOOR command running `recordReceiptInCommand` (purchasing `readOrderForFloorReceipt`: the receiving lock, then the order read under it with the screen's version, else 409; no price read; held lots to the site's one QUARANTINE place; moves without a cost, the worker's `inventory.valueFloorMoves` values a RECEIPT move at its PO line's `unitCostPerBase`), (2) `progress.ts` the PO move in the tenant's system context (`asTenantSystem`: version bump → lock → orderStatusAfter over every posted receipt; key derived from the request's, so a resend mends it), (3) `tags.ts` one HIDE label print job per hide on the station's label printer (`hide-tag.<hide>` once); a failure of (2) or (3) never undoes (1). The supplier's name comes from the system scope (the floor reads no Party).
`modules/inventory/hides` (B-W2b-94; PRD-031, SCM-249, SCM-250, SCM-258; office only; factory-domain `inventory/hides/{usable,measure,grading,area}`): an article's grade rules `GET|POST /product/materials/:materialId/grades`, `PATCH …/grades/:ruleId` (product.materials.view | manage over the material's company; one active per article and grade, retired never deleted); a hide's grade and shade group BY RECORD `GET|PATCH /inventory/hides/:hideId/grading` (inventory.hides.manage; gradeId + grade text, shadeGroupId + shade text in one update: the database refuses a text that disagrees, so nothing writes the text alone; reason once cut; CONSUMED/SCRAPPED/RETURNED read-only); re-measure `GET|POST /inventory/hides/:hideId/measures` (a FULL hide only; HideMeasure append-only via `insertMeasureInCommand`; `correctStock` = one ADJUSTMENT move to the measured area, needs inventory.adjust; Hide.area never changes); `GET /inventory/lots/:lotId/area-check` (latest measure per hide against the tannery's area, tolerance = the lot's PO line `areaTolerancePct` else 2 %, a SHORT lot gives the POST /purchasing/claims body); `GET /inventory/leather/grade-mix` (per article or lot: hides beside area, left = the site's on hand, usable = left × rule share else grade share, ungraded apart, remnants as pieces). Receipts and opening stock name the grade record when the text is an active grade's code (`gradeIdsByCode`). Events inventory.grade_rule_changed, hide_graded, hide_measured.
`modules/inventory/rolls` (B-W2b-107; SCM-260, SCM-261, SCM-263; factory-domain `inventory/rolls/{rolls,protectors,consumables}`, events in `inventory-rolls/events.ts`): fabric rolls `GET /inventory/rolls[/:id|/lookup?code=]`, `POST /inventory/lots/:lotId/rolls` (tag what a place holds of the lot that no roll claims: inventory.receive, office + floor; ROLL scan ids), `PATCH /inventory/rolls/:id` (width, shade group, shrinkage, defects, end of roll: inventory.rolls.manage), `POST /inventory/rolls/:id/issue|return|move|scrap|correct` (the lot's ISSUE through issue `issueInCommand` / RETURN of a CONSUMED roll's end / TRANSFER / SCRAP / ADJUSTMENT with the roll's lengthLeft, state and place in the SAME command; lock order roll row → `factory.fabric-rolls|tenant|lot` → work order issue lock → stock locks); protector lots `GET /inventory/work-orders/:id/protector-lots` (office) + `GET /inventory/protector-lots/work-orders/:id` (floor), `POST /inventory/protector-lots` (inventory.protectors.record, office + floor; certificate and quality state are warnings, never refusals), `POST …/:rowId/withdraw` (office), `GET /inventory/lots/:lotId/protector-recall` (quality.lots.view; batches, garments, issued without a record, the PPE register's trace concern); consumables `GET /inventory/consumables?siteId=` (packs beside the base unit, the site's MIN_MAX levels from planning's item parameters, reorder in whole packs) and `PUT /inventory/consumables/:itemId/pack` (product.materials.manage).
`modules/quality/incoming` (B-W2b-98; QHS-025, QHS-028, QHS-034, MES-054; office + floor at its own site, quality.incoming.view | record; factory-domain `quality/incoming/rules`): `GET /quality/incoming` (list, no person), `GET /quality/incoming/lots/:lotId` (scan: the OPEN one, else what opening gives), `POST /quality/incoming` (ONE command under the lot's advisory lock `factory.incoming-inspection|tenant|<lot>`, the SAME key as B-W2b-101's rule handler; a second OPEN one of a lot refused in words; the plan named or the most specific INCOMING plan that applies, its APPROVED version frozen; hides counted, else the receipt's quantity; sample by `aqlPlanFor`, a fixed sample, or the whole lot; INCOMING_INSPECTION number), `GET …/:id` (the record: sample in words, AQL result, hides with area against the tannery, shade, grade, marks, plan checks, the area check, what the bench picks from, after the decision `next`), `PUT|DELETE …/:id/hides/:hideId` (a WHOLE hide; a measure through inventory/hides `insertMeasureInCommand`; found counts from the marks, a hide once under its worst class), `PUT …/:id/results`, `POST …/:id/decide` (step-up / PIN; accepted + rejected + to sort = the lot; at the decision the hides take their grade and shade group as records and the marks become HideMark rows; the lot never moves: the lab's release with the typed code, Owner answer W3), `…/cancel`; events `incoming_inspection.*` (factory-domain `quality-incoming/events.ts`).
`modules/quality/incoming` B-W2b-111 (QHS-032, QHS-033, QHS-036; factory-domain `quality/incoming/units`): `PUT …/:id/rolls` (4-point counters, width, length; keyed by the roll's tag `rollId` or the supplier's `rollRef`; a lot counted in m/cm/mm only, its sample a number of rolls; the DB writes points per 100 sq yd), `DELETE …/:id/rolls/:rollResultId`, `PUT|DELETE …/:id/components/:seq` (kind, marking, protector type A/B, size, the PPE register's certificate; a tablet reads the register as the tenant's system caller), `PUT …/:id/counters` (the AQL counter of a lot not counted by hide), `GET /quality/incoming/receipts/:receiptId` + `POST /quality/incoming {subcontractReceiptId}` (goods back from a workshop; the decision takes `rejectedLines`, nothing to sort, and releases the receipt in its command: `purchasing/subcontracting/inspection.ts releaseReceiptInCommand`; on a tablet the workshop commands run as the tenant's system caller); the receive command opens the receipt's inspection when a GOODS_RECEIPT rule fits and then holds its bundles (no outside report, no fee `cost-source.ts`, not in the invoice match `payables/matching.ts`, close refused, the late line report refused: `subcontracting/held.ts`).
`modules/inventory/remnants` (B-W2b-100; SCM-254, SCM-206, SCM-214, SCM-257; factory-domain `inventory/remnants/rules`, `inventory/reservations/{shade,find}`): a usable offcut is stock with its own tag (RM-…): a part of a hide back from a work order (the pick's return, Hide done at the cutting table) is a REMNANT move (RETURN's twin; every reader of ISSUE/RETURN takes it, costing posts it as MATERIAL) plus a Remnant row naming it (`lifecycle.ts tagRemnantInCommand`, `allocateScanIds(tools, 'REMNANT', 1)`), refused below MINIMUM_REMNANT (10 dm², no setting column: `refusal/REMNANT_TOO_SMALL`); the row follows its hide inside the issue, return, undo, reversal, cut, write-off and transfer commands (IN_STOCK → ISSUED → USED, VOID after its move's reversal, `refusal/REMNANT_GONE_ON` while a piece went on; postMoves moves its place with a TRANSFER). Routes `GET /inventory/remnants[/:id]` (inventory.stock.view), `GET /inventory/remnants/lookup?code=` (office + floor, inventory.issue), `POST /inventory/remnants` (tag a W1 remnant, inventory.issue). One shade lot per order (`reservations/shade.ts shadeRuleFor`: ORDER by default, no setting column; `heldShades` = issued net + ACTIVE HIDE reservations): the issue and the reserve refuse another shade lot or an out-of-shade hide (`refusal/SHADE_LOT` | `OUT_OF_SHADE`) unless the office gives `shadeOverrideReason` with inventory.shade.override; cutting's scan skips it (`issueInCommand(…, { shade: false })`: B-W2b-103's warning). `GET /inventory/reservations/find-hides` (inventory.stock.view): a work order's leather need (planned − issued − reserved) or an area, hides by shade lot with ONE proposed and the running area. The pick list carries `shadeRule`, per leather line `hides` {count} and `shadeLots`, per candidate shade, `sameShade` and its `remnant` tag; a scanned RM tag issues its hide. Events inventory.remnant_tagged, inventory.remnant_changed.
`modules/inventory/{why,posting,low-stock}` + `stock/adjust-approval.ts` (B-W2b-314; SCM-191, SCM-196, SCM-281): `GET /inventory/why` the balance bridge of a figure over posting days (start → each reason → end, each step the `/inventory/ledger` query of its moves); warehouse rules `GET /inventory/rules`, `PUT /inventory/rules/{posting-dates,adjustment-approval,low-stock-alerts}` (inventory.rules.manage; loosening asks the reason + step-up; the value with the cost grain); `postingDate` on the ledger's office writes = `postMoves(…, { postingDate })` (back-dating limit in words, the period lock reads the day; `GET /inventory/posting/check`); an adjustment above the value with a STOCK_ADJUSTMENT rule on: the first run rolls back (AdjustmentAboveLimit), the second asks (202 `waiting`), the yes posts it (platform/approvals/kinds/stock-adjustment.ts); `GET /inventory/adjustments/waiting`; `GET /inventory/low-stock` + the LOW_STOCK alert finder (`low-stock/alerts.ts`).
`modules/inventory/counts-depth` (B-W2b-299; SCM-226, SCM-227, SCM-228, SCM-229, SCM-293; factory-domain `inventory/counts-depth`, events `inventory-counts/events.ts`): count schedules `GET|POST /inventory/count-schedules`, `PATCH …/:id`, `GET …/due` (tiles overdue/today/this week + rows), `GET …/abc` (ABC classes by issue value, share with the cost grain), `POST …/:id/start` (the counts' own create, started, frozen by default); a closed count moves every schedule it covered (`counts-depth/close.ts`, called by the counts' apply: every key the schedule held at the snapshot is a line; next = today + days; its COUNT task DONE); freeze = `freeze` on `POST /inventory/stock-counts` (lines frozenAt; the DB refuses moves of a frozen line's item at its place), `GET /inventory/stock-counts/frozen`; counting by scan (inventory.counts.scan, office + floor): `GET /inventory/stock-counts/to-count`, `GET …/bin?placeCode`, `POST …/:id/scans` (a hide/remnant/roll tag with its own area or length, a lot label or the number pad with `quantity`; an extra unit makes an ADDED line; a tag twice per pass ALREADY_COUNTED; a RECOUNT by the counter OWN_RECOUNT), `GET …/:id/bins/:locationId` (Bin done), `POST …/finish` (each line = Σ scans; a line over the recount rule has its recount asked: by a second SYSTEM command after a floor finish); approval kind COUNT_VARIANCE (`platform/approvals/kinds/count-variance.ts`; asked at the submit when a rule holds; the counts' approve/apply refuse IN_APPROVALS while it waits); separation switches `GET|PUT /inventory/rules/separation-of-duties` (inventory.rules.manage; off asks reason + step-up); the counter rule (counts approve, scans recount) and the receiver rule (`payables/matching.ts receiverRefusals`: approve, accept a difference). Floor-sync kinds inventory.count.scan | count.finishBin. PG: `counts-depth/counts-depth.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
`modules/shipping/{settings,shipments,packing,ddt}` (B-W1-46, B-W1-47): site settings, office shipments from released garments only (`/shipping/ready`, `/shipping/shipments…`, packing list PDF) and packing for office and floor (`/shipping/packing…`, shipping.pack); every write is a command on the Shipment (its version bump locks the row), then `cells.ts` takes the `factory.ship-cell` locks sorted and answers 409 before the trigger; the DDT (office: `…/ddt/preview`, `POST …/ddt` with optional `ship`, `POST …/ship`, `/shipping/ddts…` read, PDF, cancel; shipping.ddt.issue | cancel) takes its number with allocateNumber inside the issuing command on the Shipment (one live DDT per shipment under that lock), is append-only (a cancellation keeps the number; refused while an issued invoice's line names the DDT, DRAFT ones only warn) and its PDF (FISCAL_ARCHIVE) is stored after the commit (`POST /shipping/ddts/:id/pdf` prints a missing one). DDTs for other reasons (B-W2a-17, `ddt/sources*.ts`; LOG-056): `POST /shipping/ddt-sources/:sourceType/:sourceId/ddt[/preview[/pdf]]` (SubcontractOrder kit WORK_ON_ACCOUNT, SupplierClaim RETURN, RepairOrder REPAIR | RETURN_FROM_WORK, ReturnAuthorisation RETURN; OTHER in words) under the SOURCE's row lock (`lockSource`, FOR UPDATE, never a version bump), one live DDT per source line; other flows issue inside their own command with `issueSourceDdtInCommand(tools, company, source, body)` then `printSourceDdt` after the commit; the record, list (`sourceType`, `sourceId`, `kind`), PDF and cancel routes take any DDT.
`platform/privacy` (B-W1-36; GDPR arts. 13, 30, 35; L. 300/1970 art. 4 c.3): notice versions per company and kind (WORKER_FLOOR | OFFICE_STAFF | PRIVACY_PUBLIC; DRAFT → PUBLISHED (step-up; the one in force SUPERSEDED, frozen) per language a text and/or PDF with its SHA-256), the DPIA (stored with its signed PDF, step-up), the record of processing activities (`/privacy/notices…`, `/privacy/dpia…`, `/privacy/register…`, `/privacy/overview`; privacy.view | manage), the worker notice on the floor (`/privacy/floor/notice[/acknowledge]`, floor.work; sign-in and `/floor/me` carry `notice` {due, blocksScans}), the staff notice once per version (`/privacy/staff-notice[/acknowledge]`, any office session; `/auth/office/me` carries `staffNotice`), the public notice for anyone (`GET /privacy/public/:companyId`); `read.ts`: `acknowledgedInForce` (the scan gate: only an acknowledgement of the worker notice in force counts, none while the company has none in force; production `subject.ts` uses it, the …_privacy_guards triggers hold it), `floorPrivacyReady(tx, companyId, now)` and `floorReadinessFor(tx, siteId, now)` (DPIA + worker notice + B-W1-37's floor rules, in words; office scope or the system caller).
`modules/people/privacy` (B-W2b-27; PLT-207, PLT-209, ANA-075, MES-249, MES-281, MES-282; Statuto art. 4 c.1-3; GDPR arts. 5, 13, 22, 35): legalGate(site, purpose) = the PeopleLegalGate row of the site and purpose (ATTENDANCE | PERFORMANCE) with no switch-off; none = OFF (every site ships closed). For later People reads: `gate.ts` `legalGateFor` / `requireLegalGate` (409 invalid_state `gate/<PURPOSE>` in words) / `legalGateReader(caller, …)` (a floor caller reads the gate as the tenant's system caller: the floor role has no grant on it), `access.ts` `personFiguresAccess(caller, employeeId, purpose)` (SELF · FIGURE_ROLE: a role named in PeopleSettings.figureRoleKeys that grants people.figures.view over the person's company and site, held live and through the session · LEAD: lead of a team the person is in today, only where the gate's basis covers leads and leadsSeeFigures; GATE_CLOSED · NOT_ALLOWED; 404 out of reach) + `requirePersonFigures` + `openPersonFiguresInCommand` (audit people.openPersonFigures: who, whose, purpose, reason), `groups.ts` `minimumGroupSizeFor` / `hideSmallGroups` / `refusePersonKeys`, `decide.ts` `assertPersonDecides(context, decision)` (403 `decidedBy/<DECISION>` for the system caller). Office routes `/people/privacy` (people.privacy.view: sites × purposes with readiness and drift notes), `/rules`, `/sites/:siteId` (DPIA record, history), `POST …/sites/:siteId/purposes/:purpose/switch-on` (people.privacy.manage + reason + step-up; records the company's PUBLISHED worker notice and STORED DPIA; PERFORMANCE takes the site's basis from Production › Settings), `…/switch-off` (reason, never a step-up; a gate dated later than now 409), `…/leads` (widening: basis covers leads + reason + step-up), `GET|POST /people/privacy/companies/:companyId` (minimum group size ≥ 3, figure roles = office Role keys; lowering or naming another role = reason + step-up), `GET /people/privacy/what-you-see?siteId&purpose` (any office person over a site their grants reach: the PrivacyNote words), `GET /people/privacy/figures/:employeeId/access?purpose`, `POST …/open` (people.figures.view). Events legal_gate.changed, people_settings.privacy_changed (live people.privacy.view). B-W2b-77: an office route that takes a person's id asks `figuresAccessBehindGate` (null = the closed gate's answer, the same for a real person and an id that finds nobody; no site in it), and a command's answer names no person's figure: its receipt keeps it past the gate, so the figures are read after the command (`people.openPersonEfficiency`, `production.labourSheetOpen`, `production.labourDisputeAnswer`).
`modules/people/privacy/worker-gate.ts` (B-W2b-F3, B-W2b-F4; GATE-LABOUR, Statuto art. 4 c.1): THE rule of every office read of who did the work (a scan, a cut, a tick, a floor move): `workerGateFor(db, siteId)` → with the site's PERFORMANCE gate not open no name and no id, the answer says `worker: null`, `workerHidden: true` (`askedByHidden`, `leadHidden`, `byHidden`, `cutByHidden`, `createdByHidden`, `postedByHidden`, `actorHidden`, `authorHidden`); open: the read as before. A list over sites: `workerGatesFor(db, siteIds)`, each site's gate read once, never per row. An author column names an office user or a worker: `who.author(id, isWorker)`, a floor move by its device, a column with no device by `workersAmong(db, ids)`. A filter by author obeys the same gate (`GET /inventory/moves?createdById=`). A new office read of who did the work uses it; never a local read of the gate. Tests: `people/privacy/testing/performance-gate.ts` `switchOnPerformance(admin, place)`.
`modules/people/skills` (B-W2b-39; MES-241, MES-244, MES-246, MES-024; office only but the floor check; factory-domain `people/skills/{rules,qualification,permissions}`, events in `people/events.ts`): skills `GET|POST /people/skills`, `PATCH …/:skillId`, `…/archive|restore` (an operation, a class of machine or a general skill; of a company, or of the group with a grant over every company); levels `POST /people/skills/levels` {employeeId, skillId, level, since?, traineeUntil?, version? (of the level in force)} (a changed level names who assessed it again; the same level writes nothing; a trainee period is never cleared while open), `…/levels/:levelId/sign-off` (a signing act: requireStepUp; the trainer holds the skill at TRAINER; optional new level), `…/levels/:levelId/remove`; authorisations `POST /people/skills/authorisations` (one in force per person and class: an ENDED one is closed by the new one, revoked with "Ended on …"), `…/:authorisationId/valid-until`, `…/revoke`; reads `GET /people/skills/matrix` (rows = active people grouped line › team, cells = levels and authorisations in force, tiles count cells; no per-person figure), `GET /people/employees/:employeeId/skills`, `GET /people/skills/cell` (rows + audit history); every decision asks `assertPersonDecides` first and refuses SELF_DECISION (people.skills.view | manage, people.authorisations.manage). THE qualification check `check.ts qualificationCheck(db, {employeeId|workerId, operationId|workOrderOperationId, machineId|stationId})` (office or floor scope) → decision ALLOW | WARN | HOLD by the site rule (`rule.ts qualificationRuleFor`: the site's ProductionSettings.qualificationRule, B-W2b-81; default WARN) and the machine classes that ask an authorisation (`requiredClassesOf`: MachineClass.authorisationRequired, set with `PUT /people/skills/machine-classes/:machineClassId/authorisation-rule`, B-W2b-139; a missing one always holds, never overridden; so does a course the class asks that is expired or missing past its due day: TRAINING_EXPIRED | TRAINING_MISSING, kind AUTHORISATION, B-W2b-139); `assertAssignable(check, overrideReason)` for dispatch, `qualificationGuard(check)` for the tablet, `machineSignInCheck` for sign-in at a station; routes `GET /people/skills/check` (office), `GET /people/skills/floor/check` (floor.work; another worker: floor.lead), `GET /people/skills/line-check` (the loan dialog's "Can do n of m operations on line L"). Events skill.changed, employee_skill.changed, machine_authorisation.changed.
`modules/people/piecerates` (B-W2b-34; MES-254, MES-255, MES-262; c.c. arts. 2100-2101; GATE-PIECE-PAY; factory-domain `people/piecerates/{rates,switch,permissions}`): rate tables in versions (`/people/piece-rates/tables…`, `/versions/:id…`; a DRAFT with why and its rates, SMV × €/min rounded to 6 decimals or fixed, SMV from the operation when not typed; from PROVISIONAL on a rate never changes: a new version with a reason), `POST /versions/:id/publish` (to ACTIVE workers of the site or the line's by home line, floor and/or printed; a DRAFT goes in force in the same command, its first day never in the past; the open version before ends the day before, SUPERSEDED), `…/put-in-force`, `…/confirm` (a provisional rate from the last day of its trial; bulk `POST /versions/confirm` all or none + `/preview`), `…/cancel`, `/publications/:id/withdraw` (DRAFT only), `/publications/:id/paper-receipts` (PAPER receipts); reads `GET /people/piece-rates` (tiles), `/rates` (the grid), `/tables[/:id]`, `/versions/:id` (AckTracker: names inside this record only), `/versions/:id/publish/preview`; floor `GET /people/piece-rates/floor/mine` + `POST …/floor/publications/:id/read` (the worker's own; the PIN again; read and written in the tenant's system scope: the pay tables are office-only); the legal switch `GET|PUT /people/piece-rates/sites/:siteId/switch[/preconditions]`, `POST …/start` (people.piecerates.switch + step-up; refused 409 `preconditions/<CODE>` in words until SITE_BASIS, PERFORMANCE_GATE, WORKER_NOTICE, DPIA, CCNL, CONSULTANT, SCHEME, RATES_PUBLISHED, EARNING_CODES are met), `…/stop` (no step-up); `stopPiecePayInCommand` (switch.ts) is called by privacy's PERFORMANCE switch-off in the same command. Every rate is `money.pay` (the pay grain: owner template, HR custom roles, floor templates for their own records). Events piece_rate_table.changed, piece_rate_version.changed, rate_publication.changed, rate.read, piece_pay.switched (live people.piecerates.view).
`modules/people/schemes` (B-W2b-38; MES-253, MES-257, MES-258, MES-259; c.c. arts. 2100-2101 and the CCNL minimum; GATE-PIECE-PAY; under people.piecerates.view | manage; factory-domain `people/schemes/{rules,pay}`): incentive schemes of a company or of one of its lines (a line's own wins on it; a company's is kept only by a grant reaching every site of the company) `GET|POST /people/incentive-schemes`, `GET|PATCH|DELETE /people/incentive-schemes/:id` (a DRAFT changes freely, a column of another kind is cleared; deleted only while no site's piece-pay switch names it), `POST …/:id/activate` (DRAFT → ACTIVE with its guaranteed basis and rate per hour, first day today or later and in no LOCKED pay period; the open scheme before it of the same company and line ends the day before and is RETIRED in the same command, under `factory.pay.scheme`), `POST …/:id/end` (an ACTIVE one's last day once, with why; refused while piece pay is ON with it), `POST …/:id/example` ("Try the scheme" on made-up figures: make-up, waiting, rework and scrap, bonus, team split); every answer lists the pay periods its days touch (OPEN accrues, PREVIEW / REVIEW run again, LOCKED never). The period run (B-W2b-43) computes with `people/schemes/pay` (`makeUpOf`, `earnedForMakeUp`, `defectPayOf`, `paidTimeOf`, `teamSplitOf`, `bonusOf`, `periodPayOf`) and finds the scheme of a day with `schemeFor` (rules.ts). Rates are `money.pay`. Event incentive_scheme.changed (live people.piecerates.view).
`modules/people/clock` (B-W2b-33; MES-223, MES-224, MES-227, MES-239, MES-199; floor only; factory-domain `people/clock/{rules,permissions}`, events in `people/events.ts`): every path asks the site's ATTENDANCE legal gate first (`requireLegalGate`, 409 `gate/ATTENDANCE`; nothing of a person is read or recorded while off). `GET /people/clock/tablet` (B-W2b-67; public: the device token) answers only {open}: a tablet nobody is signed in on says the clock is closed before anyone scans a badge. `GET|POST /people/clock/floor` (floor.work: the signed-in worker's own clock; source BADGE or NAME_PIN + FORGOT_BADGE by how the session began), `POST /people/clock/scan` (public like the floor sign-in: device token + CSRF, per-device rate limit; B-W2b-52's `refuseByMode`: never on a DISPLAY, a PHONE only its own person; a badge alone at every site, or name + PIN through the sign-in's `provedWorker` / `claimProved`; `occurredAt` = a badge kept offline, OFFLINE at the tablet time; a name and PIN never offline); floor-sync kind `people.clock.record` (`clockFromQueue`: OFFLINE at the tablet time, CLOCK_SKEW when `sentAt` was > 5 min off, future > 2 min refused CLOCK_AHEAD, closed month MONTH_CLOSED). One command `people.clock` per action (key `<device>.<client event id>`; advisory lock `factory.people.clock|tenant|employee`; a badge again within 60 s answers the event before), events attendance.clock_event (live people.attendance.view, floor floor.work) and clock.out (listeners: B-W2b-40); a clock-out ends the worker's running off-standard block (`endRunningBlock(…, 'CLOCK_OUT')`). Cron `people.autoClockOut` (every 5 min, auto.ts): an open stretch past its shift end (B-W2b-32's `planningFor`, published rows: roster cell, else the default shift) + PeopleSettings.autoClockOutAfterMinutes gets an AUTO OUT at that time with the rule in words (key `clock-auto:<clock-in id>`), not where the gate is off or the month closed.
`modules/people/attendance` (B-W2b-37; MES-225, MES-226, MES-228, MES-233, MES-238; office only; factory-domain `people/attendance/{rules,absences,presence,permissions}`, events in `people/events.ts` `attendanceEvents`): every person read and write asks the site's ATTENDANCE gate first (`requireAttendance` → B-W2b-27 `requireLegalGate`, 409 `gate/ATTENDANCE`); codes and settings carry no person (no gate). THE day computation `compute.ts dayFactsOf` / `syncDayInCommand(tools, person, date, {change, fix?, manual?})` (clock events of the day before to two days after, B-W2b-32's `planningFor` PUBLISHED rows else the default shift, PeopleSettings rounding/grace/overtime unit/fixed breaks, WorkingTimeRule BREAK_AFTER + DAILY_REST, absences → domain `computeDay`; writes AttendanceDay keyed (employeeId, date) + AttendanceAnomaly raise/resolve (CLEARED by the system, FIXED by a person's fix with the reason), status OPEN | NEEDS_FIX; APPROVED/CLOSED never recomputed; under the clock's advisory lock `factory.people.clock|tenant|employee`). Reads `GET /people/attendance/days` (siteId, from/to ≤ 62 d, teamId, lineId, status, anomaly, manual, cursor: rows WITHOUT name or minutes; `employeeId`/`groupBy=employeeId` → 400 `query/…` W1), `…/counts` (tiles; a selection under the minimum group size → all null), `…/days/:dayId` (one record: name, events with source words, minutes, anomalies + rule words, absences, holds, actions), `GET /people/employees/:employeeId/attendance` (people.attendance.view). Writes (people.attendance.manage; assertPersonDecides ATTENDANCE_CORRECTION; never one's own day 409 `refusal/OWN_DAY`; closed month MONTH_CLOSED; approved day DAY_APPROVED): `POST /people/attendance/entries` (manual in/out (offSite) and/or absence + reason → MANUAL ClockEvent, attendance.clock_event + clock.out source MANUAL), `…/days/:dayId/fix` (ADD_EVENT | VOID_EVENT | SET_ABSENCE | REMOVE_ABSENCE + reason + version), `…/days/:dayId/absence` (inline code of a day with no clock events; HAS_CLOCK_EVENTS otherwise), `…/days/:dayId/reopen` (+ reason), `…/anomalies/:id/accept` (+ note); approve (people.attendance.approve, ATTENDANCE_APPROVAL; recomputed first; 409 `refusal/HELD` + `holds/<CODE>`) `…/days/:dayId/approve`, `…/days/approve` (one command per day). Absence codes `GET|POST /people/attendance/absence-codes`, `PATCH …/:codeId`, `…/archive|restore`, `…/standard` (people.attendance.settings; group codes need a grant over every company); rounding `GET|PUT /people/attendance/settings` (version null creates PeopleSettings). `GET /people/attendance/who-is-in` (view or rollcall; counts per site/area/line, groups under the minimum emptied), `POST /people/attendance/roll-call` (people.attendance.rollcall; names; audit people.attendanceRollCall). Handler `people.attendanceDays` (attendance.clock_event) and cron `people.attendanceDays` (*/15, yesterday + today of the open sites), jobs.ts. Events attendance.day_changed, attendance.anomaly_raised, absence_code.changed, people_settings.attendance_changed (live people.attendance.view).
`modules/people/overtime` (B-W2b-42; MES-229, MES-230; office only; factory-domain `people/overtime/{rules,timesheets,permissions}`, events in `people/events.ts`, decisions OVERTIME_DECISION and TIMESHEET_APPROVAL): overtime entries computed by the handler `people.overtimeEntries` (attendance.day_changed; `sync.ts` is the one place; DAY, NIGHT, HOLIDAY; never approves; a decided entry whose figures moved is taken back), read at `GET /people/overtime/entries`, `/counts`, `/entries/:entryId`, decided by `POST …/approve|reject|reopen` (people.overtime.review; only on an approved day, never one's own; a BLOCK limit needs `overrideReason`); the last decided entry of an APPROVED request makes it DONE. Timesheets `GET /people/timesheets`, `/:timesheetId`, `POST /people/timesheets/prepare`, `POST …/lead-approve|hr-approve|send-back` (people.timesheets.team over the teams one led in the period: the days and no hours; .lead anyone in reach; .hr the second approval, another person). Lists carry no name, no minutes, no person key; every read and write behind the ATTENDANCE gate. `timesheets.ts refuseCoveredDay`: a day an approved timesheet covers is not reopened.
`modules/people/efficiency` (B-W2b-44; MES-247, MES-250, MES-251, ANA-016, ANA-017; office only; factory-domain `people/efficiency/{rules,permissions}`, event in `people/events.ts`): per line and per operation, NEVER per person (Statuto art. 4; Owner answers W1, F2): `GET /people/efficiency` (siteId, from/to ≤ 62 d, lineId, compare=previous: tiles and one row per line: earned ÷ attended, ÷ minutes on standard work, ÷ planned; attended and planned only with the site's ATTENDANCE gate on), `/trend` (per line and day; target = Planning's efficiency), `/operations` (per style's routing step: SMV, actual person-minutes per piece from B-W2b-40's closed stretches, bundles, difference, sparkline, state; a bundle a trainee worked on and one-tap reports not counted), `/operations/:routingOperationId` (+ the review's place and history; no minutes per bundle: a bundle is usually one person's), `/cycle-time` (per library operation, a team once) (people.efficiency.view); a person key in a filter or group 400 `query/…`. Every line, line's day, operation and operation's day is a group: `hideSmallGroups` empties it under the company's minimum, its headcount the smaller of the people who reported and who attended; tiles add up only the groups shown. The review `GET /people/efficiency/review` (window 60 d, > 10 % either way over ≥ 5 bundles: constants, a schema need) and `POST …/review/:routingOperationId/decide` {siteId, decision KEEP|CHANGE|INVESTIGATE|SEND, reason, proposedMinutes?, basedOn} (people.efficiency.review; decisions are audit rows `people.decideStandardReview` of the RoutingOperation, read back; lock `factory.people.standard-review|tenant|site|step`; `refusal/REVIEW_MOVED`, `refusal/TOO_FEW_PEOPLE`; only writes the decision: a standard changes through a new routing revision). One person's figures `POST /people/efficiency/people/:employeeId/figures` {siteId, from, to, reason, note} (people.figures.view): the site's PERFORMANCE gate FIRST (409 `gate/PERFORMANCE` for every id, real or not), then `personFiguresAccess`: the worker and HR, a lead refused (H3), every other refusal the same 403; `openPersonFiguresInCommand` in a command that answers no figure (the receipt keeps a command's answer: figures in it would outlive the gate); the figures read after it, gate asked again; `personDaysAt` is the read for My day (B-W2b-68); not built on attended minutes (ATTENDANCE-only tag, `purpose.vitest.test.ts`). Event standard_review.decided (live people.efficiency.view).
`modules/people/myday` (B-W2b-68; MES-111, MES-112, MES-261; c.c. art. 2101; GDPR art. 15; floor only; factory-domain `people/myday/{rules,statement-document}`): `GET /people/myday` (the PIN, requireFloorStepUp; ?month): one worker's own day read fresh in the tenant's system view: `work` (own records, always), `performance` (earned and timed minutes, efficiency, `pace` = own solo pieces against the LINE's target since the first report; null with the PERFORMANCE gate off; no line output is ever an input, Owner answer F2), `pay` (B-W2b-43's floorMine; null for a month the piece-pay switch does not cover), `printer`. `GET /people/myday/answers` (no PIN: ids of own answered disputes, the tile's badge). Own APPROVED statement only, anyone else's answers 404: `GET /people/myday/statements/:id/pdf` (a PHONE device only, 409 `refusal/PHONE_ONLY`; stored nowhere) and `POST …/print` (one command: the statement's file stored once, office only, then the PrintJob on the site's ONE active A4 printer, 409 `refusal/NO_PRINTER` otherwise; the worker and the device on the job).
`modules/people/limits` (B-W2b-42; MES-232; office only; factory-domain `people/limits/{rules,checks,permissions}`): `GET|PUT /people/limits`, `POST /people/limits/:ruleId/archive` (WorkingTimeRule per group, company or site; people.limits.manage; looser than the law only as CCNL), `limitFacts` (`worked.ts`: THE read of worked days + the published roster ahead), `GET /people/limits/warnings` (tones, no name, no figure), `GET /people/employees/:employeeId/limits` (one record). Alert kind WORKING_TIME_LIMIT (`warnings.ts workingTimeFindings`): one per site and limit, naming nobody; `notices.ts` tells the roles the limit names.
`modules/people/close` (B-W2b-48; MES-231; D.L. 112/2008 art. 39; office only, per company; factory-domain `people/close/{rules,lul,permissions}`, events in `people/events.ts` `closeEvents`, decision ATTENDANCE_MONTH_CLOSE): `GET /people/month-close` (the checklist, the deadline, the seal), `GET …/months`, `POST /people/month-close` {companyId, month, phrase} (ONE command under the month's key taken EXCLUSIVE, then the calendar stored and sealed; a store that is down leaves it closed and unsealed: `POST …/:closeId/seal`), `POST …/:closeId/lul/open` (people.attendance.lul; PDF or CSV; recorded; the files are read ONLY here, `platform/files/owned.ts` MODULE_READ_FILES), `GET …/:closeId/corrections`, `GET|POST /people/attendance/days/:dayId/corrections` (append-only; people.attendance.correct). Timesheets are a note, not a hold. `monthDays` (store.ts) is THE reader of a month's figures: APPROVED overtime, effective = the closed figure replaced by its latest correction. The LUL layout is generic: `LUL_TO_CONFIRM` lists what the labour consultant confirms (GATE-PAYROLL).
`modules/people/reports/attendance` (B-W2b-48; MES-303, MES-236; factory-domain `people/reports/attendance/report`; its routes are declared in `modules/people/close`, the loader reads one folder down and `reports/` is shared with earnings): `GET /people/reports/attendance` per line or team (never per person; a group under the minimum emptied, and a sum that would give it away), `…/differences` (what no longer matches the closed LUL), `…/production` (attended, earned and off-standard minutes per line), `…/people/:employeeId` (one record).
`platform/admin/org` floor languages (B-W2a-41; PLT-128): `GET /org/floor-languages` (sites.view; per site Site.floorLocales, active workers per language with workers.view, the company's worker notice in force and its languages) and `PUT /org/sites/:siteId/floor-languages` {version, locales, reason?} (sites.manage; command org.setFloorLanguages on the Site's version): an added language needs the worker notice in force in it (text or PDF), a removed one no active worker who reads it (factory-domain privacy/floor-languages); refusals 409 `body/locales/<i>` | `remove/<code>`.
`modules/people/earnings` (B-W2b-43; MES-256, -260, -261, -264, -266, -148; office, and the floor's own pay; factory-domain `people/earnings/{rules,ledger,statement,permissions}`, code of the report in `modules/people/reports/earnings`): `/people/pay-periods`, `/people/statements`, `/people/held-scans`, `/people/earnings/*`, `/people/reports/earnings`. `ledger.ts` is the ONE writer of the append-only ledger; `scans.ts` keeps one bundle at one operation in step (handlers `people.earningsFromScan`, `people.heldScansAfterClock`); `person.ts` computes one person's period. Scans are booked as they happen; team shares, paid time and make-up only when the review starts. Pay reads APPROVED OvertimeEntry rows, never AttendanceDay.overtimeMinutes; clock state is read by employee (a MANUAL clock event names no worker). Nothing outside a site's piece-pay window.
B-W2b-62 adds to it `POST /people/statements/:statementId/pdf` (`statement-pdf.ts`: the right only, each opening recorded; an APPROVED statement's PDF stored once as `WorkerStatement.fileId`, read only through this route, `platform/files/owned.ts`), to `modules/people/piecerates` `GET /people/employees/:employeeId/rate-publications` (the Pay tab's proof of reading) and the export source `people.piecerates.ledger` (`platform/exports/sources/earnings-ledger.ts`: no name, `pay` columns by grain).
`modules/people/payroll` (B-W2b-54; MES-267, -268, -270 to -274; GATE-PAYROLL; office, and the consultant's link; factory-domain `people/payroll/{codes,values,checks,layouts,summary,mail,rules,permissions}`, events `payrollEvents` in `people/events.ts`): Nexus does not run payroll: it makes a file. `GET /people/payroll/month` (state, checks as counts, what differs from the latest file; no figure), `POST …/month/show` and `POST …/preview` (people.payroll.records, recorded; amounts `money.pay`), `GET|PUT …/codes` (mapping), `GET|POST|PATCH …/layouts`, `…/archive` (layouts are data; starters GENERIC_CSV, GENERIC_EXCEL, ZUCCHETTI, the last made up and unconfirmed in words until `confirmation` is recorded), `GET|POST …/exports` (three steps as accounting's: DRAFT under the month's lock, files outside any transaction, then the figures compared, the number, EXPORTED; `correctionOfId` makes v2, which replaces the file, withdraws its links; a leaver's PAYROLL_FLAGGED ticks), `…/exports/:id/cancel`, `…/values` and `…/files/:fileId/open` (the right, recorded), `…/send` and `…/links/:linkId/withdraw` (a DOCUMENT_VIEW link of the PayrollExport whose party id is the export's own; token in the sent mail only, no attachment), link session `GET /people/payroll/link/files` and `POST …/link/files/:fileId/download` (a counted use, evidence, audit). Files are in MODULE_OWNED_FILES and MODULE_READ_FILES. What an export carried: `values`, and the CARRIED row in `checks`; `pending.ts` feeds the close panel's `payrollCorrectionsPending`.
`modules/shipping/labels` (B-W1-48): carrier labels through the company's OWN Sendcloud account (`adapters/sendcloud`: API v3 for LIVE/SANDBOX, `FakeSendcloud` for local/test; runtime `processCarrier()`/`useCarrier`, gateway ops carrier.create_label | label_document | void_label | tracking): `POST /shipping/shipments/:id/labels` (shipping.labels.buy, office; PACKED + live DDT; per parcel outside any tx, one Sendcloud shipment each, reference `<parcelId>.v<version>` so a repeat is the same label; partial success → `outcomes`; then one command on the Shipment records trackingNumber, carrierParcelId = `<sendcloudShipment>:<parcel>`, labelledAt and the files (PDF; ZPL with a label printer), current files = the Parcel's StoredFiles made at labelledAt), `…/labels` GET and `…/labels/print` (shipping.pack, office and floor; ZPL print jobs, labelKind CARTON), `…/parcels/:parcelId/label/void` (shipping.labels.void, before SHIPPED), `…/tracking` (shipping.view); webhooks via INBOUND_PROVIDERS.SENDCLOUD (Sendcloud-Signature) → `recordTracking` (shipment row FOR UPDATE, dedupe, SHIPPED → DELIVERED | RETURNED); hourly clustered cron `shipping.trackingPoll` → event shipment.tracking_poll_due → worker handler `shipping.pollTracking`. B-W2b-F14 (wave 23): the purchase keeps the carrier's tracking page (`Parcel.trackingUrl`, https only — anything else is never kept nor shown, factory-domain `shipping/labels/tracking trackingPageOf`) and its expected delivery (`Shipment.expectedDeliveryAt`, the latest of the labels'); a void clears the page (and the ETA once no label is left); the poll passes the carrier's ETA now to `recordTracking` (moves it while PACKED or SHIPPED, never on a move to DELIVERED/RETURNED; audit `shipping.etaMoved`); the office record, labels and tracking reads and the portal's orders (`expectedDeliveryAt` while packed or on the way, `trackingLink` "Track on the carrier's site") show both. The FAKE says both (`FakeSendcloud`: ETA + 2 days, an EXCEPTION a day later, `delay`, `trackingPageNext`); the v3 adapter maps no ETA yet.
`modules/shipping/documents` (B-W1-49): what travels with a shipment (factory-domain `shipping/documents`: the DDT, the packing list, per style × the work orders' FROZEN revision its DoC and leaflet via product/compliance `currentDocFor`, in the order's language, else en, else it; DoC/leaflet blockers in the compliance step's words: `read.ts shipmentDocumentBlockers(tx, shipmentId)` for the ship step); `GET /shipping/shipments/:id/documents` (shipping.view), `POST …/documents/attach` and `…/documents/link` (shipping.manage): copies made with platform/files `prepareFileCopy` + `recordGeneratedFile` on the Shipment, shared with its party (PARTNER, LINK; attached = a file with the same SHA-256), sources and copies kept ship date + 10 years (`retainFilesInCommand`), under an advisory lock per shipment; a DOCUMENT_VIEW link of the Shipment per address (365 days, 100 opens; the office's send revokes the address's older live one) mailed on the Shipment thread: the thread keeps the text WITHOUT the token, the mail is composed in memory and sent in-process (`deliverOutbound`, no email.queued); link session `GET /shipping/link/documents`, `POST /shipping/link/documents/:fileId/download` (Idempotency-Key, audited; a DOCUMENT_VIEW session holds files.read, so platform/files' link rule decides what it reads); public `POST /shipping/link/renew` (one answer, work after it: a new link only to the old link's address while an ACTIVE contact, never for a revoked or live link, 10-minute coalesce); worker handler `shipping.shipDocuments` (shipment.status_changed → SHIPPED: attach + link; ddt.issued replacement: attach).
`modules/shipping` every day (B-W2a-48; LOG-004, LOG-012, LOG-028, LOG-105, LOG-090; factory-domain `shipping/{shipments,packing,evidence}`): the backorder (`GET /shipping/orders/:salesOrderId/backorder[?shipmentId]`, `GET /shipping/backorders` paged in SQL; shipping.view) and the caps the shipment commands hold (`shipments/backorder.ts assertOrderCaps`, after the cells' locks, under the order lines' `factory.work-order-line` locks: never more than ordered per line × size over DRAFT/PACKED/SHIPPED/DELIVERED, nothing of a line closed short; a REPAIR cell is a 400); `/shipping/ready` pages orders in SQL (`cursor`, `limit`, `nextCursor`; `inStock` per cell); the leaving hook `shipments/leave.ts whenShipped(tools, shipment)` called by ddt's shipGoods (ship-cell then order-line locks; a line closed short after packing stops it; finished goods out: ONE SHIPMENT move (B-W2b-19; W2a: ADJUSTMENT) out of the FG place per shipment line × place naming its `shipmentLineId`, with the work order, key `shipping.fromStock:<shipment>:<n>`; never reversed on its own (`shipments/stock.ts`; B-W2a-12's completion subtracts `takenFromStockByCell` from its on-shipments); the freight by the site's rule unless typed (`shipments/freight.ts`); AWAITED evidence for taxMode EU_B2B to another EU state); freight `GET|PUT /shipping/sites/:siteId/freight-rule`, `PUT /shipping/shipments/:shipmentId/freight` (shipping.freight.manage; money.price; typed vs rule = Shipment.freightBasis MANUAL | RULE, B-W2b-19); packing list versions (`POST …/packing-list` reuses the file of the same contents + language, else version n+1 numbered `<number>/<n>`; `GET …/packing-lists`; B-W2b-19: each version a GeneratedDocument row PACKING_LIST, the newest is current, contents back to an older version's = a new row with its file); `POST /shipping/shipments/:shipmentId/labels/void` (every label; a parcel the carrier has, latest tracking past LABEL_CREATED, keeps it, also for the single void); transport evidence `documents/evidence.ts` (`GET …/evidence`, `POST …/evidence/papers[/:fileId/complete]` with the paper's kind as StoredFile.category (B-W2b-19), `…/evidence/complete|reopen|needed`; shipping.evidence.manage). W2b follow-ups (B-W2b-19): `PUT /shipping/shipments/:id/freight-cost` (shipping.freight.manage + money.cost: the carrier's invoice, Shipment.freightCost + currency, until CANCELLED); `GET|POST /shipping/shipments/:id/carrier-return` (view | shipping.manage; a RETURNED shipment's garments back into finished goods once: ADJUSTMENT in per cell, key `shipping.carrierReturn:<shipment>:<n>`, valued at the frozen standard, `periodLockOverride`; they count in the cell's stock, never in `takenFromStockByCell`; B-W2b-F1: the same command writes `Shipment.carrierReturnReceivedAt` and puts the shipment's SHIPPED serials back to RELEASED, unpacked, so the release cap (`cells.ts cellCounts`, the ready list, the ShipmentLine trigger) no longer counts it and the same cell ships them again); PATCH shipment `transportMode` (Intrastat, until CANCELLED); the DDT keeps `language` and `transportMode` (re-prints read the language); the labels read lists `voided` labels (office; audit rows `shipping.voidLabel`); a repair cell's line never grows or leaves; ship and DDT-with-ship take `periodLockOverride`.
`modules/shipping/tracking-link` (B-W2b-170; LOG-062; design links.tracking, SCR-194): the customer's branded tracking page, a SHIPMENT_TRACK link of a Shipment (open and download never counted; no grain). Made when the goods leave (worker handler `shipping.trackingLink` on shipment.status_changed → SHIPPED: the party's ACTIVE ORDERS contacts, once per address), by the office (`GET|POST /shipping/shipments/:id/tracking-links`, `…/:linkId/revoke`; shipping.view | shipping.manage; mailed or one link to pass on, its URL once), from the portal (`POST /portal/sales-orders/:orderId/shipments/:shipmentId/tracking-link`, portal.salesOrders.view, at most 10 live), by the public search (`POST /tracking/search` {tenant, company?, reference, postcode, proof?}: abuse guard `public.lookup`, one answer `{shipments: []}` for every miss, 600 ms answer floor, a one-day link per shipment found) and by "Ask for a new link" (`registerLinkRenewer('SHIPMENT_TRACK', 'Shipment')`). The link's note says how it came (factory-domain `shipping/tracking-link` TRACKING_NOTES); every link is made for 180 days and the page ends 60 days after delivery (`trackingEndsAt`). Link session `GET /link/tracking` (codes only: steps, pill, carrier events without the carrier's text, town and postcode only, papers = packing list + DoC + user information, never the DDT or a price; none for a search link), `POST /link/tracking/papers/:fileId/download` (DOWNLOADED evidence). Event `shipment.tracking_link_made`; mail template `shipping.tracking_link` (subject kept, body with the link in memory only).

`platform/floor-sync` (B-W0-30; PLT-237, PLT-239, PLT-240, PLT-189; SCR-023): floor `POST /floor/sync` (floor.work; the tablet's queue, ≤ 50 events {clientEventId, seq, kind, occurredAt, workerId, payload} applied BY SEQ, each through its own module command in-process (kinds.ts: production.scan.report|undo|ask → scans `sendBatch` one item; production.cut.log|remnant → cutting `logCut`; production.bundles.make → bundles `makeBundles`, with `ticketCode` from the tablet's own BUNDLE block → `useScanIds`; plant.report → plant `report` (B-W2a-73: a machine problem from the station, the tablet's time kept; a plant refusal becomes the sync's refusal in its words)); each kind's permission checked with can() at the site; the ledger first (FloorSyncEvent, append-only, once per device × client event id: a resend answers `duplicate` and never reaches the module; the same id for another kind → CLIENT_ID_REUSED); another person's event at a WORKER site → `waiting` (not kept); refused → FloorRefusedAction (once per device × id × code; code, words, ONE fix, final; the scans module's FloorScanRefusal named as `moduleRefusal`); a later accept RESOLVES the event's open refusals in the ledger command; `blocks` [{kind, remaining}] tops the tablet's DEVICE blocks up in the same request; `clock` from `sentAt`), `GET /floor/sync` (own open refused, others counted, lastRecordedAt, subject), `POST /floor/sync/refused/:id/discard` (own; reason "Discarded on the tablet…"); office `GET /floor-sync/refused[/:id]` (floorsync.view; scopeWhere company/site; OPEN by default; a list names devices, one record its worker) and `POST /floor-sync/refused/:id/discard` {reason, version} (floorsync.discard). A refused scan is discarded through the scans module's `discardRefusal`, whose command also discards the floor-sync rows naming it (`discardForModuleRefusal`), so the two registers close together. Events floor.action_refused, floor.refused_action_closed. Seam: store.ts.

`platform/views` + `platform/exports` (B-W2a-23; PLT-163, PLT-179; SCR-232): saved views of the Office's lists and the export of a list. A view (`GET|POST /views`, `GET|PATCH|DELETE /views/:id`; reading is any office session, writes views.save) keeps what the web's list frame keeps (factory-domain `views/state`: the list's address terms, its column layout, its density), for its owner, the holders of a role the owner holds, everyone (views.manage) or as a role's ONE default view of the list (views.manage; a second default takes the mark off the first in the same command); listKey = the Office page with dots (`sales.orders`); tenant-wide (companyId null). The rules are factory-domain `views/rules`. An export (`POST /exports` views.export + the list's own permission over the screen's company, at most 3 running per person; `GET /exports[/:id]`, `GET /exports/:id/download`, the requester only) is QUEUED with what the server applied (ExportJob.query: the address, the source's query, the columns with their headings, title, language; omittedColumns = the columns of a money grain the person lacks), then the worker's `exports.run` (on export.requested) reads the list through its SOURCE as the person (their live roles now; a hybrid caller: their office context over the system client) page by page (≤ 50 000 rows), writes CSV (BOM, `;` + decimal comma in Italian) or XLSX (exceljs) with the omitted columns named above the header, puts it with putGeneratedFile and records it (StoredFile of recordType `ExportJob`) in exports.finish → DONE (expires after 7 days); cron `exports.expire` (hourly) deletes the file and marks EXPIRED, and fails a RUNNING job untouched for 30 minutes. A list exports once it has a source in `platform/exports/sources/` (one file + one line in `sources/index.ts`: `defineExportSource({ listKey, permission, query(address), read(caller, query, cursor, limit) → the module's own list function, columns {id: {grain?, value(row, words)}}, words {it, en} })`): W2a-23 has sales.orders, purchasing.orders, production.work-orders. ExportJob.viewId stays null (the screen sends the view's name, kept in the query; since B-W2b-03 the key is ON DELETE SET NULL). W2b (B-W2b-11; PLT-184): every W1/W2a list has its source (sources/*.ts; a page's second frame is `<key>.2` with `page`), every report of the pack is a DYNAMIC source `home.reports.<report key>` (sources/reports.ts, from the report registry; the worker loads the reports in background.ts), optional source parts `page` (the e-mail's link), `refusal(actor)` (words beyond the permission), `count(caller, query)` and `reimport { importer, key(row) }` ("Full file for re-import": XLSX_REIMPORT writes the importer's layout from its own `load`, reads back UNCHANGED; materials, operations, locations, customers, suppliers); the worker COUNTS the rows before the run (`exports.count`: rowCount = the total while RUNNING, raised when the list grew; a count above the limit refuses before writing); handler `exports.mailLink` (mail.ts, on export.finished DONE) e-mails the link (`<web>/w/<company>/<page>?export=<job>`, never the file) once when the export finished ≥ 15 s after it was asked (ExportJob.emailedAt; template `exports.ready`, FAKE SES locally).

`platform/views/bulk` (B-W2b-13; PLT-182, PLT-166, PLT-167): bulk edit with preview and inline edit of a list. `POST /views/bulk/preview { listKey, ids ≤ 500, changes {colId: value} }` (nothing written) answers old → new per row or a refusal CODE (held + status, missing, forbidden; never the API's sentence); `POST /views/bulk/apply { listKey, rows [{ id, version, changes }] ≤ 500, undo? }` writes each row through the record's OWN command (its permission over the row, version check, audit, events; key `<Idempotency-Key>:<row id>`), one refused row (held, stale, missing, forbidden, invalid, failed) never stopping the others; Undo = the same apply with each row's earlier values from the version the change gave it; one grid cell = one row, one field. Both are registered in `platform/views/routes.ts` (the autoloader does not look inside a folder that has a routes file). A list is edited in bulk once it has a target in `platform/views/bulk/targets/` (one file + one line in `targets/index.ts`: `defineBulkTarget({ listKey, permission (the list's write permission), fields {colId: {kind date|integer|text|choice, limits, grain?, read(row), held?(row)}}, load(caller, ids), idOf, labelOf, versionOf, statusOf?, rowHeld?, write(caller, row, changes, version, key) → the module's own command })`; rules in factory-domain `views/bulk/rules`): production.work-orders (due, priority). `POST /exports` takes `ids` (≤ 500, the selection row's Export N): the worker keeps the view's rows whose `source.idOf?.(row) ?? row.id` was selected (total = their number, no count read) and FAILS "None of the selected rows is in this view any more…" when none is left; the job view says `selected`. Quick filters keep a WORD in the list's address (`due=this-week`, `owner=me`): the list's read resolves it when it runs (factory-domain `views/quick/relative`: relativeRange against the site's today, meOf against the asker) and its export source reads the same term (work orders: `due`).
