`modules/plant/{lists,machines,requests,jobs,floor}` (B-W2a-35; QHS-162, QHS-178, QHS-184; Owner answer H1): the machine register (`/plant/machines…`, plant.view | plant.machines.manage; the cost with the cost grain), the group's machine classes and symptoms (`/plant/classes`, `/plant/symptoms`, `POST /plant/starter`; writes over every company), machine requests (`POST /plant/requests` from the station or the office, plant.report: every floor role; unsafe = the machine DO_NOT_USE in the same command; `/plant/requests…` queue, tiles, triage with plant.requests.triage; `…/sign-safe` plant.signSafe, a signing act: office step-up / floor PIN), maintenance jobs (`/plant/jobs…`, numbered MAINTENANCE_JOB; plant.jobs.manage plans, assigns, closes, cancels; plant.jobs.work starts, waits, hands back, reopens; RUNNING only once signed safe) and the floor reads (`/plant/floor/machines`, `/plant/floor/jobs`). Every machine move goes through store.ts `moveMachine` with the machine row locked first; `checks.ts stationBlock(tx, stationId)` is the one-line hook a floor work module calls to refuse work at a station whose machines are all do-not-use. Templates `maintenance` (office) and `technician` (floor) sign safe. Notices: platform/notifications/rules/plant.ts. B-W2a-53 adds the office screens' pickers (`modules/plant/office`): `GET /plant/places` (plant.view: the request's company's sites with their active lines, work centres and stations) and `GET /plant/technicians?siteId` (plant.jobs.manage there: office users holding plant.jobs.work, and the site's floor technicians by name only for a caller with workers.view).
B-W2b-96 (QHS-179, -180, -181, -183, -189, -190; factory-domain `plant/requests/{routing,progress,lock,merge,refusals}`, `plant/jobs/close`, events `depthEvents` in `plant/events.ts`): routing at the report (`requests/requests.ts reportInCommand`, also the office lock's: the site's first active RequestRoutingRule by (seq, id) for the machine's class or every class, written in the INSERT; the site's PlantSettings, none = H1, say who may lock); escalation = clustered cron `plant.escalateRequests` (every minute, `requests/escalation.ts`: NEW, unacknowledged, routed, rule active; one system command per level keyed `plant-escalate:<request>:<level>`; event maintenance_request.escalated); notices `requests/notices.ts` (routed: ASSIGNMENT to the team's members' / technician's office accounts when push is on; escalated: ALERT to the person); progress `requests/depth.ts stepRequest` (`POST /plant/requests/:id/on-the-way` plant.jobs.work, `…/works` plant.report; floor `POST /plant/floor/requests/:id/acknowledge|on-the-way` plant.jobs.work, `…/works` plant.report; floor-sync kind `plant.request.progress`; a MERGED request follows its target; requestOut.progress / .routing / .voiceNotes); `POST /plant/requests/:id/lock` (a NEW unsafe office request of the machine, the open source MERGED into it; event maintenance_request.locked with `hazardReport`, also for every unsafe report: THE seam for Incidents), `GET /plant/requests/locks|duplicates`, `POST /plant/requests/merge` (all or none, into a request or the machine's open job); photos and voice notes `requests/files.ts` (`POST /plant/requests/:id/files[/:fileId/complete]` plant.report, `POST /plant/jobs/:id/files…` plant.jobs.work with BEFORE_WORK | AFTER_WORK photos, as the tenant's system caller scoped to the record's company and site, OFFICE + FLOOR; floor look `GET /plant/floor/files/:fileId/download`); the one-screen close (hand-back `action`, `failureCode`, minutesSpent counted from the start; reopen clears them and the lead's confirmation) and `POST /plant/jobs/:id/confirm` (office, plant.requests.triage) / `POST /plant/floor/jobs/:id/confirm` (floor.lead); job record `lock`, `files`, `requestFiles`, `close`, `canConfirm`; floor reads `GET /plant/floor/requests[/:id]` (the station's under way), `GET /plant/floor/queue` (technician: routed or escalated to me first); plant settings `GET|POST /plant/settings/routing`, `PATCH …/:ruleId`, `POST …/:ruleId/remove` (unused deleted, used switched off), `POST …/order`, `GET …/preview`, `GET|PUT /plant/settings/lock` (H1 held: other values 409 `refusal/H1_HELD`; hazard report off = step-up) with plant.settings.manage (production_manager, maintenance). Refusal codes of W2b are DEPTH_REFUSALS (W2a's PLANT_REFUSALS list is mirrored by the Office screens and stays as it is).
B-W2b-124 `modules/plant/statutory` (QHS-205; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 71: results kept at least 3 years; factory-domain `plant/statutory/{rules,report,permissions}`, events in `plant-statutory/events.ts`; office only): check types `GET|POST /plant/checks/types`, `PATCH …/:checkTypeId` (the group's: plant.checks.manage over every company; code and class fixed, retired never deleted; the law is the office's words, no legal interval built in); plans `GET /plant/checks/plans[/:planId]`, `POST /plant/checks/plans` (one ACTIVE per machine × type: `replaceReason` ends the old one in the same command, else 409 refusal/PLAN_EXISTS), `PATCH …/:planId`, `POST …/:planId/move` (reason in the audit row, never before firstDueOn) and `…/end`; the register `GET /plant/checks/results[/:resultId]`, `POST /plant/checks/results` (ONE command: the append-only result with keptUntil = day + max(3, type years), the plan's next due moved and its reminder cleared, and in the same step `job` (plant.jobs.manage: making.ts makeJobInCommand + the job's checkResultId) and `lock` (plant.report: requests' reportInCommand, unsafe, H1; with a job the lock request goes JOB_MADE); a correction names the result it corrects, once), `POST …/:resultId/job|lock` afterwards; the report `POST …/:resultId/reports[/:fileId/complete]` (DOCUMENT | CERTIFICATE, category CHECK_REPORT, OFFICE, retained until keptUntil; the generic routes cannot set the category), `GET …/reports/:fileId/download`, the PDF `GET|POST …/:resultId/pdf` (GeneratedDocument CHECK_REPORT, versions by fingerprint); reads `GET /plant/checks/counts|calendar`. Clustered cron `plant.checkReminders` (hourly :07, `reminders.ts`): once per due date (lastRemindedAt), system command keyed `plant-check-remind:<plan>:<day>`, event machine_check.due. `plans.ts endPlansOfMachineInCommand` is the retire hook. Refusals STATUTORY_REFUSALS (`refusal/<CODE>` + `fix`).
`modules/production/reports` (B-W2a-38; MES-301, MES-160; SCR-240, SCR-073 Lines): `GET /production/reports/daily?siteId&date` (production.reports.view; the day per lane = the reporting station's line, else its work centre, × style × size: good through the lane's LAST operation, scrap and rework anywhere on it plus a DONE rework's scrap and rework opened by hand; a correction counts on its REPORT's day; line efficiency = earned SAM minutes / people × the line's calendar minutes; rules factory-domain `production/reports/daily`; scans read WITHOUT workerId), `GET /production/reports/lines?siteId` (workorders.view: per active line its state RUNNING|WAITING|NO_WORK, output today vs the CURRENT published plan's slots prorated by working minutes, work orders, bottleneck, per station only signedIn + machinesDown, Owner F2), subscriptions `GET|POST /production/reports/subscriptions`, `PATCH …/:id` (production.reports.view: myself; colleagues need production.reports.subscribe and must read it themselves → 400 `body/recipients/<i>`), `GET /production/reports/recipients?siteId` (subscribe); one ReportSubscription `production.daily` per recipient and site; clustered cron `production.dailyReport` (*/15, mail.ts `runDailyReports`): due in the SITE's zone, the person's live roles checked, the site's previous working day, EmailOutbound record User template production.daily sent in-process (FAKE locally), then lastSentAt + production.daily_report_sent. Sites are the request's company's (x-factory-company).
`modules/insights/reports` (B-W2a-79; ANA-001, ANA-029, ANA-034; route lines in insights' routes.ts/manifest.ts): the report pack is the REGISTRY FOLDER `reports/defs/` (one `<area>.<name>.ts` per report default-exporting `defineReport({ ...meta, read })`, meta = factory-domain `insights/reports/definition`; `report-files.ts` finds them for the routes, the tests and `build.mjs`; a step that adds a report adds its file and its words in factory-web `messages/*/home-reports.json` `report.<key>.*`, edits no list). `read(run)` reads AS THE PERSON through the module's own list (its scopes) and returns every column's cell; `run.ts` refuses NO_ACCESS (`permission/<perm>`) and NEEDS_GRAIN (`grain/<g>`), drops the columns of grains the person lacks BEFORE the answer, blanks `people` figures of rows with `people` < 5 and says both (`rights`). Routes `GET /reports` (home.reports.view), `/reports/:key`, `/reports/:key/results?<filters>&group=&lang=`, `/reports/:key/schedule`, and My numbers `GET /home/numbers` (home.view; factory-domain `insights/reports/numbers` NUMBER_RULES, one reader per number in `numbers.ts`; a failing number answers `problem`, never 0). Read only: no command, no event.
`modules/people/{employees,teams,leavers}` (B-W2b-26; MES-208…211, MES-214, PLT-005, PLT-020; office only; factory-domain `people/{employees,teams,leavers}` + module events `people/events.ts`): employees `GET /people/employees[/counts|/unlinked-workers|/:id|/import/:jobId/missing]` (people.view; the HR register: names and contract facts, NEVER pay, attendance, health or a figure about one person's work; tiles ACTIVE · STARTING (14 d) · LEAVING · AGENCY_ENDING (30 d) · UNLINKED_WORKERS), `POST /people/employees` | `/from-worker` | `/from-user`, `PATCH /people/employees/:id` (a site move within the company ends the team membership, clears the home line, refuses an open loan), `POST …/:id/workers`, `…/workers/:workerId/unlink`, `PUT …/:id/user` (people.employees.manage): ONE record per person, every DB-at-commit rule (one account per person, the linked worker's account agrees, the code unique and not on another person's floor sign-in, the agency an ORGANISATION) answered first as 409 `refusal/<CODE>` + `body/<field>` (`employees/access.ts refuse`); W0's `PATCH /auth/floor/workers/:id` answers the account disagreement in words too. Teams `GET /people/teams[/mine|/:id]` (people.view; `/mine` = people.team.view: the caller's Employee.userId's current memberships), `POST /people/teams`, `PATCH`, `…/archive` (empty only), `PUT …/lead` (a current member; the old flag first), `POST /people/teams/moves[/preview]` (all or none, effective today: the old row ends yesterday or is removed when it began today; 409 `refusal/MOVES_REFUSED` + `body/moves/<i>`) (people.teams.manage); loans `GET|POST /people/loans`, `…/:id/end` (running only), `…/:id/remove` (planned only) (people.loans.manage; the guard's advisory key `factory.people.loan|tenant|employee` taken first); the org tree `GET /org/tree`, `POST /org/sites/:siteId/areas`, `PATCH /org/areas/:id`, `…/archive`, `PUT /org/lines/:lineId/area` (sites.view / sites.manage). Leavers `GET /people/leavers[/:caseId]`, `GET /people/employees/:id/leaving/preview`, `POST /people/employees/:id/leaving`, `POST /people/leavers/:caseId/steps/:stepId/done|skip|reopen`, `…/cancel` (until the last day), `…/check` (people.leavers.manage); `leavers/automatic.ts settleCase` runs in every case write and in the clustered cron `people.leaverSteps` (hourly :17; a command only when something is due): after the last day the floor sign-in is switched off (workers INACTIVE, badges REVOKED, sessions ended) and BADGE_OFF/SESSIONS_ENDED tick, ACCESS_ENDED ticks when the account is DEACTIVATED (never done by the system), SHIFTS_CLEARED when no WORK roster entry follows the last day; nothing open + the last day come → DONE, the person LEFT with endDate, membership ended, planned loans removed. Importer `people.employees` (platform/imports/importers/employees.ts; keyed by the employee code; per company; a known record's site never moves by import, LEFT never changes). Threads: Team only (an Employee thread would be read by every threads.read role of the site: waits for a per-record read permission). Events employee.created|updated, team.changed, line_loan.changed, org_area.changed, leaver.started|step_done|ended.
`modules/people/shifts` (B-W2b-32; MES-218, MES-222; factory-domain `people/shifts/{rules,limits,permissions}` + its block in `people/events.ts`): the roster `GET /people/shifts/roster` (people.shifts.view; people × days: each cell's RosterEntry or the default shift — the person's default pattern, else their home line's or site's calendar pattern; a closed day OFF; two shifts that weekday give none — the planned overtime, the absences only behind the site's ATTENDANCE gate (`attendance.ts`, THE one attendance read), the warnings in words: 11 h rest, break after 6 h, 48 h weekly average; the calendar's days, the limits in force, the shift choices, people per shift per line and day), `POST /people/shifts/roster/cells` (autosave with the version read; null = back to the default), `…/assign[/preview]`, `…/copy-week[/preview]`, `…/publish` (handler: people.shifts.manage, or people.shifts.team for a team the caller leads; DRAFT until published, a published cell changed stays published; a NEW warning of a BLOCK rule = 409 `refusal/LIMIT_BLOCKED`; no shift after a leaver's last day or outside the employment; a closed attendance month refused first); overtime `GET|POST /people/overtime-requests`, `…/check`, `GET|PATCH …/:id`, `…/:id/people`, `…/ask`, `…/answers/:agreementId` (a spoken answer, OFFICE) (people.overtime.manage), `…/approve` (people.overtime.approve; ASKED → AGREED → APPROVED in one command: the stored status stays ASKED while answers come, so a yes is taken back until the approval), `…/cancel`; the tablet `GET /people/floor/my-shifts` (the worker's PUBLISHED rows + their default shift read in the system scope for that one person) and `POST /people/floor/overtime-asks/:agreementId/answer` (floor.work, the worker's own answer, FLOOR, signed with the PIN). `planner.ts planningFor` is THE planned-shift read (B-W2b-37 attendance, B-W2b-49 dispatch). Events shift.assigned, roster.published (+floor), overtime_request.changed, overtime.asked (+floor), overtime.answered.
`modules/safety/{organisation,calendar}` (B-W2b-120; QHS-224, QHS-226, QHS-227; office only; factory-domain `safety/{organisation,calendar}/{rules,permissions}` + `safety/events.ts`; perms safety.view | organisation.manage | calendar.manage; templates hr, safety_officer, company_doctor): the safety organisation (`GET /safety/organisation`, appointments with letter = file of the row, the appointee's signature OFFICE|PAPER once, end; an RLS/RLST opens its RlsNotice in the same command), the RLS notice to INAIL (a manual send: `POST /safety/rls-notices/:id/file|due|cancel`, receipt = file of the notice), the safety calendar (`GET /safety/calendar?from&to[&late]`, `/counts`) read from a FOLDER of sources `modules/safety/calendar/sources/*.ts` (one file per kind, `defineCalendarSource`, loaded by registry.ts at route registration and in background.ts for the export; build.mjs makes each an entry point: a later step adds a file, never edits a list), and the typed deadlines (`/safety/deadlines…`: evidence = file of the row, done makes the next occurrence of a repeating one). Health visits and INAIL reports are counts only (no person, no ProtectedView); export source `plant.calendar`.
`modules/safety/risk` (B-W2b-122; QHS-230, QHS-234; D.Lgs. 81/2008 artt. 28-29; office only; factory-domain `safety/risk/{rules,seal,permissions}`, events `safety-risk/events`; perms safety.view | safety.risk.manage | safety.risk.sign (step-up)): the risk assessment per site or production unit (`GET|POST /safety/risk/assessments`, `PATCH …/:id`, `GET /safety/risk/counts`); a version freezes the consultant's PDF (a file of the RiskAssessment, AVAILABLE) with its SHA-256 and the time (`POST /safety/risk/assessments/:id/versions`; numbered above every earlier one; one waits at a time; signers from the organisation in force: the employer always, RSPP, RLS or RLST, doctor; a person holding two roles signs once), signed by each signer once (`POST /safety/risk/versions/:id/sign`: the holder's own OFFICE signature, requireStepUp after every other check; `POST …/signatures`: a signed paper, its scan first as a file of the signature whose id `dvrSignatureId(version, role)` the version announces; nobody signs a version twice), SIGNED with its certain date (the last signature's day) in the same command, then the seal GeneratedDocument DVR_FROZEN (`GET|POST …/seal`; a store that does not answer leaves it signed and unsealed); `…/withdraw` before it is signed. B-W2b-F13b: an outside signer's APPROVED approval request of kind SIGNATURE (platform/approvals-out) is a signature, method LINK (`signature-link.ts` settle: approvalRequestId, the typed name, the answer's Rome day; the last one makes the version SIGNED; the office keeps the seal after). The improvement plan `GET|POST /safety/risk/measures`, `GET|PATCH …/:id`, `POST …/:id/status` (the owner moves their own with safety.view; a manager verifies or cancels), `GET /safety/risk/owners`; cost = the cost grain; `addMeasureInCommand(tools, assessment, body)` is the seam for another step's command.
`modules/safety/incidents` (B-W2b-125; QHS-265, QHS-268, QHS-270; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 18 c.1 r; GDPR art. 9; factory-domain `safety/incidents/{rules,permissions}` + the `incidentEvents` block of `safety/events.ts`; perms safety.incidents.view (anonymised: no person, injury or filing type) | .report | .manage | .people (protected, a write risk: read_only never holds it); tablets report with floor.work): office `/safety/incidents…` (list, counts with the critical banner of an INAIL deadline under 24 h or late, record with people as counts; report; edit, seen, close (held while an action is open or a filing waits), reopen, cancel; actions made into a MaintenanceJob through plant `makeJobInCommand` (source INCIDENT), a RiskMeasure (source INCIDENT naming the incident) or a TrainingSession (OPEN) in one command; people add/edit/remove; INAIL filings: the absence decides STATISTICAL (≥ 1 day) or INSURANCE (> 3), the certificate makes it DUE with dueAt = received + 48 h exactly, file with the protocol number, not needed / needed; photos and INAIL's receipt as module-owned and module-read OFFICE files (platform/files/owned.ts) through the scoped system caller); THE protected reads `protected.ts` (`…/people/open`, `…/filings/open`, `POST /safety/incidents/people`, `…/receipt/:fileId/open`): a ProtectedView row per person/filing/list written in a command FIRST, the values read after it commits (never in a receipt), audit rows never carry a value; floor `/safety/floor/incidents…` (start: 112 + first aiders in force + "here"; report (named, or WITHOUT A NAME: a near miss or hazard naming nobody, written in the floor scope as the system actor — access.ts `anonymousCaller` + `anonymousKey`: no worker or tablet in its audit row, number ledger, receipt, event or the floor-sync ledger; never queued); seen; photos within 30 minutes, up to 4). Floor-sync kind `safety.incident.report` (named only). Reminders: date timers `INAIL_FILING_DUE:-24h|-4h|0` per DUE filing (filings.ts `syncReminders`, rules `reminderPlan` folds passed stages into one that fires at once), told by the fan-out rules `notices.ts` (ALERT to safety.incidents.people; a new report ALERT to safety.incidents.manage). Handler `safety.hazardOnLock` (maintenance_request.locked with hazardReport → one HAZARD incident, OFFICE, reported by the system actor, key `incident-on-lock:<request>`). Events incident.reported | changed | seen (the only one to the floor, floor.work) | incident_action.changed | inail_filing.changed: ids, kinds, states, deadlines only.
`modules/safety/rolesheets` (B-W2b-128; QHS-236, QHS-253; office only; factory-domain `safety/rolesheets/{rules,permissions}`, events `safety-rolesheets/events`; perm safety.rolesheets.manage, reads safety.view): the role risk sheets (`GET /safety/rolesheets`, `/catalogue`, `/:id`; `POST /safety/rolesheets` with its content; `POST …/:id/preview` = "What this changes" (changes + impact lines + fingerprint), `POST …/:id/apply` = ONE command writing RoleSheet (version), RoleSheetItem (replaced), PpeKitLine and TrainingRequirement ROLE_SHEET (kept by item/course) and the role's HealthPlan.needed (a new needed plan asks 12 months until the doctor sets it); `…/archive|restore`). A person's role: `modules/people/employees/role-sheet.ts` (routes registered by the rolesheets routes file): `GET|PUT /people/employees/:id/role-sheet`, `POST …/role-sheet/preview` (people.view / people.employees.manage); the person's visit only for `VISIT_NEED_READER`; a site move clears the sheet (writes.ts). `needs.ts` = the read B-W2b-133/134/135 share (roleNeedsOf, peopleOfRoles, holdingsOf, roleSinceOf).
`modules/safety/training` (B-W2b-133; QHS-241, QHS-242; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 37; office only; factory-domain `safety/training/{rules,permissions}`, events `safety-training/events`; perm safety.training.manage, reads safety.view): the course catalogue (`GET|POST /safety/training/courses`, `GET /safety/training/courses/:id` with who asks it; hours and refresher period/hours are the company's SETTINGS, no number of the law in code; `GET|POST …/courses/standard` = the standard list: types, names, the law, hours left for the person to type; `POST …/:id/preview` + `PATCH …/:id` (version, impactFingerprint): a new refresher period says whose dates WOULD move, records keep theirs (validUntil frozen on the append-only record); `…/archive` only when no ACTIVE role sheet or active machine type asks it, `…/restore`; a course of the group asks a grant over every company), who needs what (`GET /safety/training/requirements` counts per role and machine type, `…/role-sheets/:id` and `…/machine-classes/:id` with the people by name), and a machine type's courses (`POST …/machine-classes/:id/preview` + `PUT …` with the fingerprint, one command under an advisory lock; group-wide ACTIVE courses only; reuses the role sheets' sheetChanges/sheetImpact). `needs.ts` = THE who-needs-which-course read (trainingPeopleOf, authorisationsOf, classRequirementsOf, trainingNeedsOf: due = role since or authorisation start + withinDays, MET | DUE | OVERDUE) for B-W2b-139/146.
`modules/safety/ppe` (B-W2b-134; QHS-259, QHS-260, QHS-261; D.Lgs. 81/2008 arts. 18 c.1 d, 77 c.4; factory-domain `safety/ppe/{rules,receipt,permissions}`, events `safety-ppe/events`; perms safety.view | safety.ppe.manage | safety.ppe.issue, floor.work on the tablet): the PPE catalogue (`GET|POST /safety/ppe/items`, `GET|PATCH …/:id`, `…/:id/archive|restore` (refused while an ACTIVE role kit names it), `POST /safety/ppe/items/common`; unit cost = the cost grain), who lacks what (`GET /safety/ppe/counts`, `/gaps` (the role sheet's kit via rolesheets `roleNeedsOf` minus `holdingsOf`; units past `lifeMonths` = `replace`), `/kits`, `/people/:employeeId` = the person's PPE card), issues and receipts (`GET /safety/ppe/issues`, `GET /safety/ppe/receipts/:id` (any row id; a PIN receipt `verified` against its SHA-256), `POST /safety/ppe/issues` = a PAPER receipt (its scan a file of record PpeIssue = the receipt's id; until then "not signed"), `POST /safety/ppe/issues/:id/void` (a VOID row, once; the receipt stays), `GET|POST /safety/ppe/receipts/:id/pdf` (preview; keep = GeneratedDocument PPE_RECEIPT, PIN receipts only)); a receipt = the ISSUE rows of one person, site, instant and method, named by its smallest id (factory-domain `receiptsOf`); every issue/void takes `factory.ppe-issue|<employee>`. Floor (`/safety/floor/ppe/items|person|receive`): the person receiving signs with THEIR OWN badge or name and PIN through the floor sign-in's `provedWorker` + `claimProved` (lockout counts), one FLOOR_PIN row per line with the SHA-256 of `signedReceiptText`; online only (no floor-sync kind: a PIN is never queued). The leaver step PPE_RETURNED: `modules/people/leavers/ppe-step.ts` (`GET /people/leavers/:caseId/ppe`, `POST …/steps/:stepId/ppe-returned` through leavers `moveStep`, recordType PpeIssue).
`modules/safety/chemicals` (B-W2b-143; QHS-277, QHS-278; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 223, REACH art. 35; factory-domain `safety/chemicals/{rules,permissions,events}`; safety.view reads, safety.chemicals.manage keeps): the chemical register `GET /safety/chemicals` (site, status, tile withoutSds|toRequest, use, cabinet, q; `sheetState` MISSING | OTHER_LANGUAGE (no current sheet in the company's language) | OK), `/counts`, `/:id` (every sheet), `/data-sheets` (the library: current per product and language with the superseded kept, and the products with none), `/cabinets`, `/cabinets/:siteId/:code` (the wall sheet), `/choices?siteId`; writes `POST /safety/chemicals`, `PATCH …/:id`, `…/:id/archive|restore`; a sheet's PDF `POST …/:id/data-sheets/upload` (+ `/:fileId/complete`: a DOCUMENT of the Chemical, PDF only, shared OFFICE + FLOOR at its site, as the tenant's system caller holding files.upload + files.manage over that site) then `POST …/:id/data-sheets` (Replace SDS: the current one of its language SUPERSEDED, kept, in the same command; never older, never the same version; event sds.replaced), `GET …/:id/data-sheets/:sheetId/download`; floor (floor.work at the device's site): `GET /safety/floor/chemicals` (what a tablet keeps for the cabinet QR) and `GET /safety/floor/data-sheets/:sheetId/download` (CURRENT only). Export source `plant.chemicals`. No calendar source (the plan names no SDS deadline). Events chemical.changed, sds.replaced (live safety.view + floor.work).
`modules/safety/incidents` (B-W2b-125; QHS-265, QHS-268, QHS-270; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 18 c.1 r; GDPR art. 9; factory-domain `safety/incidents/{rules,permissions}` + the `incidentEvents` block of `safety/events.ts`; perms safety.incidents.view (anonymised: no person, injury or filing type) | .report | .manage | .people (protected, a write risk: read_only never holds it); tablets report with floor.work): office `/safety/incidents…` (list, counts with the critical banner of an INAIL deadline under 24 h or late, record with people as counts; report; edit, seen, close (held while an action is open or a filing waits), reopen, cancel; actions made into a MaintenanceJob through plant `makeJobInCommand` (source INCIDENT), a RiskMeasure (source INCIDENT naming the incident) or a TrainingSession (OPEN) in one command; people add/edit/remove; INAIL filings: the absence decides STATISTICAL (≥ 1 day) or INSURANCE (> 3), the certificate makes it DUE with dueAt = received + 48 h exactly, file with the protocol number, not needed / needed; photos and INAIL's receipt as module-owned and module-read OFFICE files (platform/files/owned.ts) through the scoped system caller); THE protected reads `protected.ts` (`…/people/open`, `…/filings/open`, `POST /safety/incidents/people`, `…/receipt/:fileId/open`): a ProtectedView row per person/filing/list written in a command FIRST, the values read after it commits (never in a receipt), audit rows never carry a value; floor `/safety/floor/incidents…` (start: 112 + first aiders in force + "here"; report (named, or WITHOUT A NAME: a near miss or hazard naming nobody, written in the floor scope as the system actor — access.ts `anonymousCaller` + `anonymousKey`: no worker or tablet in its audit row, number ledger, receipt, event or the floor-sync ledger; never queued); seen; photos within 30 minutes, up to 4). Floor-sync kind `safety.incident.report` (named only). Reminders: date timers `INAIL_FILING_DUE:-24h|-4h|0` per DUE filing (filings.ts `syncReminders`, rules `reminderPlan` folds passed stages into one that fires at once), told by the fan-out rules `notices.ts` (ALERT to safety.incidents.people; a new report ALERT to safety.incidents.manage). Handler `safety.hazardOnLock` (maintenance_request.locked with hazardReport → one HAZARD incident, OFFICE, reported by the system actor, key `incident-on-lock:<request>`). Events incident.reported | changed | seen (the only one to the floor, floor.work) | incident_action.changed | inail_filing.changed: ids, kinds, states, deadlines only. B-W2b-149: export source `plant.incidents` (platform/exports/sources/incidents.ts: the list's own read, no person/injury/filing type column) and search indexer `Incident` (platform/search/indexers/incident.ts: number, kind words, place codes; never a person or the free words).
`modules/safety/health` (B-W2b-135; QHS-252, QHS-253, QHS-254, QHS-256; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 41; GDPR art. 9; office only; factory-domain `safety/health/{rules,permissions}`, events `safety-health/events` (ids and states only: never a person, a type, an outcome or a reason); perms safety.health.view | manage (hr, company_doctor), .plans (safety_officer, company_doctor), .views (hr) — all write risk, so read_only holds none; live use waits for the company doctor (`LIVE_USE_WORDS` in every frame): the judgement only (outcome, task limits checked for medical words by `clinicalWordsIn`, day, expiry), never a diagnosis. THE rule of every read that shows a name beside a health fact: `protected.ts recordViews` writes ProtectedView in a command FIRST, the values are read after it commits (lists HEALTH_VISIT_LIST LIST/PRINT per company; a visit or a judgement OPEN; the copy COPY); the judgement list and the tiles are anonymised and record nothing. `GET /safety/health` (frame + tiles), `GET|PATCH /safety/health/plans[/:id]` (safety.view reads: no person; `needed` stays the role sheet's), `POST /safety/health/visits/list|print|proposals|plan|book`, `…/visits/:id/open|unbook|cancel|done`, `PATCH …/visits/:id`, `GET|POST /safety/health/judgements`, `…/judgements/show`, `…/:id/open|void|copy` (+ `/copy/:fileId/complete|open`; FitnessJudgement and HealthVisit are MODULE_OWNED_FILES + MODULE_READ_FILES), `GET /safety/health/views` (who looked + the fit checks), `POST /safety/health/people/:employeeId/fitness` (people.authorisations.manage: fit yes/no only, the check audited without its answer; `fitness.ts fitnessYesNoOf` is the read other modules call), the leaver's step `POST /people/leavers/:caseId/exit-visit/open` and `…/steps/:stepId/exit-visit` (`people/leavers/visit-step.ts`). store.ts `planVisitInCommand` is the one insert of a visit. jobs.ts: handlers `safety.healthVisitOnRoleChange` (employee.role_changed), `safety.healthVisitOnLeaving` (leaver.started/ended) and the daily cron `safety.healthDueList` (periodic visits; return visits after > 60 days of SICKNESS/INJURY absence only where the ATTENDANCE gate is open, the length only) plan only from plans whose doctor is recorded. Calendar counts: modules/safety/calendar/sources/health-visits.ts (B-W2b-120).
`modules/safety/records` (B-W2b-139; QHS-243, QHS-244, QHS-246, QHS-247, QHS-248; D.Lgs. 81/2008 art. 37, c.5, c.13; factory-domain `safety/records/{rules,documents,permissions}`, events `safety-records/events`; perms safety.view | safety.training.record | safety.instruction.record, floor.work on the tablet): training sessions (`GET|POST /safety/training/sessions`, `GET|PATCH …/:id` (once people signed only where and the note), `POST …/:id/paper` (PAPER attendances; the sheet's scan = a file of TrainingSession), `…/:id/countersign` (office PAPER | OFFICE, with each person's language check), `…/:id/attendances/:attendanceId/void`, `…/:id/close` (heldOn ≤ today, every attendance countersigned or voided; one TrainingRecord per countersigned attendance, valid until heldOn + the course's refresherMonths, FAILED when the language was not understood), `…/:id/cancel` (with the reason)); every session command takes `factory.training-session|<tenant>|<session>` first; a person's records (`GET /safety/training/people/:employeeId`: records, DueCells over B-W2b-133's `trainingNeedsOf`, instruction entries), `POST /safety/training/records` (training given elsewhere, ≤ 100 people; another valid-until only with its reason; certificate = a file CERTIFICATE of the record), `POST /safety/training/records/:id/correct` (a NEW record with correctsId + reason, once; VOID = never happened; records are append-only), `GET …/records/:id/pdf` (in memory); due and overdue (`GET /safety/training/due` + `/due/pdf`: one row per person × course by name; tiles over the company/site/role asked, never narrowed by the search); the instruction register (`GET|POST /safety/training/instruction`, `…/:id/paper`, `…/:id/void`). Floor (`/safety/floor/training/sessions[/:id[/sign|/countersign]]`, `/safety/floor/training/instruction/pending|/:id/sign`): the person signs with THEIR OWN badge or name and PIN (`provedWorker` + `claimProved`), the trainer countersigns with theirs; FLOOR_PIN rows keep the SHA-256 of the words signed; online only (no floor-sync kind). Reads of what people hold (`holdings.ts`: `recordFactsOf`, `classCoursesOf`, `courseHoldingsOf`) are floor-safe columns only: the qualification check reads them.

`platform/search` (B-W2a-32; PLT-153; SCR-004 palette, SCR-248 All results): ONE SearchEntry per record (search.prisma), written only by the worker in the tenant's system context. The registry is the FOLDER `platform/search/indexers/` (one `<type>.ts` per record type, default-exporting `defineIndexer({ recordType, area, viewPermission, on: { '<event>': 'subject' | (event, tx) => ids }, read(tx, filter, take) })`; `indexer-files.ts` finds them for the worker, the tests and `build.mjs`: a step that makes a record searchable adds its file and edits no list). Handlers `search.index` (every event an indexer follows: one command per event, key `search-<eventId>`, entries upserted only when they change, removed when the record is gone) and `search.rebuild` (`search.rebuild_requested` from `POST /search/rebuild`, search.rebuild: every record read again a page of 200 per command; entries of gone records swept); at worker start a tenant whose index was never built (no `search.rebuild` audit row) is built. `GET /search?q=&type=&limit=&offset=` (any office session): every query word inside the folded text or, from 3 letters, `word_similarity` ≥ 0.45; the query typed whole matches `number` exactly first; filtered by the entry's viewPermission and company/site/party against the reader's grants (null = not limited); `counts` per type, `asOf`. An entry holds no money and no worker's name (factory-domain `search/text` `searchTextOf` refuses an amount with a currency). W2a-32 indexes Party, Quote, SalesOrder, Style, Item, PurchaseOrder, WorkOrder, Lot, Hide, GoodsReceipt, Shipment, Invoice. B-W2a-83 adds ReturnAuthorisation, RepairOrder (sales), Complaint (quality) and SupplierClaim (purchasing). B-W2b-23 adds every other W1 and W2a record type: Payment, SupplierInvoice (finance), Inspection, QualityHold (status ACTIVE said as HELD, the Holds page's words), Nonconformance (quality), Certificate, LabelTemplate, PpeRegisterEntry (compliance), StockCount, Location, Ddt (warehouse; a DDT has no page and opens its record panels), SubcontractOrder (purchasing), Operation, SizeScale (products), Machine, MaintenanceJob (plant); locations and certificates had no event, so `inventory.location_changed` and `compliance.certificate_changed` are emitted by their commands (and the locations import) for the index. Needs the `pg_trgm` extension on the server (…_platform_w2a_extensions).
`platform/search/partner` (B-W2b-168; SAL-084): portal search, `GET /portal/search?q=&group=&limit=` (portal.search, every main partner role; routes in `platform/portal/search/` because the route loader does not look under a folder with its own routes file). A partner view per record type (`partner/views/<type>.ts` + one line in `partner/views.ts`; `definePartnerView({ recordType, group, kinds, grain?, read, lookup? })`) names its partner-visible fields: their reference first, ours second, the states the partner knows it in, read in the PARTNER scope (never select a hidden column: the query fails). Candidates come from SearchEntry (system scope: party, the view types, the session's company or tenant-wide) and the views' lookups (their refs and article codes the index lacks); each is re-read through the wall, checked with can() (and the view's grain: an invoice needs the price) and kept only when the query matches its partner fields (factory-domain `search/partner/match`). Groups orders, documents, returns, jobs, pos per kind; `single` for Enter. A returns view waits for the partner role's read of returns.

## Commands (from the repository root unless noted)
    cp apps/factory-api/.env.example apps/factory-api/.env   # once per worktree; loopback values only, your <step>
    cd packages/factory-db && npx prisma generate            # once after npm ci (typecheck and tests need the client)
    npm run dev -w @nexus/factory-api           # :8190 unless FACTORY_PORT
    npm run dev:worker -w @nexus/factory-api    # outbox relay + event handlers; ONLY on this worktree's own database
    npm run dev:scheduler -w @nexus/factory-api # clustered crons; same refusals (start:worker / start:scheduler on dist/)
    npm run typecheck -w @nexus/factory-api
    cd apps/factory-api && npx vitest run       # needs FACTORY_DATABASE_URL: factory_test… or factory_<branch>;
                                                # *.redis.vitest.test.ts and the outbox pg test need the local Redis
    node scripts/factory/run-pg-tests.mjs apps/factory-api   # the *.pg.vitest.test.ts files, on throwaway databases
    npm run build -w @nexus/factory-api         # prisma generate, then esbuild → dist/ (index, background, routes files)
    node scripts/factory/run-guards.mjs         # includes check-route-manifest

## The core (`src/core/`)
- `app.ts`: `buildApp(config, dependencies)` returns a ready instance, or rejects (the process does not start).
  `appConfig(env)` maps the env. Dependencies: `database` (`openFactoryDatabase`), `redis` (ioredis),
  `authenticate`, `routesRoot` (`src/` or `dist/`), `routes` (tests), `logger`, `clock`.
- `context.ts`: the request context per audience (office, floor, partner, link, platform; system for jobs), its
  factory-db scope (`scopeForContext`) and its actor for `can()` (`actorOf`). A route reaches the database only
  through `request.withClient(work)` (= `withContextClient`): no context → 401 before any query.
- Database clients (B-W2b-F9): factory-db keeps one Prisma client (about 90 MB of heap) per tenant (office, system),
  floor site and partner party, shared by every person and link of it, at most `FACTORY_DATABASE_CLIENTS` (1-64,
  default 16) per process (API, worker, scheduler each). A new user, worker or link costs no client; a new tenant,
  site or party does. PG tests may mint a new actor per request again: it no longer opens a client.
- `manifest.ts` + `autoload.ts`: every route has a manifest entry `{ audiences, permission, scope }`. Routes live
  ONLY in `src/platform/<name>/routes.ts` (auth, admin, outbox, numbering, …) and `src/modules/<name>/routes.ts`
  (sales, product, …), each with `manifest.ts` beside it: `routes.ts` default-exports
  `defineRoutes(name, manifest, plugin)`, `manifest.ts` holds `defineManifest({...})`. A folder without a routes file
  of its own may group parts one level down (`platform/auth/office/routes.ts`); nothing deeper is loaded. Write full
  paths (no prefixes); test helpers that call `.post('/…')` live in a `testing/` folder (the guard skips it).
  `routesFiles()` (autoload.ts) finds them for the app, `build.mjs` and the guard alike.
  A route without an entry, an entry without a route, a bad entry, or a route anywhere else stops the boot or fails
  `check-route-manifest.mjs`.
- `build.mjs`: entry points `src/index.ts` + every routes file, ESM with code splitting, so shared code is one
  instance (the build fails if a source file lands in two outputs). `dist/` mirrors `src/`
  (`dist/platform/<name>/routes.js`, chunks beside `dist/index.js`).
- The access check runs before the body is read: session (none → 401) → audience → permission → scope
  (`company`/`site`/`party` from the context; `record` = the handler uses `can()` and `scopeWhere()` from
  `@nexus/factory-domain/core/permissions`).
- `errors.ts`: domain errors (`@nexus/factory-domain/core/errors`) and `ApiError` → `{ error: { code, message,
  requestId, issues? } }` with a stable code. Anything unexpected → 500 `internal_error`, logged, never its message.
- `logger.ts`: redacts secret keys (passwords, PINs, tokens, cookies, keys, hashes, `…Enc`, cost/margin/price/
  amount/total/wages) at any depth; requests are logged by route pattern, never URL, headers or body.
- `security.ts`: strict headers on every answer, CORS only for `FACTORY_WEB_ORIGIN`, writes with another Origin
  refused, rate limit (Redis when present), 1 MiB body limit.
- `ids.ts` (request ids), `time.ts` (the server clock). UUIDv7 `newId()` and Europe/Rome dates live in
  `@nexus/factory-domain/core/{ids,time}`.
- `queue.ts`: BullMQ queues `events`, `event-handlers`, `dead-letter`, every key under FACTORY_QUEUE_PREFIX
  (`openQueues`). `add` needs a deterministic `jobId`; a job that fails its last attempt is copied to `dead-letter`.
- `cron.ts`: `defineCron({ name, schedule, run })` + `startCrons` (scheduler only): each tick claims
  `<prefix>:cron:<job>:<second>` in Redis (SET NX PX); fails closed when Redis is down.
- `sse.ts`: live screens. One Redis subscriber per process (`LiveFeed`), `GET /live` (platform/outbox) streams what
  `liveViewFor` lets each office or floor viewer see; heartbeat 20 s.

## The outbox (`src/platform/outbox/`)
- `writer.ts`: command()'s EventWriter. An emitted event must be declared in `@nexus/factory-domain/events/catalogue`
  (module lists in `factory-domain/src/<module>/events.ts`); an undeclared type or version, or a payload its strict
  schema refuses, rolls the command back. Only this folder writes OutboxEvent rows (`check-event-contract`).
- `relay.ts` (worker): per active tenant, in its system context (office role, RLS forced), `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`
  → `events` queue (job id = event id) → PUBLISHED; failures back off, DEAD after 10 tries.
- `dispatch.ts` (worker): an event → its live message (Redis pub/sub) + one `event-handlers` job per handler
  (`defineHandler`, listed in `background.ts`; handlers write through `tools.caller` = command() as the system).
- `lag.ts` (scheduler): the `outbox.lag` cron logs pending/dead events, dead letters and failed jobs every minute.

## Office sign-in (`src/platform/auth/`, plan A1 §3.10)
- `sessions/office-auth.ts`: `createOfficeAuth({ database, environment, cookieSecret, webOrigin, clock?, mailer? })`,
  made in `src/index.ts` and passed to `buildApp` as `officeAuth`: its `authenticate` reads a one-off link's token
  (`x-factory-link`, platform/links), else the floor session cookie (the floor authenticator, below; no office
  fallback), else the office cookie (the default authenticator); the auth and link routes read it with
  `officeAuthOf(app)` (503 without it). Partner routes read the portal cookie first (Portal sign-in, below).
- Identity is per tenant: `User` (email unique in the tenant), `Session`, `Invitation`, `PasswordReset` (auth.prisma),
  all behind the tenant wall. The tenant is known before any lookup: sign-in names its slug (`factory_platform` reads
  only `Tenant`), cookies and links carry `<tenantId>.<userId>.<secret>`; only SHA-256 of a token is stored.
- Cookies `__Host-factory_session` (HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax) and `__Host-factory_csrf` (readable). Every unsafe request
  of a cookie session, and sign-in, sign-out and invitation accept, send `x-csrf-token` = the CSRF cookie
  (`GET /auth/csrf` makes one). Idle 8 h (slid at most once a minute), absolute 7 days, user and tenant ACTIVE.
- Writes go through command() on the auth object's clock (`auth.command`); the idle slide is the one unaudited write.
- Every office secret (session and CSRF token, invitation and reset token, TOTP secret, recovery codes) is minted
  BEFORE its command and is never in its input, result or audit row (sign-in and resets hand only the hash in:
  `createSession`, `createPasswordReset`); `invitations/tokens.pg.vitest.test.ts` gives each office auth command a
  receipt and scans it.
- Grants come from the person's live role assignments (below), read on every request (`sessions/grants.ts`). Risky
  routes call `requireStepUp(auth, request)` (a TOTP code on this session in the last 5 minutes; 403 with issue path
  `stepUp` otherwise): every permission declared with risk `stepUp` does.
- Links never leave the API in an answer, a stored row or a log: the `AuthMailer` seam (`sessions/mail.ts`), by default
  email out's `emailAuthMailer` (below); tests read them from the harness's fake SES mailbox (`h.mailer.last(kind, to)`).
- PostgreSQL tests: `sessions/testing/harness.ts` (`startHarness`, `Browser` cookie jar, `movableClock`, `enrolTotp`,
  `stepUp`). Keep a test file's clock moving forward once a TOTP code was used: an older step is a replay.

## Roles, permissions and money grains (`src/platform/auth/roles/`, plan A1 §2.3, §2.6, §3.11)
- Every permission is declared once in `@nexus/factory-domain/auth/permissions` (`definePermissions('<area>', {…})`,
  assembled in `catalogue.ts` with a description and a risk: read, write, stepUp). The app refuses to boot a routes
  file whose manifest names an undeclared one (`core/app.ts`; test routes passed in `routes` are not checked). A module
  that adds routes declares its permissions in the same PR and gives them to the templates that need them.
- Role templates (`@nexus/factory-domain/auth/roles/templates`): office owner, admin, finance, sales, planner, buyer,
  warehouse, production_manager, quality_inspector, floor_supervisor, read_only; floor (audience FLOOR, given to floor
  workers, never to people) operator, cutting_operator, floor_inspector, team_leader: only `floor.*` permissions, at
  most wages, no two-factor. Office roles never hold `floor.*`; custom roles are office roles. Each tenant has them as system Role
  rows, synced with the templates for every ACTIVE tenant when the worker starts and by the daily clustered cron
  `roles.syncTemplates` (`roles/sync.ts`, in each tenant's system context), at tenant set-up and the seed, and on demand
  by `POST /auth/roles/sync-templates`; one audit row per changed role. Custom roles (`custom.<key>`) are copies the
  tenant edits; a template change never widens them (the list shows `templateAdds`).
- RoleAssignment: one role for one person over every company or a list, optional sites and parties, from/to; ended,
  never deleted. A session holds the union (one Grant per live assignment); a role with `requireMfa` gives nothing
  until two-factor is proved on the session. A person gives and ends roles only within their own `roles.assign` scope
  (companies, sites, parties): a wider assignment or invitation, or ending one their scope does not wholly reach, is a
  403. Nobody gives themselves a role or edits one
  they hold; the tenant keeps an administrator (an ACTIVE person with `roles.assign` over every company): users'
  suspend/deactivate, ending an assignment and editing a role all refuse to remove the last one.
- Money grains (price, cost, margin, wages) travel in grants as the permissions `money.<grain>`; floor sessions hold at
  most wages, partner and link sessions at most price. A contract field that carries money is `money.<grain>(schema)`
  (`@nexus/factory-domain/money/marked`: optional, typed `Money<grain>`; return it with `asMoney`); free JSON holding
  money uses `markMoney(grain, value)` (audit before/after) inside a `grainedJson()` field. `roles/grain-filter.ts`
  (installed by `core/app.ts`) drops unheld marked fields from every answer and refuses a body that sends one.
  `check-money-grains` fails on an unmarked money-looking contract field, audit value or Prisma column.
- PostgreSQL tests: the harness's `addTenant` gives a tenant its system roles; `addUser({ role: 'owner' })` and
  `addRole(h, tenant, userId, key)` give a system role over every company.

## Floor sign-in (`src/platform/auth/floor/`, plan A1 §3.8, §3.10 "Floor")
- Tables (floor-auth.prisma): Device, DeviceEnrolment, Worker (+ PIN hash and lockout), Badge, WorkerRole (a worker's
  FLOOR roles), FloorSession, FloorSettings (per site, its id = the site's id). Everything a tablet does runs in the
  FLOOR scope (`factory_floor_runtime`) of the device token's tenant and site: the device (or the enrolment code) is the
  actor until an operator is signed in, then the worker. Office administrators keep them in the office scope.
- Secrets: device token `<tenantId>.<siteId>.<deviceId>.<secret>` (cookie `__Host-factory_device`, 400 days),
  enrolment code `<tenantId>.<siteId>.<enrolmentId>.<secret>` (QR code of a web link, 15 minutes, once), floor session
  token (cookie `__Host-factory_floor`, until the shift end), badge code (128 bits, Crockford base32): SHA-256 only.
  The PIN: HMAC-SHA-256 with the server's pepper (`FACTORY_PIN_PEPPER`, required in staging and production; a
  placeholder locally), then scrypt, stored `$pin$<pepper id>$scrypt$…`; `FACTORY_PIN_PEPPER_PREVIOUS` still opens
  older hashes during a rotation (re-hashed at the next sign-in). `auth.pins` is the keyring. Every secret is minted
  BEFORE its command and never enters its input, result (receipts keep results) or audit row; the Office's enrol,
  new-code, badge and PIN-reset routes take an Idempotency-Key, and a repeat answers from its receipt with the secret
  `null` (`invitations/tokens.pg.vitest.test.ts` scans receipts, audit rows and events for each floor secret). Unsafe
  floor routes pass the office CSRF double submit.
- `floor/authenticator.ts` (wired in `sessions/office-auth.ts`): the floor session cookie → floor authenticator, no
  office fallback. Live = the device token matches an ACTIVE device, the session is the device's `currentSessionId`
  (one operator per device), not ended, before its idle end (site setting, default 10 minutes, slid at most once a
  minute) and its shift end (first site shift end at least 30 minutes after sign-in, at most 12 hours); worker ACTIVE,
  its badge (if any) ACTIVE. Grants: the worker's live FLOOR roles over the device's company and site, grains capped to
  wages (`factory-domain auth/floor/grants`); none while `pinMustChange`.
- Sign-in: badge + PIN or worker id (name) + PIN; one uniform 401 for every failure; 5 wrong PINs lock the worker
  (5 minutes, doubling, not the device). A badge ALONE where the site's `FloorSettings.badgeOnlySignIn` says so (Owner
  answer F1 a; `GET|PUT /auth/floor/sites/:siteId/settings`, `GET /floor/device` → `signIn.badgeOnly`): no PIN checked,
  the session's `pinVerifiedAt` stays null, wrong-PIN counts are never cleared by it, locked or PIN-less workers are
  refused as usual; a name always needs the PIN; a badge without a PIN elsewhere is a 400 (`pin`) before any lookup.
  The floor step-up (`floor/step-up.ts`): signing acts and My day call `requireFloorStepUp` (403 issue `stepUp` unless
  a PIN was proved in the last 2 minutes: at a PIN sign-in, or `POST /floor/step-up`); answers carry
  `session.stepUpUntil`. Another worker live on the device → 409 (use `POST /floor/switch`); the same
  worker → REPLACED; an expired one → closed (IDLE, SHIFT_END). The device row is locked (`FOR UPDATE`) in the command.
- Floor commands use `floorCommand` (store.ts): a fresh server Idempotency-Key each (a sign-in is never a replay).
- Revoking a device or badge, deactivating a worker or resetting a PIN ends the sessions (`endSessions`); the
  authenticator re-checks each on every request anyway. Office routes `/auth/floor/*` are scoped by record (can() on
  the device's, worker's or site's company and site; scopeWhere() for lists); nobody manages the roles, badge or PIN of
  the worker linked to themselves. A worker's floor roles (`workers.roles`, also when a worker is made with roles) and
  credentials (`workers.credentials`: badges, PIN reset) are step-up permissions: `requireStepUp` after the record and
  scope checks.
- Legal: a worker is a name, language, optional employee number, site, status, badge and PIN hash (GDPR art. 5(1)(c));
  no biometric, photo, birth date or national id (MES-276); no productivity figure, and the registry shows "in use",
  never who (Statuto dei Lavoratori art. 4). The PG tests that prove it name these articles.
- The tablet's own state (B-W2a-42; PLT-241): `POST /floor/device/state` (public: the device token + CSRF, signed
  in or not; per-device rate limit) {appVersion, batteryPercent|null, batteryCharging|null, queuedActions}: bookkeeping
  like `lastSeenAt` (no audit row, no version bump: an office edit never conflicts); a NEW appVersion is one floor command
  `floor.deviceAppUpdated` (the device as the actor, before → after) for the Activity tab's "app updated". The office's
  device view carries batteryPercent, batteryCharging, queuedActions, statusReportedAt (a count, never whose).

## One-off links (`src/platform/links/`, plan A1 §3.10 "Links", §2.2; features SAL-099, PLT-007, PLT-035)
- A link opens ONE record of ONE party for ONE purpose (factory-domain `links/purposes`: QUOTE_ACCEPT, PO_CONFIRM,
  DOCUMENT_VIEW, DEPOSIT_INFO; since B-W2b-163 also SHIPMENT_TRACK, MEASUREMENTS, CLAIM, PAYMENT, SUPPLIER_ONBOARD,
  JOB_ACCEPT, APPROVAL, RECORD_INVITE, SECURE_FILE), with an expiry and a use limit. Token `<tenantId>.<partyId>.<linkId>.<secret>` (256
  bits, `links/token`); only its SHA-256 is stored. The web link is `<web>/l#token=…` (fragment: in no server log); the
  web sends the token in the `x-factory-link` header.
- `authenticator.ts` (chained before the office cookie): the link is read in the token's PARTY scope
  (factory_partner_runtime), hash compared in constant time, live (not revoked, not expired, uses left), tenant
  ACTIVE → context `link` with its one record and the grants of its purpose (never a role's: `links.open`, its answers
  `links.accept|decline|confirm`, `money.price` only for purposes that show the party its own document; PO_CONFIRM
  none). Anything else is ONE 404 (`LINK_UNAVAILABLE`), whatever was wrong. `linkSessionOf(request)` gives the link.
- Every use goes through `useLink(tools, request, { action, typedName?, email? })` INSIDE a command (`linkCommand`, on
  the sign-in clock): the purpose's evidence rules (`links/evidence`: typed name, email check), the use counted by one
  UPDATE that matches only a live link (a race uses a one-use link once), a LinkEvidence row (append-only), an audit
  row and the `link.used` event. Link writes need an Idempotency-Key. A module's answer route (W1: accept a quote,
  confirm a PO) has the `link` audience, its action's permission and scope `record`, and calls useLink beside its
  change; command() refuses any record but the link's.
- Office: `POST /links` (links.manage over the record's company and party; the URL is in the answer once, never in the
  command result, so no receipt holds it), `GET /links?recordType=&recordId=` (links.view, scopeWhere), `POST
  /links/:linkId/revoke` (final). A record type is linkable only once its module registers a source
  (`records.ts`, `registerLinkRecordSource`); W0 has none. Rate limit `AuthRateLimits.linkUse` on link routes.
- The partner wall (factory-db `src/ownership.ts`): a column marked `/// @grain cost|margin|wages` or `/// @internal`
  must be in the partner role's hiddenColumns (check-ownership). The OneOffLink guard trigger keeps a link fixed but for
  its uses, revocation and note.
- PostgreSQL tests: `links.pg.vitest.test.ts` (a made-up module with in-memory records registers sources and link
  routes), `guards.pg.vitest.test.ts`, `depth.pg.vitest.test.ts` (B-W2b-163).
- B-W2b-163 (links in depth; COL-051, MES-274): `POST /links/files` (a DOCUMENT_VIEW or SECURE_FILE link of a set of files,
  LinkFile; record = the first file; optional password sent apart, hashed; mailed to the contact on file or the URL once),
  link session `GET /link/files`, `POST /link/password` (5 tries, 15-minute hold; right → an unlock key, header
  x-factory-link-unlock), `POST /link/files/:fileId/download` (DOWNLOADED; a use on SECURE_FILE), `GET …/preview`
  (DOCUMENT_VIEW), `POST /link/files/received` (SECURE_FILE, once), `GET|PUT /link/draft` (MEASUREMENTS, CLAIM,
  SUPPLIER_ONBOARD; sealed; cron `links.purgeDrafts`), public `POST /link/renew` ("Ask for a new link" for every purpose: one
  answer, the abuse guard, a module renewer via `registerLinkRenewer` or the file-set renewal). Pay figures never go by link:
  `pay.ts assertNoPayByLink` in every link maker (POST /links, file sets, renewals, the payroll Send).

## Portal sign-in (`src/platform/auth/portal/`, B-W2b-159; PLT-050, PLT-066; Owner answer C3: e-mail codes only)
- Public `POST /portal/signin/start` {tenant, company?, email} answers `sent` at once for any address; the work runs
  after the answer (`auth.afterAnswer`): a PARTNER person (ACTIVE, SUSPENDED, or INVITED with an open PARTNER invitation)
  whose party is tenant-wide or of that company gets ONE mail (`mail.ts`, template `auth.portal_sign_in`, sent in-process
  like the office's auth mail; no body stored) with a 6-digit code AND a link `<web>/p/signin/continue#token=pl1.…`;
  one new code a minute per address. `POST /portal/signin/code` {tenant, company?, email, code}; `POST
  /portal/signin/link/preview` {token} reads the link WITHOUT using it (scanner-safe); `POST /portal/signin/link` {token}
  (Continue, needs the CSRF double submit) uses it. `POST /portal/sign-out`. Every unsafe call passes the office CSRF.
- Codes (factory-domain `auth/portal/codes`): 15 minutes, 5 tries, used once; stored as `pc1.` + HMAC-SHA-256 over the
  purpose, tenant, person and what it is bound to (SIGN_IN: the company; STEP_UP: the session) with a key derived from
  the cookie secret — the binding lives in the hash, PortalSignInCode has no company or session column. `issueCode`
  revokes the person's live code of the purpose first (the one-live partial index). Checked under the row lock in ONE
  command; a wrong code counts a try. An address without an account or a code answers the same words from the abuse
  guard's own count (`tries`, reset with each new code), after the same answer floor (`service.ts`, 400 ms).
- After a right code only: refused in words (`access/<PROBLEM>`, 403) when the person is paused, the access ended, the
  invitation ended, the portal is off (PortalSettings.portalOn) or the company has no PORTAL_TERMS in force
  (GATE-PRIVACY; `terms.ts requirePortalTerms` is the invitation command's check, B-W2b-162). Else ONE command opens a
  PARTNER Session (30 min idle slid once a minute, 12 h; Session.companyId = the portal's company) and, for an invited
  person, accepts the invitation (ACTIVE, its end of access, the partner roles it names that exist).
- `authenticator.ts`: cookie `__Host-factory_portal` = `ps1.<tenant>.<party>.<user>.<secret>`; the Session is read by its
  hash in THAT party's partner scope, the person, party, portal switch and terms in the office scope, on every request;
  grants = the person's live PARTNER roles over the session's company and party, price at most, NONE until the first
  visit acknowledged the terms in force; an office preview (read-only) holds the reads its party's people could do, no
  money (B-W2b-162 `partners/preview-grants.ts`). `withPortalSessions` (chained in
  `sessions/office-auth.ts` after links): a partner-only route reads the portal cookie or nothing; a route shared with
  office or floor (files, threads) reads it only when it is the request's one session cookie. `portalSessionOf(request)`.
- `GET /portal/me`, `POST /portal/sessions/refresh`, `GET /portal/terms`, `POST /portal/first-visit` {displayName?,
  locale?, noticeId, noticeLocale, sha256} (PartnerNoticeAck once per version), `POST /portal/step-up/start` + `POST
  /portal/step-up` {code}: a STEP_UP code bound to the session; sensitive partner routes call
  `requirePortalStepUp(app, request)` (403 issue `stepUp` unless proved in the last 5 minutes).
- B-W2b-173 (the portal frame): `GET /portal/me` also names `party.code` (the portal address `/p/<code>`), `party.kinds`
  (an office preview's rail) and `company.legal` {it, en} (the foot's c.c. art. 2250 block, as the documents print it);
  `POST /portal/me/locale` {locale} = the person's own language (command `portal.changeLocale`, audited; a preview: 403).
- B-W2b-196 (the customer's Account, `self.ts`): `POST /portal/me/profile` {displayName?, locale?} (command
  `portal.changeProfile`, audited before → after, nothing twice), `GET /portal/sessions` (the person's own live PARTNER
  sessions: started, last used, the User-Agent, `current`; never a preview's) + `POST /portal/sessions/end-others` (revokes
  the others, SIGNED_OUT, audited on the User), `GET /portal/company` (portal.colleagues.view: the party's names, the tax
  identity in force, the default invoice address, the registered office, the payment terms it/en; no amount). All in the
  office scope as the person. Partner live (factory-domain `PARTNER_LIVE_EVENTS`): `delivery_address.checked`,
  `partner_user.invited|changed` reach the party's sessions.
- Scheduler cron `portal.purgeCodes` (`purge.ts`, minute 41 hourly): codes expired more than a day ago are deleted.
- Office paths answer a partner address in words: an office invitation 409, a password reset request sends nothing,
  confirm and "require a reset" 409 (a partner person has no password).
- The abuse guard (`src/platform/abuse/`, factory-domain `abuse/{policies,pow}`): a public route calls
  `abuseGuardOf(app).check(request, reply, '<policy>', { subject, proof })` first: per-subject and per-network (IPv6 /64)
  windows → 429 "Wait N minutes before asking again." (issue `retryAfter`, Retry-After); after suspicious traffic (hits or
  `failed()` wrong answers) 429 issue `challenge` until the body carries `proof` {challenge, nonce} from public `GET
  /abuse/challenge?policy=` (self-hosted SHA-256 proof of work, HMAC-signed for the network, 5 minutes, used once). Counters
  in Redis under `<prefix>:<env>:abuse:` (keys are HMACs), this process's memory while Redis is down; tests
  `useAbuseGuard(createAbuseGuard({ store: memoryAbuseStore() … }))`. Policies `links.renew` and `public.lookup` are
  declared for B-W2b-163/166/170. PG tests: `portal.pg.vitest.test.ts` (helpers in `portal/testing/portal.ts`).
- B-W2b-164 (the sign-in screen): public `GET /branding/portal-sign-in?tenant=<slug>&company=<code>` or `?companyId=` (platform/
  branding `portal-head.ts`, rate-limited like the public branding): whose portal — the slug and company code the start route
  takes, the public branding, the company's default CONVERSATION address (whom to ask) and its legal block {it, en}; one 404.

## Partner organisations, people and roles (`src/platform/auth/partners/`, B-W2b-162; PLT-006, PLT-021, PLT-022, SAL-060, SCM-092)
- A partner organisation is a Party; its people are User rows (PARTNER, one party, a kind fitting the party's roles:
  CUSTOMER ← CUSTOMER/BRAND, SUPPLIER, SUBCONTRACTOR, GUEST ← CONSULTANT). Roles: factory-domain `auth/roles/partner-templates.ts`
  (customer Buyer, Finance, Quality, Designer, Viewer, Admin, price only for Buyer, Finance, Admin; supplier Orders, Invoices,
  Quality, Admin; subcontractor Jobs, Admin — never a price, C2; guest Accountant), system roles of audience PARTNER synced
  with the office ones (`SYSTEM_ROLE_TEMPLATES`, roles/system-roles.ts). A person holds ONE main role and the capabilities
  `partner-comment|upload|approve` (COL-062) beside it; a portal write checks its page's permission AND portal.comment,
  portal.upload or portal.approve. Partner permissions (`portal.*`) are flagged `partner` in the catalogue: never in an office
  or floor role (`assignable` leaves them out; `partnerAssignable`). A portal step gives its permissions to the partner
  templates in templates.ts PARTNER_ADDS (one line per step); a kind's Admin follows.
- Office (`/partners…`, can() over the party, a tenant-wide party written only over every company; step-up but preview):
  the Portal access tab, settings (portalOn, maxUsers, allowedRoleKeys, progressShared = C1, customers only), invite (the
  sign-in page by mail, no token; GATE-PRIVACY `requirePortalTerms`; seats = ACTIVE + SUSPENDED + openly INVITED), resend,
  revoke, change (role, capabilities, end of access), suspend/reactivate/deactivate, preview (a PARTNER Session marked as
  the office person's preview, 1 h, the portal cookie in the office browser; audited `partners.preview`), organisations.
- B-W2b-180 (office screens): `GET /auth/users` also says `audience`, `partyId`, `partnerKind`, `accessUntil` (Admin › Users
  keeps partner people out of Office and lists them under Partners); every portal sign-in emits `partner_user.signed_in`
  {userId, partyId, firstSignIn} (live partners.view: the Portal access tab's live line).
- Portal (`/portal/access`, `/portal/colleagues…`): the admin (portal.colleagues.manage + `requirePortalStepUp`) invites,
  changes and deactivates colleagues of its own kind only, never itself; a colleague gets the admin's end of access.
  Commands run as the partner person in the office scope (`byPartner` in audit and events); the first sign-in accepts the
  invitation (B-W2b-159). Events `partner_portal.settings_changed`, `partner_user.invited|changed|access_ending`.
- Scheduler cron `partners.accessEnding` (07:20): people whose access ends within 7 days are mailed once per end
  (receipt key `partners-access-ending:<person>:<end>`). PG tests: `partners.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Tasks and notifications for partners (`src/platform/portal/{tasks,notifications,home}/`, B-W2b-167; COL-043, PLT-148, COL-063)
- ONE task list (PartnerTask) for every request to a partner. A source (one file in `portal/tasks/sources/`, one line in its
  index.ts) opens its request IN the command that makes it (`openTaskInCommand`, sourceKey `<prefix>:<what>`, one per record,
  kind and source) and the answering command closes it (`closeTasksInCommand`; a partner/link scope only answers: DONE). No
  assignee: a task is for the party's people whose roles hold its kind's needs (factory-domain `portal/tasks/kinds.ts`,
  `isTaskFor`: portal.home.view + portal.approve|upload|comment, the price grain for money). Office: `GET|POST
  /partners/:partyId/tasks` (asks by hand: source `Ask`, partner may mark done), `POST /partner-tasks/:id/cancel|reopen`,
  `PATCH /partner-tasks/:id` (due). Portal: `GET /portal/tasks` (due first, badges), `POST …/:id/done|undo` (Undo 10 min,
  reopen in the office scope as the person), `GET /portal/tasks/handover/:userId` (admin). Cron `portalTasks.remind` (:25).
- Partner notifications: worker handler `portalNotifications.fanOut` runs `portal/notifications/rules/*` (task opened/
  reminded, PO sent, order confirmed, promise moved, shipped, invoice issued, claim sent, our partner message) for the party's
  readers (`tasks/people.ts partyReaders`: portal ON, active, roles reaching the record's company); rows carry partyId;
  INSTANT topics mailed at once (`portal.notification`), DAILY/WEEKLY by cron `portalNotifications.digest`
  (`portal.digest`); deliveries EMAIL / EMAIL_DIGEST. Every mail: Manage e-mails + the signed unsubscribe link (key from
  FACTORY_COOKIE_SECRET). `GET|PUT /portal/email-settings` (transactional ORDERS/SHIPPED/INVOICES locked INSTANT; advisory
  lock per person), public `POST /portal/unsubscribe[/preview]`, `GET /portal/notifications[/counts]`, `POST …/read` (office
  scope as the person), `GET /portal/live` (core/sse.ts partner audience: one stream per portal session,
  factory-domain `portal/notifications/live.ts PARTNER_LIVE_EVENTS` decides). `GET /portal/home`: counts from
  `portal/home/sources/`. PG tests: `portal/tasks/partner-frame.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
- B-W2b-F14 (PartnerTask.assigneeId, wave 23): a task is still for everyone `isTaskFor` names, and one of them may hold it:
  `POST /portal/tasks/:id/assign {version, assigneeId|null}` (take it, hand it to an ACTIVE colleague of the party whose role
  acts on it, or back to everyone; refused first in words — `task/<STATUS>`, `body/assigneeId/INACTIVE|NO_ACCESS|CANNOT_ACT`,
  another party's person 404 — then the office scope as the person: `store.ts assignInCommand`, event
  `partner_task.assigned`), `GET /portal/tasks?mine=true` ("Mine"; counts.mine), the office list and the hand-over read name
  who has it (`assigned`). Deactivating a partner person (`auth/partners/people.ts statusInCommand`, `auth/users/lifecycle.ts`)
  gives their OPEN tasks back to everyone in the same command (`tasks/people.ts handBackInCommand`). PG tests:
  `portal/tasks/assign.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Supplier orders in the portal (`src/modules/purchasing/portal-orders/`, B-W2b-174; SCM-094, SCM-086; Owner answers C2, P2)
- Partner routes (in purchasing's routes.ts/manifest.ts; `portal.purchaseOrders.view`, scope party, no grain): `GET
  /portal/purchase-orders?chip=&q=` (chips, To confirm / late counts), `GET …/:id` (lines in the supplier's terms, what
  was confirmed, proposed and received, what changed in the held version, answers so far, substitute choices), `GET
  …/:id/revisions`, `GET …/:id/copy` (the price-free PDF, painted on request, never stored), `POST …/:id/confirm`
  (ALL_AS_ASKED or BY_LINE with SUBSTITUTE and CHANGE besides W1's four) and `POST …/:id/decline` (PRICE besides W1's
  reasons). Answers also need the person's `portal.approve` (403 in words; never a preview) and run in the partner scope
  with the order's version; lines as asked are confirmed at once, changes wait for the buyer (CHANGES_PROPOSED). Since wave
  23 (B-W2b-F13a) a portal answer is stored as what it is: confirmedVia PORTAL + confirmedByUserId, a proposal
  proposedByUserId with proposalLinkId cleared (a link proposal clears proposedByUserId); the buyer's accept confirms a
  portal proposal PORTAL by its person; a change back to DRAFT clears confirmedByUserId too (the guard refuses it otherwise).
- Only orders of the portal's company that we sent; a DRAFT of revision ≥ 2 shows the revision the supplier holds (its
  revision row), never the draft. Item, colour, company, site, sender, receipts and substitute choices (the party's
  approved sources) are read in the system scope for that order only.
- CONFIRM_PO tasks (`portal-orders/tasks.ts`, source `PurchaseOrder`): opened in the send, done by the answer (portal or
  link), withdrawn by cancel, change/counter or goods received while SENT, reopened by the buyer's refusal. Home counts
  `purchase_orders.to_confirm|late`. W1's accept refuses a proposal with a substitute (counter-propose instead); the
  revision start clears the substitute columns; the buyer's note says "in the portal" (event `source`). PG tests:
  `portal-orders/portal-orders.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
## Subcontractor jobs (`src/modules/purchasing/portal-jobs/`, B-W2b-176; SCM-154 to SCM-157, PLN-163; Owner answers C2, C3)
- A job is a subcontract order as its workshop sees it: never a fee or amount. Routes live in purchasing's routes.ts and
  manifest.ts (autoload does not look under a module folder that has its own routes file). Reads (`read.ts`): the order,
  kit lines, progress reports and return notices in the caller's own scope (portal / link: factory_partner_runtime; select
  JOB_ORDER_COLUMNS, never the hidden columns), the operations, cells, bundles, items, lots and DDTs in the system scope.
- Portal (`portal.jobs.view`, the workshop's Jobs role; an act also needs the capability: accept, decline, kit
  `portal.approve`; progress, send-back `portal.comment`; a damage photo `portal.upload`; a preview never acts):
  `GET /portal/jobs?chip=&q=`, `GET /portal/jobs/:id`, `POST …/accept|decline|kit|progress|returns`,
  `POST …/kit/photos` (+ `/:fileId/complete`: a PHOTO of the kit line through the system uploader, OFFICE + PARTNER).
- Link JOB_ACCEPT: the office's `POST /purchasing/subcontracting/orders/:id/job-link` (mailed, template
  `purchasing.job_link`, or the URL once); `GET /link/job`, `POST /link/job/accept|move-date|decline` (useLink in the act).
- Office: `GET …/orders/:id/workshop` (what the workshop said, the links, the receive back each announced send-back
  prefills), `POST …/workshop/date` (the answer to a proposed date), `…/workshop/accept|decline|kit|progress|returns`
  (C3: the office acts for a workshop without e-mail, via OFFICE), `…/workshop/returns/:noticeId/cancel` (receive).
  `POST …/receive` takes `returnNoticeId` (the notice becomes RECEIVED with the receipt).
- Every act is ONE command naming the order with the page's version (`acts.ts`); events `subcontract_order.job_sent|
  job_answered|date_decided|kit_checked|progress_reported|return_announced|return_cancelled` (factory-domain
  `subcontract-jobs/events.ts`). Tasks CONFIRM_KIT and REPORT_PROGRESS (source `SubcontractJob`) open at the kit send;
  W2a's commands call `tasks.ts settleJobInCommand` after they move an order. PG tests: `portal-jobs.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Ship notices and the inbound board (`src/modules/purchasing/ship-notices/`, `modules/inventory/receiving/from-ship-notice.ts`, B-W2b-182; SCM-106, SCM-107, SCM-110)
- Partner routes (purchasing's routes.ts/manifest.ts; `portal.shipNotices.view`, scope party, no grain): `GET /portal/ship-notices` (state
  Announced / In transit / Received / Received with differences), `…/shippable` (open lines of CONFIRMED / PARTLY_RECEIVED orders),
  `…/template` (the packing list's CSV/XLSX), `…/:id` (lines, packing list, a verdict per hide and line, what holds the send), `POST` (a DRAFT
  from a confirmed order), `PATCH …/:id` (terms, lines; until received), `PUT …/:id/packing-list` (by hand), `POST …/:id/packing-list/sheet`
  (the tannery's sheet read in the request with the registry's importer `purchasing.packingList`, verdicts per row; `apply` keeps it; the
  file is never stored), `POST …/:id/send`, `POST …/:id/cancel` (a SENT one with a reason; a DRAFT is removed). Writes also need the
  person's `portal.upload` (403 in words; never a preview). Drafts, lines and hides in the partner scope (no createdById: no grant);
  the SEND as the tenant's system: `assertRequiredDocuments` ('ShipNotice' → SENT), the ASN number, SENT; NOT AS ORDERED needs a note.
- Checks (factory-domain purchasing/ship-notices/rules.ts `checkShipNotice`): grade, thickness, one dye batch (also across earlier
  deliveries), area vs the line and the order's tolerance (with what was received and is on other sent notices), hide count, Cr VI test
  report on file (the notice's or the supplier's TEST_REPORT). CHECK never holds anything.
- Office: `GET /inventory/receiving/inbound?from&days&siteId` (inventory.stock.view; SENT notices + CONFIRMED/PARTLY_RECEIVED orders'
  expected lines not on a sent notice), `GET /inventory/receiving/ship-notices/:id` (the receipt prefilled: a lot per dye batch, each hide's
  declared area), `PATCH …/:id` (dock note, inventory.receive). `POST /inventory/receipts` takes `shipNoticeId` (office only): the receipt
  names it and, POSTED, the notice is RECEIVED in the same command with each line's received quantity; a reversal puts it back to SENT.
  Events `ship_notice.sent|changed|cancelled|received|reopened` (module folder `factory-domain/src/ship-notices/`), partner live too.
  Home counts `deliveries.to_announce|on_the_way`. PG tests: `ship-notices/ship-notices.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
## Supplier documents and profile in the portal (`src/modules/purchasing/portal-profile/`, B-W2b-189; SCM-096, SCM-097)
- Portal (purchasing's routes.ts/manifest.ts, scope party, no grain): `GET /portal/supplier-documents` ("We ask for": the
  company's required-document rules MISSING/EXPIRED on the supplier's record and its open POs, ship notices and jobs, a
  company-wide kind once on the party, plus open UPLOAD_DOCUMENT tasks; "Your documents"), `POST /portal/supplier-documents`
  (+ `/:fileId/complete`; `portal.supplierDocuments.view` + the person's `portal.upload`; `assertOutsideUploadsOpen`): a
  CERTIFICATE file of the party (or the record asked about) uploaded as the tenant's system caller, OFFICE only, NOT tagged,
  so it counts for nothing until accepted. `GET /portal/supplier-profile`, `POST …/changes` (`portal.supplierProfile.change`,
  the Admin), `POST …/changes/:requestId/withdraw`: a change request (≤ 20 address/contact changes, parties' own rules, the
  version each row was seen at) that changes nothing until the buyer's yes.
- Office: `GET /purchasing/supplier-documents`, `POST …/:fileId/accept {category, expiresOn, replacesFileId?, version}`
  (tags it, shares it PARTNER with the party; a replacement becomes the replaced document's NEXT VERSION as a copy, which
  supersedes it and closes its expiry request) and `…/refuse {reason, version}` (an UPLOAD_DOCUMENT task, source
  `SupplierDocument`, due in 14 days) — `purchasing.supplierDocuments.review`; `GET /purchasing/supplier-changes`, `POST
  …/:requestId/approve|refuse` — `purchasing.supplierChanges.decide` (applies with the parties module's inserts and version
  bumps; a row changed since stops it, 409). Today kinds SUPPLIER_DOCUMENT and SUPPLIER_CHANGE (Approvals).
- Since wave 23 (B-W2b-F13a) uploads and change requests are rows of SupplierDocumentUpload / PartyChangeRequest
  (`store.ts` the only reader): announce = INSERT WAITING (party row locked: one upload per file), complete =
  `receivedAt`, accept/refuse = the decision columns by the row's compare-and-set; a request = INSERT OPEN (party row locked:
  one open request per address, contact or website), withdraw / approve / refuse = `version = read + 1` (409 on 0 rows).
  Each act still writes its audit row on the party (the actions above; the older ones are kept as history). A request may
  change the website (COMPANY change; Party.website, also in the parties contract). `parties.ts` moveParty calls
  `assertRequiredDocuments` for a move to ACTIVE. PG tests: `portal-profile.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Supplier questionnaire (`src/modules/purchasing/questionnaire/`, B-W2b-172; SCM-023; Forced Labour Regulation (EU) 2024/3015)
- Versions (factory-domain `purchasing/questionnaire`: questions as data, `STARTER_QUESTIONS`, `questionProblems`): `GET|POST
  /purchasing/questionnaires` (the screen's company; `from` STARTER | LATEST or the questions), `PATCH …/:id`, `POST …/:id/publish`
  (purchasing.questionnaire.view / .manage); one draft at a time; a published version is fixed (the DB holds it).
- The link: `POST /purchasing/suppliers/:partyId/questionnaire-link` (.send; SUPPLIER_ONBOARD × Party, the company's newest
  published version must exist; earlier open links of the supplier withdrawn in the same command; mailed, template
  `purchasing.questionnaire_link`, or the URL once). Renewer registered for SUPPLIER_ONBOARD × Party.
- Link session: `GET /link/supplier-questionnaire` (the version read in the system scope), `POST …/vat-check` (shape; EU outside
  Italy via the VIES runtime, nothing kept), `POST …/documents` (+ `/:fileId/complete`; `assertOutsideUploadsOpen`; a CERTIFICATE
  file of the Party, OFFICE+LINK, category QUESTIONNAIRE), `GET …/code-of-conduct` (the SIGN question's document as a PDF),
  `POST /link/supplier-questionnaire` (checked whole with `answerProblems`, then ONE link command: useLink submit + the answer
  INSERT with `linkId`; then the PDF put; then ONE system command: the PDF on the Party's files (audit
  `purchasing.questionnaire.answerPdf` names it), the draft dropped, the review Task (id `reviewTaskId(responseId)`)).
- Office: `GET /purchasing/suppliers/:partyId/questionnaire` (.view), `POST /purchasing/questionnaire-answers/:id/review`
