  (.review; once: ACCEPTED tags each document with its question's category + expiry and shares it PARTNER; QUESTIONS writes an
  OUTBOUND message on the Party thread; the task done). Events `supplier_questionnaire.*` (factory-domain
  `supplier-questionnaire/events.ts`). PG tests: `questionnaire/questionnaire.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Supplier qualification and bank details (`src/modules/purchasing/{bank,qualification}/`, `portal-profile/bank.ts`, B-W2b-315; SCM-007, SCM-020, SCM-022, SCM-027)
- Bank details (factory-domain `purchasing/bank`; B-W2b-287's SupplierBankAccount, never changed in place): `GET|POST /purchasing/suppliers/:partyId/bank`
  (masked accounts, pills, the numbers on file a call-back may dial; a proposal asks a fresh step-up), `POST …/bank/:accountId/call-back`
  (never who proposed or wrote it; a contact or address created and last changed BEFORE the proposal), `…/verify` (step-up; the second
  person: a switched-on SUPPLIER_BANK_CHANGE rule asks a yes first — 202, the approver's yes activates it in `platform/approvals/kinds/
  supplier-bank-change.ts` — else ACTIVE now, the old account RETIRED first), `…/refuse`, `…/retire`, `…/reveal` (finance, step-up,
  audited; the only answer with an IBAN). Portal: `GET|POST /portal/supplier-bank` (masked; the proposal in the PARTNER scope after an
  e-mail step-up). Payments read `bank/store.ts payableAccountOf` (the ACTIVE account only). No IBAN in any mail, event, audit row or list.
- The vault and qualification (factory-domain `purchasing/qualification/{vault,rules}`): `GET /purchasing/suppliers/:partyId/documents`
  (what we ask per supplier kind and per material, what is in force, the office review of uploads and profile changes — decided with
  B-W2b-189's routes), `GET /purchasing/suppliers/documents-summary`, `GET|POST|PATCH /purchasing/suppliers/:partyId/qualifications[/:id]`
  + `…/decide|suspend|lift|withdraw`; planning asks `qualification/qualifications.ts qualificationsFor` (qualified now, else why).
- The compliance gate on a PO (`qualification/gate.ts`, three lines in send.ts): a leather needing a chromium VI test without a test
  report in force, a PPE component without a VALID register certificate, a company rule before SENT: held at Send (send checks
  COMPLIANCE_*); `GET /purchasing/orders/:id/compliance`, `POST …/compliance-override` (reason; purchasing.send), `…/decide` (a yes with
  step-up, purchasing.compliance.override, never the asker — the approvals history guard refuses it too), `…/withdraw`,
  `GET /purchasing/compliance-overrides[/:purchaseOrderId]`. Requests are the engine's domain lines on the PurchaseOrder (what each
  covers on its `purchasing.compliance.overrideAsk` audit row); the order's version does not move. PG tests: `bank/bank.pg`,
  `qualification/qualification.pg`.

## Purchase orders in depth (`src/modules/purchasing/po-depth/`, B-W2b-296; SCM-052, SCM-061, SCM-065, SCM-066, SCM-088, SCM-147)
- Its routes and manifest lines are in purchasing's routes.ts / manifest.ts (one block each: a folder with a routes file is not
  looked into by the autoloader).
- Rules in factory-domain `purchasing/po-depth/` (schedule, dates, currency, ship-to, tolerances; send-check codes in `codes.ts`,
  listed by `purchasing/orders` SEND_CHECK_CODES). Office routes (purchasing.view / .manage; one command on the order, its version):
  `GET /purchasing/orders/:id/depth` (deliveries, the supplier's days and their history, rate + value (money.cost), ship-to, each
  line's receipt tolerance), `PUT|DELETE …/lines/:lineId/deliveries` (a DRAFT line split by day, Σ = the line, the line's need
  date = the first day), `POST …/lines/:lineId/supplier-date` (a delivery's first day; else a move with its PoDateChange row; a
  scheduled line's own day follows its earliest delivery with a row of its own), `PUT …/rate` (DRAFT in another currency;
  `value.ts refreshCompanyValue` keeps companyNetAmount current from orders.ts refreshTotal), `PUT …/ship-to`.
- Hooks other files call (`hooks.ts`, no command imports): lines.ts (remove deliveries first; a scheduled line's quantity, unit and
  need date are refused alone), revisions/change.ts (deliveries lose the supplier's days), confirm/answer.ts, portal-orders/answer.ts,
  confirm/decide.ts (a line confirmed as asked confirms its deliveries), inventory/receipts/record.ts (`receiptPlaceOf`: a ship-to
  order lands on the workshop's place; the floor is refused). views.ts readFacts/checksOf add the depth facts and checks; send.ts
  prints the ship-to address and the deliveries.
- Delivery tolerances: `GET|POST /purchasing/tolerances`, `PATCH …/:id`, `POST …/:id/archive|reactivate`
  (purchasing.tolerances.manage over the company; every company's: a role over every company). Read at every receipt by
  `purchasing/receiving.ts receiptTolerancesOf` (record.ts, receiving/progress.ts, receipts/reverse.ts): the line's own area
  tolerance, else the most specific ACTIVE rule; beyond it BLOCK refuses and APPROVAL refuses too (no receipt approval kind).
  Events purchase_order.dates_confirmed | date_changed, delivery_tolerance.changed. PG: `po-depth/po-depth.pg.vitest.test.ts`.

## Purchase lines by kind (`src/modules/purchasing/line-kinds/`, `src/modules/product/materials/variants.ts`, B-W2b-328; SCM-076, SCM-077, SCM-186)
- Rules in factory-domain `purchasing/line-kinds/` (rolls, certificates on B-W2b-255's bought-in rule, variants, the send checks
  in `codes.ts`/`checks.ts`, listed by `purchasing/orders` SEND_CHECK_CODES). Routes in purchasing's and materials' routes.ts /
  manifest.ts (one block each). `GET /purchasing/orders/:id/line-kinds` (purchasing.view: rolls with what they make, a protector's
  certificate with its check today and the register's choices, the variant with the material's choices, the kind checks) and
  `PUT …/lines/:lineId/kind {version, rolls?, certificateId?, variantCode?}` (purchasing.manage, DRAFT; rolls set the quantity
  when the unit can say it; a line without a colour takes its variant's).
- Variants: `GET|POST /product/materials/:id/variants`, `PATCH …/variants/:variantId` (supplier code, archive/restore; never
  archived while an open PO line buys it), `GET …/variants/bom-sizes` (also product.styles.view: the variant each BOM size uses,
  by size and colour — no BOM column names one). Event material.updated (fields ["variants"]).
- Hooks (`line-kinds/hooks.ts`, no command imports): lines.ts insertLine names a PPE line's certificate by default, editLine refuses
  a quantity/unit its rolls make or a colour its variant is not of; inventory/receipts/record.ts gives a variant line's lots their
  variant (the database requires it) and checks a named certificate again on the receipt's day (no longer holding: the lots start
  in quarantine with the reason). views.ts readFacts/checksOf add the kind facts and checks; send.ts prints them on the PDF.
  PG: `line-kinds/line-kinds.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
## PO approvals and worklists (`src/modules/purchasing/{po-approvals,expediting}/`, B-W2b-305; SCM-057, SCM-064, SCM-068, SCM-078, SCM-080)
- Every send passes the approval gate (`po-approvals/gate.ts`, called by send.ts sendAs, also Send n): a DRAFT a switched-on
  PURCHASE_ORDER rule holds for is checked (W1's send checks), then asked IN the send's command (engine askApproval; the order
  DRAFT → PENDING_APPROVAL naming the task; the request keeps the send's words as its changes) → 202 `{order, approval}`; nothing
  is made or mailed. A revision 2+ shows the revision and the value since the revision sent before (`orderValue` cost); the kind's
  facts add `revision` and `valueIncrease`. The kind (`platform/approvals/kinds/purchase-order.ts`) fingerprints the order without
  its status (DRAFT = PENDING_APPROVAL), with its depth (schedule, rate, ship-to) and its new line columns; its hooks are
  `po-approvals/hooks.ts`: the yes emits purchase_order.approved (the order stays frozen), a no / withdrawal / STALE → DRAFT.
- The yes sends it: worker handler `purchasing.sendApproved` (`po-approvals/approved-send.ts`) runs send.ts `sendAs` as the person
  who asked (their live grants over the system client; `SendHost` gives the link address and the system caller), key
  `po-approved-send-<taskId>`; a refusal in words is held (thread note mentioning them, purchase_order.send_held), and the
  buyer's Send sends an approved waiting order (or a draft a generic request approved as it is) without asking again.
- `GET /purchasing/orders/:id/approval`, `POST …/approval/withdraw` (purchasing.send), `POST …/duplicate` (purchasing.manage; a new
  draft from some or all lines; `purchase_order.copied`), `GET …/demand` (the latest DONE plan's pegging on each line),
  `GET /purchasing/expediting` (late · not confirmed after confirm-by · confirmed later than needed, ranked by customer orders at
  risk; what each line blocks), `GET|POST …/chase` (purchasing.send; a mail without link or price; `purchase_order.chased`).
  Cancel of a waiting order withdraws its request (orders.ts). PG: `po-approvals/*.pg`, `expediting/*.pg`.

## Integrations (`src/platform/{gateway,secrets,inbound}/`, plan A1 §4, §5.4)
- `gateway/`: EVERY call to an outside service is `processGateway().call(gatewayAccount(caller, accountId),
  { name, idempotent, idempotencyKey?, request? }, async (call) => …)`. It re-reads the IntegrationAccount (office or
  system caller only), refuses FAKE in production, PAUSED and DISABLED, an open circuit and an empty rate bucket (Redis,
  `<prefix>:gateway:…`; Redis down = refused), applies the timeout, retries only idempotent operations, and writes ONE
  IntegrationCall row per call (summaries redacted: secrets, personal data, the account's own secret values). Throw
  `ProviderError(message, { httpStatus })` from `fn`. Never call it inside a command's transaction.
  `scripts/factory/check-integration-calls.mjs`: no HTTP client, SDK or fetch() outside `platform/gateway/`,
  `src/adapters/` and `platform/secrets/aws-kms.ts`.
- `gateway/boot.ts`: `assertNoFakeAccountsInProduction(database, env.FACTORY_ENV)` runs at boot in index.ts and
  background.ts, right after openFactoryDatabase: production does not start while any account is FAKE.
- `secrets/`: envelope encryption (AES-256-GCM, a data key per value, wrapped by AWS KMS in staging/production or by
  FACTORY_SECRETS_MASTER_KEY in local/test). `processSecrets().seal/open(…, { tenantId, purpose })`, the `s1.` string
  form for a column of another table, `rotate`; SecretValue rows: `storeSecret(tools, …)` inside a command,
  `readSecret(client, …)`, `rotateSecret(caller, …)`. A value never appears in a row, a log or an audit row.
- `inbound/`: `POST /inbound/<provider>/<tenantId>/<accountId>` (public, raw body): verify (FAKE accounts: the fake
  HMAC verifier; others: INBOUND_PROVIDERS) → InboundWebhook receipt FIRST (dedupe key unique per provider; a bad
  signature is kept as REJECTED) + the event integration.inbound_received in one command → 200. The worker's
  `inboundHandler()` (in background.ts HANDLERS) applies a receipt once with its provider's processor. INBOUND_PROVIDERS:
  SES (SNS: basic-auth verifier; processor: platform/email-in's for "Received", in front of platform/email-out's).

## Files on R2 (`src/platform/files/`, `src/adapters/{r2,local-files}/`, plan A1 §3.6; COL-045, COL-050, PLAN §2.8)
- `StoredFile` (files.prisma): one row per file, for ONE record (`recordType` + `recordId`), never the bytes. Flow:
  `POST /files` (kind's types and size limit, sharing, retention) → files.requestUpload (PENDING_UPLOAD, random key
  `t/<tenant>/c/<company>/<yyyy>/<uuid4>`, `legal/…` for FISCAL_ARCHIVE) → a presigned PUT (5 min; type, length and
  SHA-256 signed) → `POST /files/:id/complete` (the store's HEAD) → AVAILABLE + event `file.available`, or QUARANTINED
  with its reason → `GET /files/:id/download` (60 s presigned GET, always an attachment). Rules: `rules.ts` (pure).
- Visibility (COL-050): OFFICE always; FLOOR with `floorSiteId`, PARTNER/LINK with `partyId`. Those two columns are the
  floor and partner roles' policy keys, set exactly when shared (the …_files_guards trigger), so RLS keeps a floor to its
  site's floor files and a party to its own; `audienceSees()` adds portal vs link (LINK also needs the link's record).
- Module-owned files (`owned.ts`, MODULE_OWNED_FILES): a record type whose "current" file its module decides (ComplianceDocument and Parcel: every kind; Certificate: CERTIFICATE; Ddt: FISCAL_ARCHIVE, the first stored print being the DDT's PDF, `ddt/store.ts pdfFileIdsOf`) is uploaded and confirmed only through that module's route: requestUpload/completeUpload refuse it (409 naming the route) unless the module passes `{ viaModule: true }`; generated files are not affected. Add a record type with its upload route.
- Retention: a delete is refused under legal hold or before `retainUntil` (the state machine's guard and the trigger);
  FISCAL_ARCHIVE keeps ten years at least; content columns never change; rows are never deleted (status DELETED).
- Store calls go through the gateway (`store.ts`: files.presign_upload, files.presign_download, files.head, files.delete,
  files.read (server-side work on a file's bytes, e.g. an import's sheet, size-capped); one IntegrationCall row each)
  for the tenant's R2 account: LIVE/SANDBOX → `adapters/r2` (S3 API), FAKE → the local
  store (`adapters/local-files`, local and test only; its presigned requests are `PUT|GET /local-files?token=…` on this
  API, HMAC-signed). Floor, portal and link downloads presign as the tenant's system caller after the audience check.
  Tests: `useFiles(createFilesRuntime({ gateway, local, r2: r2FileStore({ clientFor: new FakeR2().clientFor }) }))`
  (`adapters/r2/testing.ts`: the real S3 client on an in-memory transport, no network).
- Permissions (declared in `@nexus/factory-domain/auth/permissions/catalogue`, FILES_DEFINITIONS): files.read,
  files.upload, files.delete, files.manage (sharing beyond the office, also at upload; retention; legal hold). Every
  office template has files.read; the roles that work on records also files.upload; admin (and owner) files.manage and
  files.delete. Floor, portal and link sessions see files through their audience rule above, never an office template.
- Virus scan on uploads (B-W2a-42; PLT-245; `platform/files/scan/`, `adapters/clamav/`): `FACTORY_VIRUS_SCAN` off
  (default: confirmed uploads AVAILABLE unscanned, NOT_SCANNED) | fake (local/test: EICAR infected) | clamav (clamd INSTREAM
  over `node:net`, `FACTORY_CLAMAV_HOST`/`_PORT`/`_TIMEOUT_MS`; platform infrastructure, no IntegrationCall row; live use
  needs the Owner's spend yes). With a scanner, `completeUpload` holds a matching upload QUARANTINED `scan_pending` (scan
  PENDING), then scans it inline (`scan.ts scanFile`: files.read → scanner → ONE command files.recordScan): CLEAN →
  AVAILABLE + `file.available`; INFECTED → `virus_found` + the signature (never served; the trigger refuses AVAILABLE);
  FAILED → `scan_failed`; the scanner or the store not answering changes nothing (held, words on the file). Confirming a
  held file again is "Try again" (FAILED → PENDING via files.rescan). Clustered cron `files.scanPending` (5 min) scans
  what is still PENDING after a minute. Modules that act on `status === 'AVAILABLE'` after completeUpload keep working:
  a clean file comes back AVAILABLE from the same call. Tests: `useScanner(fakeScanner())` (`scan/fake.ts`).
- Files in depth (B-W2b-160; COL-046, COL-047, COL-052, COL-058, PLT-216; `platform/files/{versions,preview,thumbs,multipart}/`,
  factory-domain `files/{versions,preview,thumbs}`): a NEW VERSION is a new StoredFile (new key: nothing stored is overwritten),
  `seriesId` = the first version, `versionNo` = max + 1; `POST /files/:fileId/versions` (+ `/versions/uploads` in parts), the
  usual confirm; when it becomes AVAILABLE (confirm, clean scan, large completion, restore) `versions/supersede.ts` supersedes
  the earlier ones IN THE SAME command (the DB checks one current version at commit) — call it from any new path that makes a
  version AVAILABLE. `GET /files/:fileId/versions`, `POST /files/:fileId/restore` (a copy naming its source). Preview:
  `GET /files/:fileId/preview` (60 s; inline for JPEG/PNG/WebP/PDF, else attachment). A portal or a link gets a PICTURE only as
  its stripped copy (`thumbnailKey`: EXIF/GPS/XMP/IPTC removed, orientation kept, not resized; `thumbs/outside.ts keyShownTo`,
  made by the worker handler `files.stripPictures` on `file.available`, or on the first outside look); HEIC is never shown outside.
  Uploads in parts: `POST /files/uploads`, `…/:sessionId/{parts,sync,complete}`, `DELETE …/:sessionId`; the plain confirm refuses
  them; cron `files.expireLargeUploads` (hourly). Copies: recordGeneratedFile writes `sourceFileId` from prepareFileCopy (FV1:01).
  Uploads from outside (portal, link) call `multipart/outside.ts assertOutsideUploadsOpen()` first: closed on staging/production
  unless FACTORY_VIRUS_SCAN=clamav (FV1:02; GATE-SPEND).
- Required documents, expiry and markup (B-W2b-177; COL-048, COL-049, COL-055; `platform/files/{required,expiry,markup}/`,
  factory-domain `files/{required,expiry,markup}`): a module's status command calls `required/check.ts assertRequiredDocuments(tools,
  { recordType, recordId, toStatus })` — BLOCK rules not met refuse the move (409 in words), WARN ones come back; satisfied by a
  CURRENT categorised version valid today on the record or its party (registry: PurchaseOrder, GoodsReceipt, ShipNotice,
  SubcontractOrder, Shipment, Party). Rules: `GET|POST /files/required-rules`, `PATCH …/:ruleId` (documents.view /
  documents.required.manage; switched off, never deleted). `GET /files/documents?recordType&recordId` (each file's category and
  expiry, each move's needs), `PUT /files/:fileId/document` {category?, expiresOn?, version} (files.upload; a new date clears the
  reminder). Cron `files.remindExpiry` (06:40 Europe/Rome): 30 then 7 days before, `file.expiry_due` (fan-out
  `expiry/notices.ts`: the version's uploader and the record's followers), a partner's shared file opens a PartnerTask
  UPLOAD_DOCUMENT (source `Expiry`, closed once the series' current version is valid beyond 30 days). Markup: `POST
  /files/:fileId/markups` (a picture: its marked copy announced as the next version, then PUT + the usual confirm; a PDF page: an
  overlay), `GET …/markups`, `GET /files/:fileId/history` (the Versions pop-up: who, uses by sourceFileId, the markup a version
  came from). Event `file.marked_up`.
- Error tracking (B-W2a-42; PLT-218; `src/observability/`): `reportError(error, context)` from the 500 handler
  (core/errors.ts), a job's last failed attempt (outbox/dispatch.ts) and a failed cron tick (core/cron.ts). Every report is
  factory-domain `observability/report` (allow-listed context, scrubbed text: no person, secret or money). Sinks:
  `FACTORY_ERROR_TRACKING` fake (local/test default: memory) | off (staging/production until a live EU service and the
  GDPR art. 28 agreement; fake is refused there). Tests: `useErrorTracker(createErrorTracker({ sink: memorySink() … }))`.

## Record threads and the timeline (`src/platform/threads/`, plan A1 §2.3, §3.7; COL-001, COL-002, COL-003)
- `Thread` (threads.prisma): ONE per record (`recordType` + `recordId`, unique per tenant), made with its first message
  by `ensureThread` (INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING: racing first posts share one row). Its company, site and party are
  the RECORD's, read by a resolver per record type in `records.ts` (`THREAD_RECORDS`; W0: Company, Site), never sent by
  the caller: a module adds its record types there with its thread panel; an unknown type is a 404. `replyToken`
  (26 lower-case base32, unique across tenants) is the reply address's secret part; it never leaves the API.
- `Message`: append-only (`@append-only`, the …_threads_guards triggers). INTERNAL_NOTE, and only it, is OFFICE; OUTBOUND
  and INBOUND are PARTNER and carry `partyId` = the thread's party (the partner role's policy key; the reference
  (tenantId, threadId, partyId) → Thread pins it), so an internal note never reaches a party. `Message.forFloor` (B-W2b-F5, set when written, hidden from the partner role): the tablets read this note too. Attachments are StoredFile
  ids of the SAME record (checked at write: AVAILABLE, the writer's files.read, and for a shared message shared with the
  thread's party); each reader sees only the attachments its own file rules allow (RLS, `audienceSees`, can()).
- Routes: `GET /threads/:recordType/:recordId` (office; partner and link: the shared messages of their party, a link only
  its record), `POST …/notes` (threads.post; `forFloor` a WorkOrder's only, else 400 `body/forFloor` in words; mentions: ACTIVE people whose roles read the thread; `GET …/people` lists
  them by name for the composer, threads.post), `PUT …/status`
  (threads.manage: close and reopen, version-guarded), `GET …/timeline` (office). Pages newest first; `nextCursor`.
- The timeline is computed at read time, not stored: the record's AuditLog rows + its thread's messages + its files
  (`availableAt`), merged by (time, id) (factory-domain threads/timeline). A change shows its action, actor, status move
  and changed field names to threads.read; its values only with audit.read over the row, money only with its grain.
- Events `thread.message_posted` (live: threads.read) and `thread.user_mentioned` (one per person; no live rule: the
  notification centre consumes it).
- The email steps write through the same seam inside their own command: `ensureThread`, `attachmentProblems(…,
  'PARTNER')`, `writeMessage` with direction OUTBOUND (the mail's delivery state is their own table; Message never
  changes) or INBOUND (`bodyHtml` sanitised, `emailMessageId` unique per tenant). The seam checks no permission.
- Portal threads (B-W2b-168; COL-059, COL-060; `platform/threads/partner/`, routes in `platform/portal/threads/`): `GET /portal/threads/:recordType/:recordId` (threads.read; a record a partner view lets the person open, else 404): the shared messages (side COMPANY | PARTNER, `mine` from the post's audit actor), the files the party sees, the composer (portal.comment, open thread, not a preview; words in the person's language; `replyByEmail` when our mail on the thread carries its reply address). `POST …/messages` (portal.comment): the thread made first in the system scope when missing (ensureThread; another party's thread refuses), then ONE command in the partner scope writes INBOUND/PARTNER/PORTAL/PARTY without an author id (the portal-frame guard), its audit row and `thread.message_posted`; a CLOSED thread 409 `thread/CLOSED` in words; lastMessageAt moved right after in the system scope. `GET /portal/threads?from=any|company`: the party's conversations. A link session does not post (B-W2b-200 decides). Email-in counts the party's ACTIVE portal people as known senders of its threads (`email-in/match.ts`).

## Notifications, followers, @mentions and tasks (`src/platform/{notifications,tasks}/`, `platform/threads/followers.ts`, `platform/email-out/digest/`; B-W2a-20; PLT-138, PLT-142, COL-006, COL-011, COL-037)
- Mentions (COL-006): `writeMessage` writes one MessageMention per person a note mentions (not its author) in the note's own command, makes them follow the record (MENTIONED, `followInCommand`: only when they have no follow row, so a muted one stays muted) and emits thread.user_mentioned. No teams before People (W2b): people only.
- Followers (COL-011, `threads/followers.ts`): `GET /threads/:type/:id/followers` (threads.read: who and why, `me`), `PUT …/followers/me` {following, muted} (unfollow deletes the row, mute keeps it muted), `POST …/followers` {userId} (threads.post; the person must be ACTIVE and read the record's thread, else 400 `body/userId`); event thread.followers_changed (live threads.read). Seam for modules: `followInCommand(tools, {type, id, companyId, siteId}, userId, 'CREATOR' | 'ASSIGNEE' | …, by)` in their create/assign commands. Labels for chips and notifications: `threads/labels.ts recordLabelOf` (one line per THREAD_RECORDS type; a floor worker never by name).
- Notifications (PLT-138/142/152): the worker's handler `notifications.fanOut` (fan-out.ts) runs the rules of `notifications/rules/*.ts` (registry: mentions, followed records' messages, Today and task assignments, quote approval requested/decided, nudges; B-W2a-33 alerts and B-W2a-23 exports add a file + one line in rules/index.ts) in ONE system command per event (idempotency key `notify-<event id>`; createMany skipDuplicates on (user, eventType, eventId): a replay makes none). QUIET BY DEFAULT: only the people a rule names, never the actor; each reader's params built from their LIVE roles (an amount only with its grain, `markMoney`), names as user ids, groupKey shared within 15 minutes per person+record+category; one `notification.created` (live home.view, names nobody) per fan-out. Office routes (home.view, own rows only): `GET /notifications` (tab ALL|MENTION|ASSIGNMENT|APPROVAL|SYSTEM, state OPEN|DONE|SNOOZED, groups folded, sentence in English + key/params with only the grains held now), `GET /notifications/counts` (the bell), `POST /notifications/read|done|snooze|wake` (ids, at most 200; snooze until null = until new activity: snoozedAt set, a newer notification of the same record wakes it at read time).
- Digest (the ONLY e-mail of a notification in W2a, off by default): `GET|PUT /notifications/digest` (a ReportSubscription `notifications.digest` of the person: on, HH:MM, weekdays, categories; version 0 = never on); clustered cron `notifications.digest` (*/15) → `runDigests`: per due subscription (Europe/Rome, once a day) the person's open notifications since the last run (≤ 7 days, ≤ 50 listed) in their language with their grains NOW, an EmailOutbound (record User, template notifications.digest, no body) from the default NOTIFICATION identity sent in-process by deliverOutbound, then ONE command: a NotificationDelivery per listed notification (SENT with the mail id, or FAILED with why), lastSentAt, event notification.digest_sent. FAKE SES locally and in tests.
- Tasks (COL-037, `platform/tasks/tasks.ts`): `GET /tasks` (scope MINE (assignee me) | CREATED | RECORD (threads.read over it); tiles OVERDUE · TODAY · THIS_WEEK · NO_DATE · DONE_7D; tile/status/q filters; ≤ 500 read), `GET /tasks/:id`, `POST /tasks` (tasks.manage; on a record also threads.post there; scope + label from the record; no assignee named = the maker), `PUT /tasks/:id` (OPEN only; checklist ticks keep who/when), `POST /tasks/:id/assign` (assignee ACTIVE with home.view + tasks.manage + threads.read over the record; they follow it, ASSIGNEE), `…/status` (DONE | OPEN | CANCELLED; closing clears the snooze), `…/snooze` (OPEN; until > now ≤ 90 days, null wakes), `POST /tasks/bulk` (DONE | REASSIGN | SET_DUE, ONE command, any refusal refuses all: 409 with `body/items/<i>`). Who sees: maker, assignee, a record's thread readers; who changes: tasks.manage among those (a record's reader also needs threads.post). Version CAS on every write (409). Events task.created|assigned|completed|changed (live home.view, ids only).

## Exception rules, stored alerts and date timers (`src/platform/{alerts,timers}/`; B-W2a-33; ANA-036, PLT-100)
- Rules (AlertRule; kinds factory-domain `alerts/kinds`: CONDITION ORDER_LATE_RISK, HOLD_TOO_LONG, MATERIAL_SHORT, APPROVAL_OVERDUE, PO_NOT_CONFIRMED; DATE CERTIFICATE_EXPIRING, INVOICE_DEADLINE): one BUILT-IN rule per kind and tenant, switched on, telling a system role (made by the evaluation when missing, `built-in.ts`; never removed). Engine `engine.ts`: clustered cron `alerts.evaluate` (*/5) and worker handler `alerts.recheck` (the kinds' `recheck` events, that record only); per kind it PLANS read-only (`evaluators.ts` → factory-domain `alerts/rules planEvaluation`) and writes only when there is work, in ONE system command under the kind's advisory lock (`store.ts lockKind`, plan made again there): raise (`raise.ts raiseInCommand`: Alert OPEN + RAISED history + `alert.raised`, one unresolved alert per `<kind>:<record>`, the next occurrence only after the rule's cooldown; several rules of a kind → the most specific place raises) and clear (CLEARED by nobody + RESOLVED history + `alert.resolved`). DATE kinds keep ONE timer per record and rule (`KIND:-Nd`, calendar or working days of the record's company through `platform/calendars/service addWorkingDaysFor`) and clear the alerts whose record left its window; their alerts are raised by the timer.
- Timers (`platform/timers/service.ts`, THE date-timer service): `setTimerInCommand` (same date kept, moved = CANCELLED MOVED + new), `cancelTimerInCommand`, `cancelTimersOfRecord`, `cancelTimersOfRule`, `applyTimerPlan`; `fire.ts` clustered cron `timers.fire` (every minute, ≤ 200 per tenant and tick, one command per timer keyed `timer-fire:<id>`; the kind's lock before the timer's row): a DATE rule's timer re-reads its record (gone → CANCELLED GONE, date moved → MOVED, rule off → RULE_OFF) else FIRED + `timer.fired` + the rule's alert; a module's timer (no rule) is FIRED + `timer.fired` for its own handler (B-W2a-52/54/55/66 reuse it).
- Routes (office; alerts.view + the record's own view permission over the alert's place, `store.ts visibleWhere/maySee`): `GET /alerts` (tiles OPEN_FOR_ME · CRITICAL · UNASSIGNED · ACKNOWLEDGED · RESOLVED_TODAY; tile/state/severity/ruleKind/ruleId/record/q/snoozed/company/site; ranked critical first then oldest; ≤ 1000 read, `complete`), `GET /alerts/counts`, `GET /alerts/:id` (history, rule sentence, ≤ 5 similar open), `POST /alerts/:id/acknowledge|assign|snooze|resolve` (alerts.handle; version CAS 409; history row with the same actor and time; assign only to an ACTIVE person who may handle it there, 400 `body/assigneeId`; snooze ≤ 30 days; resolve needs a note; refusals 409 `alert/<CODE>`), `POST /alerts/acknowledge` (bulk, all or none, `body/items/<i>/<CODE>`); `GET /alerts/rules[/:id]` (sentence en/it, numbers, place, recipient, cooldown words, last fired, fired 7 days, open alerts, DATE timers; the kinds), `POST /alerts/rules`, `PUT /alerts/rules/:id` (alerts.rules.manage over the place; a tenant-wide rule needs a grant over every company; the bell is required: e-mail alone 409 `body/channels`; a DATE rule switched off or re-numbered cancels its PENDING timers), `POST /alerts/rules/:id/test` (a dry run now, nothing saved), `GET /alerts/rules/:id/timers` (alerts.rules.manage); B-W2a-78: `GET /alerts/:id/assignees` (alerts.handle: the ACTIVE people `mayBeGiven` passes, ≤ 500 by name) and `GET /alerts/rules/choices` (alerts.rules.manage: office roles, and everywhere / the companies and sites `mayManage` passes). Notifications: `platform/notifications/rules/alerts.ts` (alert.raised → ALERT to the holders of the rule's role at the alert's place who may see it; alert.assigned → ASSIGNMENT). Today: kind RULE_ALERT with `include=ALERTS` (`modules/insights/sources/alerts.ts`); then the W1 checks ORDER_PROMISE and PO_NOT_CONFIRMED are not computed. No instant e-mail (the person's digest carries ALERT notifications).

## One approval engine (`src/platform/approvals/`; B-W2b-282; PLT-042, PLT-086, PLT-091, PLT-093, PLT-104, PLT-105)
- Tables approvals.prisma (B-W2b-276): ApprovalRule, ApprovalTask, ApprovalEvent; the …_approvals_w2b_guards triggers refuse a decision by whoever asked or wrote a request (or the office account of the floor worker who asked), the Owner included (Owner answer Q3 = A), on every path. Pure logic: factory-domain `approvals/rules` (kinds, the RuleBuilder condition checked and evaluated against a kind's facts, `chooseRules`, `approvalFingerprint`, refusals in words, `ruleSentence`).
- Kinds are files in `kinds/` (`kind.ts` the port: fields, `read` → company, site, label, summary without money, facts, the snapshot the fingerprint covers, the default change, `held`; `lock`, `recent`, hooks `onApproved|onRejected|onWithdrawn|onStale` run in the decision's command), one line each in `kinds/index.ts` (a merge point). B-W2b-282 ships PURCHASE_ORDER (the reference kind; its yes changes nothing on the order until B-W2b-305 wires send.ts).
- Seams for modules, INSIDE their own command (`engine.ts`): `askApproval(tools, { kind, recordId, requester: {userId} | {workerId, siteId}, note?, changes? })` → `{ asked: false }` when no switched-on rule in force holds (go on as today) or the PENDING request (409 when one waits on the subject; 409 when the only matching rules name the person asking); `withdrawPending(tools, { kind, recordId, byId })`; `historyLine(tools, …)` for a domain approval's own line.
- Routes (office): `GET /approvals/kinds`, `GET|POST /approvals/rules`, `POST /approvals/rules/test`, `GET|PUT /approvals/rules/:id` (written OFF; switched on by PUT active), `GET /approvals/tasks` (tiles WAITING_FOR_ME · OVERDUE · ASKED_BY_ME · DECIDED_7_DAYS, notice, re-check per pending row), `GET /approvals/tasks/:id`, `POST …/approve` (re-check first: changed → STALE committed + 409 `refusal/STALE`; then the step-up a signed rule asks, 403 `stepUp`), `POST …/reject` (an APPROVAL_REJECT code + words), `POST …/withdraw` (the person who asked), `POST /approvals/requests` (the generic ask), `GET /approvals/history/:recordType/:recordId` (the engine's lines and the domain approvals of `history/domain.ts`, newest first). Permissions approvals.view | request | decide | rules.manage; events approval.requested | decided | withdrawn | stale | rule_changed (live approvals.view). A money fact (value, threshold, from → to) only with its grain.

## Imports with a dry run (`src/platform/imports/`, plan A1 W0-22; PLT-176)
- Engine only in W0; business importers come with their modules. An importer = `defineImporter` in
  `@nexus/factory-domain/imports/importer` (key `<module>.<name>`, columns with `cell.*` types (Italian/English numbers,
  day-first dates), aliases, required, `grain` for money; `keyColumns` for upsert matching; optional `validateRow`,
  `plan`) + `defineImportAdapter` here (`permission`, `viewPermission`, `load` = the records the keys find, read only;
  `write` = one planned row inside the batch's command, audited, money marked). Add the adapter to `IMPORTERS`
  (`registry.ts`, one line); tests use `useImporters([...])`.
- Flow: `POST /imports` (importer, company, mode DRY_RUN|APPLY, sheetName, encoding, numberFormat, header `mapping`, the
  sheet announced as a file: needs imports.run, the importer's permissions and files.upload) → PUT the bytes → `POST
  /files/:fileId/complete` → `file.available` → worker `imports.planUpload`: read the sheet (`sheet.ts`: CSV UTF-8 or
  Windows-1252, `;`/`,`/tab; xlsx first or named sheet, formulas as values, macros and zip bombs refused), map headers,
  refuse money columns outside the creator's grains (`ImportJob.grains`), plan in batches (`load` in a transaction that
  is always rolled back and refused if it wrote) → PLANNED. Review: `GET /imports/:id`, `/rows` (before/after money only
  with its grain), `/errors` (CSV, never cell values). `POST /imports/:id/apply` (imports.apply, the importer's
  permission, the grains of the mapped money columns) → `import.apply_requested` → worker `imports.applyPlan`: batches
  of the importer's batchSize, each ONE command (job row locked; rows changed since the dry run SKIPPED, never
  overwritten); a throw rolls back its batch and, after the last attempt, fails the job; apply again resumes.
  `POST /imports/:id/cancel`. Handlers are in background.ts HANDLERS (`importHandlers()`).
- Permissions: imports.view (read), imports.run, imports.apply (write); admin's template has all three (owner and
  read-only follow the registry). A module that adds an importer gives imports.* to the templates that use it.
- An adapter's optional `authorize(actor, job, columns)` adds refusals (issues; never fewer checks): called at the start
  (the mapping's columns), in the dry run (the starter's live roles, two-factor taken as proved; the job fails with the
  issues) and at the apply (the person who applies). The materials importer uses it for its routes' rules.
- An adapter's optional `check(client, rows, scope, actor)` judges each row against the database (the records it names): one issue list per row; dry run = same read-only tx as `load`, issues → ERROR row; each apply batch again, for the applier → SKIPPED `changed_since_dry_run`. Use it for references a row names (opening stock does).
- Templates (`templates/`, B-W2a-41; PLT-177): `GET /imports/importers/:key/template?format=csv|xlsx&numberFormat=it|en` for any importer the caller may use (its view or write permission): the columns in the importer's order, made-up examples (`templates/examples.ts`, one list per importer key; opening stock keeps its own; the unit test reads every example through its importer in both number formats), a Columns sheet in Excel; money columns only with their grain (factory-domain imports/templates). A new importer adds its examples there.
- Guided first load (`guided/`, B-W2a-41; PLT-185): `GET /imports/first-load?companyId=` (imports.view) and `POST /imports/first-load/:step/done` {companyId, jobId?, nothing?, reason?} (imports.apply + the importer's permission): customers → suppliers → materials → openingStock → openOrders (factory-domain imports/first-load), each step's latest APPLIED import of its importer (the company's or tenant-wide) reconciled against the records now (opening stock: posted lines by their line reference, quantities per unit, the value only with the cost grain); "Done" refused out of order, without an import or with a difference, kept as an audit row `imports.firstLoadStepDone` (no table yet); open orders: "nothing to load" only.

## Modules
- Product › materials (`src/modules/product/materials/`, B-W1-09): office routes `/product/materials…` (product.materials.view | manage | cost; the standard cost has its own PUT route, money.cost to send or see it), the `product.materials` importer in IMPORTERS; the rules and unit conversions are factory-domain `product/{materials,units}`; every read names its columns (`store.ts`); no floor route (the floor reads materials through its work orders).
- Product › BOM (`src/modules/product/bom/`, B-W1-13): office routes `/product/styles/:styleId/revisions/:revisionId/bom…`, `…/copy`, `…/release`, `…/obsolete`, `PATCH …/revisions/:revisionId` and `/product/styles/:styleId/bom/compare` (product.bom.view | manage | release); every write sends the revision's `version`: lock the style row, check the revision is a DRAFT (409 first), compare-and-set the revision, then the parts (`store.ts lockDraft`); rules and math in factory-domain `product/bom` (`planLine`, `bomIssues`, `requirements`); release obsoletes the old RELEASED revision first and makes a DRAFT style ACTIVE.
- Product › BOM structure (`src/modules/product/bom-structure/`, B-W2b-223; PRD-077, PRD-078, PRD-087, PRD-106; factory-domain `product/bom-structure/{rules,check,explode,contracts}`): office `GET …/revisions/:revisionId/bom/structure|check|explosion`, `PUT …/bom/lines/:lineId/structure` {section, parentLineId, subAssembly}, `POST …/bom/structure/change` (the selection), `GET /product/styles/:styleId/used-in` (product.bom.view | manage; W1's `lockDraft` first, then the line's material and the named revision FOR SHARE and the DB trigger's own advisory lock 'factory.bom-sub-assembly'; event W1's `style.bom_changed`). THE BOM check = W1's release check + the structure's (`check.ts`; the release calls `structureBlockersInCommand`); THE multi-level reader = `modules/product/bom/explode.ts readExplosion` + factory-domain `explodeExact` (planning, kits and cost call it). W1's line writes, delete, copy and Duplicate answer 211's BomLine rules in words through `guards.ts` and carry the structure through `copy.ts`.
- Product › BOM lines in depth (`src/modules/product/bom-lines/`, B-W2b-228; PRD-083, PRD-084, PRD-099, PRD-108; factory-domain `product/bom-lines/{rules,contracts}`): office `GET …/revisions/:revisionId/bom/depth`, alternates `POST …/bom/lines/:lineId/alternates`, `PATCH|DELETE …/alternates/:alternateId`, `PUT …/bom/lines/:lineId/alternate-order`, the supply `PUT …/bom/lines/:lineId/supply` {customerSuppliedByPartyId, issueToSubcontract}, `POST …/bom/supply/change` (the selection), `PUT …/bom/output` {outputQuantity (1 only for now), outputUom PCS|PAIR|SET} (product.bom.view | manage; W1's `lockDraft` first, then the materials or party FOR SHARE; event W1's `style.bom_changed`; DB refusals of 211's guards → 409 in words, `store.ts guarded`). A customer's line has no cost and is not bought: costing/store.ts and planning/store.ts read lines through `withoutCustomerSupplied`. The subcontract kit takes the BOM's lines for the workshop: `purchasing/subcontracting/kit-lines.ts kitLinesOf` (none marked: every planned material, W2a).
- Product › routing and operations (`src/modules/product/{routing,operations}/`, B-W1-14): office routes `/product/styles/:styleId/revisions/:revisionId/routing…` (steps add/edit/remove, `order`, `copy` from a RELEASED routing), `/product/styles/:styleId/routing/compare`, `/product/operations…` (product.routing.view | manage, product.operations.manage); routing writes take the BOM's lock order (lockStyle → revision CAS → Operation rows FOR SHARE → steps; `routing/store.ts lockDraft`), a reorder parks moving steps at -seq first; rules and minutes in factory-domain `product/routing` (`routingMinutes`, `routingIssues`, `finalStepOf` = last scanned step); operations are archived, never deleted.
- Product › pattern pieces (`src/modules/product/pieces/`, B-W2b-95; PRD-219, MES-055; factory-domain `product/pieces/{rules,contracts}`, event `style.pieces_changed`): office routes `/product/styles/:styleId/revisions/:revisionId/pieces` (GET, POST, `PATCH|DELETE …/:pieceId`, `PUT …/order`, `POST …/remove` and `…/change` for the list's selection) with the BOM's permissions (product.bom.view | manage); a piece = name (unique per revision), pieces per garment, BOM line, minimum leather grade (only on a HIDE line; a retired grade only when already set), grain, sizes (empty = every size); writes take the BOM's lock order with their own `lockDraft` (words: "its pattern pieces are frozen"); `copy.ts copyPiecesInCommand` = THE copy, called by bom/revisions.ts copyRevision and styles/duplicate.ts (BOM part) AFTER the lines (`lineMapBySeq`); `guards.ts` = THE BOM hooks (bom/store.ts deleteLine: a line a piece names → 409; writeLine: a graded line to a non-leather material → 409); the cutting table reads the grade by piece name (`gradeVerdict`, B-W2b-103).
- Product › styles (`src/modules/product/styles/`, B-W1-11): office routes `/product/styles…` (list filter `draftRevision`; rows carry `releasedRevision` / `draftRevision` numbers), `/product/size-scales…` and the colour library read `GET /product/colours` (product.styles.view | manage): styles, colourways, the colour x size SKU matrix (generate, follow the range and colourways, cells on/off, GTIN), DRAFT revision shells, size scales; rules in factory-domain `product/{styles,sizes}`; lock the style (or scale) row first, then compare-and-set the child's version (`store.ts`); `Style` has a thread.
- Product › compliance (`src/modules/product/compliance/`, B-W1-20): office routes `/product/styles/:styleId/compliance` (the Compliance tab: PPE class, coverage per colourway × size from B-W1-12's gate, DoC and leaflet per language for a revision, blockers in the work-order release's words) and `…/compliance/documents…` (list, versions, announce an upload, confirm, download) (compliance.certificates.view | compliance.declarations.manage + files.upload); a DoC or leaflet is a ComplianceDocument per style, kind, language and style revision NUMBER (no styleRevisionId column yet) whose files are DOCUMENT PDFs on it (newest confirmed = current, earlier ones kept); confirm = files.completeUpload, then one command on the document: DRAFT → ISSUED (older revisions' → SUPERSEDED) or a new version, file kept 10 years; rules in factory-domain `product/compliance`; `currentDocFor(tx, styleId, revisionNo, kind, language)` (read.ts) is the shipping step's question (B-W1-49).
- Product › lifecycle, types and settings (`src/modules/product/{lifecycle,types,settings}/`, B-W2b-221; PRD-007, PRD-008, PRD-105, PRD-182; factory-domain `product/{lifecycle,types,settings}`, events `product-lifecycle/events.ts`): `GET|POST /product/styles/:styleId/lifecycle` (one move per command; product.styles.view | product.styles.lifecycle), `PUT …/replaced-by`; `/product/types…` (product.styles.view | product.settings.manage), `GET|PUT /product/styles/:styleId/type`; `GET|PUT /product/settings[?companyId]` (edit rules per state, consumption tolerance). THE edit rule: every product write calls `lifecycle/edit-rule.ts requireEditRule(tx, styleId, kind)` after the style's lock (DETAILS, SPEC, NEW_REVISION, DRAFT, RELEASE; W1's styles, colourways, revisions, bom/routing/pieces lockDraft, release, copy); `lifecycle/style-guards.ts` says the database's Style rules in words; a new style takes its type's defaults in W1's create (`types/types.ts typeDefaultsForNewStyle`). The cut consumption confirmation reads the tolerance: `production/lays/consumption-tolerance.ts`.
- Product › spec versions (`src/modules/product/versions/`, B-W2b-220; PRD-085, 166, 167, 168, 194, 195; factory-domain `product/versions/{compare,drift,lock,contracts}`; reads only, no permission or event of its own): office `GET /product/styles/:styleId/versions` (revisions with who, when, why, locked words, records made from each; product.bom.view), `…/versions/compare?from&to[&companyId]` (every part as VersionCompare's sections: revision, bom, routing, measurements, protectors, pieces; `cost` section money.cost with costing.view), `…/versions/drift` (open quotes and orders on another revision than the released one; rows per quotes.view / orders.view, `held` otherwise; each cost money.cost), `GET /production/work-orders/:workOrderId/spec/versions` (workorders.view: the banner, the change order's decision, differs, its order lines' drift at release) and `…/spec/newer` (floor at its site or office, production.scan: the tablet's banner; the decision read in the system scope). The lock: `versions/lock.ts assertRevisionOpen(tx, styleId, revisionId, part)` (409 `refusal/REVISION_LOCKED` in words; every LATER write path of a revision's part calls it after the style lock) and core/errors maps the database's part / revision guard refusal of a locked revision to that 409 (never a 500).

No new permission, no new event.

P2 answer A: the buyer decides) → useLink → audit `purchasing.confirmByLink|declineByLink|proposeByLink` → purchase_order.confirmed | declined | changes_proposed; public `POST /link/purchase-order/renew` (one 202 whatever the token, work in afterAnswer: only an EXPIRED link of a SENT order with no live link, to the old link's address while it is the order's ACTIVE contact's, 10-minute coalesce; a link session writes no message). sends only for a company whose automatic reminders are on: the Owner's switch, B-W2b-14 below); `GET /purchasing/expected` (open lines by confirmed else need date: LATE / THIS_WEEK / LATER) and the list's `?due=LATE|THIS_WEEK`, `?pastConfirmBy=true`, tiles dueThisWeek/late/pastConfirmBy; `POST /purchasing/orders/send-checks` + `send-many` (Send n: each order its own send and key). A hand-added line defaults from the order's supplier's approved source (lines.ts insertLine). Events purchase_order.revision_started | lines_cancelled | lines_closed_short | confirm_by_moved | reminder_sent.

The ladder (`ladder.ts`, rules factory-domain `receivables/reminders planLadder`): one pass per customer — stale open reminders CANCELLED (and prepared again with today's figures), due reminders PREPARED only when a timer fired (DueTimer record Invoice, key `PAYMENT_REMINDER:±Nd`, company + party on the timer: the due level's and the next step's), timers kept in step; worker handlers `receivables.ladderTimer` (timer.fired) and `receivables.ladderRecheck` (invoice.issued, payment.*, payment_reminder.*, receivables_settings.changed), cron `receivables.ladder` (06:00); read-only plan first, no command when idle. Nothing is SENT by the ladder: a person approves and sends (the database refuses SENT while switched off); e-mail on the FAKE provider locally (PaymentReminder is a thread record).

Permissions production.recuts | secondary | trolleys (shared with the floor); lists read with production.cutting.view.

- Production › lays and reconciliation (`src/modules/production/lays/`, B-W2b-116; MES-038, MES-039, MES-045, MES-046; factory-domain `production/lays/{rules,consumption,reconcile}`, events `production-lays/events.ts`: lay.changed, cut_consumption.changed, cut_order.reconciled): the table (office + floor, production.cut at the lay's site; floor readiness first) `GET /production/lays/:layId` (the lay report, its sums, the count, the fabric used, `can`), `POST …/spread` (from the lay's STARTED ticket: PLANNED → SPREADING, the allocated LayRoll rows count as spread; `layLength` = the ply on the table, ≥ the marker), `POST …/rolls` (a scanned roll — code or id — with plies (default: still needed, as far as the roll goes), splices, end loss, end returned; an allocated roll is confirmed; B-W2b-112's rollCheck / layMixRefusal; never beyond the lay's plies or the roll's length left less what other lays not yet issued take), `POST …/rolls/:layRollId` (correct), `POST …/rolls/undo` (Undo last roll), `POST …/count` {left, right} (a difference refused in words and stored nowhere: PLIES_DIFFER "Left 24, right 23. Recount before cutting.", COUNT_NOT_SPREAD; else CHECKED; B-W2b-112's end scan cuts it); a write while PLANNED starts the lay, after the count sends it back to SPREADING (counts cleared). `POST …/fabric-used` (a CUT lay; the FIRST confirmation writes CutConsumption (estimated = marker × plies counted, recorded = Σ plies × ply + end loss, confirmed) AND issues each roll's use to the cut order's work orders split by their garments a ply through inventory `issueInCommand` (W1 rules; an over-issue only the office, `overIssueReason`), key `lay-fabric:<lay>:<roll>:<work order>`, with the roll's lengthLeft/state in the same command (roll rows FOR UPDATE → lot roll locks → WO issue locks → stock): that feeds the cost ledger; later corrections change `confirmed` only, until RECONCILED). Office `GET /production/cut-orders/:id/reconciliation` (production.cutting.view: lines ordered / cut (fabric ratio × plies counted on CUT lays; leather the cell's cut log less earlier reconciliations) / recut (RECUT tickets done since the release), sizes, the material per lay in metres (estimated, recorded, used, issued, end loss, ends returned), reasons, refusal) and `POST …/reconcile` {version, lines[{lineId, reasonCode, note}]} (production.cutorders.manage: once CUT and every CUT lay's fabric used confirmed; one append-only CutReconciliation per open line, a reason (PLIES_SHORT, FABRIC_SHORT, ROLL_FAULT, MISCUT, OVERCUT, OTHER + words) exactly where cut ≠ ordered; RECONCILED). Floor-sync kinds production.lay.roll | undo | count.
- Production › cutting room board and yield (`src/modules/production/cutboard/`, B-W2b-130; MES-163, MES-302; factory-domain `production/cutboard/{board,yield,shade}`): office reads only, no command, no event, no new permission. `GET /production/cutboard?siteId=&date=` (production.cutting.view; Floor now's Cutting room: open cut orders by state, late, lays at the tables and cut lays still to confirm, cut against plan of the day in garments (`dayPlan`: due by the day, not cut before it; fabric = lays cut that day × plies counted × Σ ratio, leather = the order when its ticket ended), the cut log of the day per area unit (hides, yield), fabric of the lays cut that day (CutConsumption estimate vs confirmed), the open list cutting first ≤ 8 + `more`); `GET /production/cutboard/yield?siteId=&from=&to=` (production.reports.view; 30 days to today by default, ≤ 366: cut vs ordered of the cut orders cut in the period — reconciled lines frozen, fabric ratio × plies counted, a leather line not reconciled counts its order as `waiting` —, fabric per material, leather per unit, material, hide lot (worst first) and supplier); `GET /production/cutboard/work-orders/:id/leather` (workorders.view; each HIDE material's shade lots held / reserved / cut and `shadeSummary` under `shadeRuleFor`). Never a worker column read.

- Production › checks before a scan counts (`src/modules/production/guards/`, B-W2b-46; MES-070/097/098/114): ONE hook in the scans' report, `scans/hooks/guards.ts` (reportDecision `guarded: true`, then `guardReportInCommand` inside the report's command and `recordGuardsInCommand` after the row): the site's `ProductionSettings.quantityGuard` (STRICT default: more than the previous operation passed is held, W1's MORE_THAN_PASSED words; also a late report of a skipped operation with fewer good than the next one took, LESS_THAN_TAKEN) and `skippedOperationRule` (WARN default: counts, the team leaders are told; STRICT: W1's PREVIOUS_NOT_REPORTED, not final), WRONG_BUNDLE (the WO's line ≠ the station's and that line has a station of the kind; always STRICT); more than the bundle's live garments is never taken (W1's words); a held report is sent again through `POST /production/guards/override` (floor, the team leader's badge or name + PIN + reason tile; their own floor role holds production.scan.override; never on their own scan; wrong PIN counted on them; rate-limited per device); `components: [{ code, garments }]` on the report body and the batch item (lots of the cell's lot-traced materials; asked at the sewn operation of a SERIAL style for its PPE components); the record is the audit log (`production.scanGuardFlagged|scanGuardOverridden|scanComponentsTraced` on the scan, the traced lots also on the WO); events production.scan_guard_flagged|overridden; reads `GET /production/guards/check` (floor+office), `…/flags[/:scanId]` (production.scan.correct; no names in the list), `…/work-orders/:id/components`, `…/lots/:id/garments` (workorders.view).

- Production › an unplanned operation as a real step (`src/modules/production/extraops/`, B-W2b-82; MES-086; factory-domain `production/extraops/rules.ts` says what it is): the add writes a WorkOrderOperation row with no routing step (routingSeq null, operationId, unplannedReason, `scanRequired` false, after every operation, once per library operation), still ONE command with its audit row and event. `ScanOperation.unplanned` (scans/store `readOperations`, capacity `wipForMany`) keeps it outside the routing's order: no previous, no next, never final or sewn, up to the bundle's own garments, good only (`UNPLANNED_GOOD_ONLY`), nothing waits for it in WIP. It pays its piece rate by the library operation, costs like any scan, stays out of the estimate, the spec's minutes, planning and dispatch (`routingSeq: { not: null }` on their reads), and earns minutes in efficiency but stays out of the standard review.

Notices: `notices.ts` (ALERT to the responder team's office accounts). - Production › line targets and boards (`src/modules/production/boards/`, B-W2b-50; MES-084, MES-161, MES-162, MES-166, ANA-007; events in factory-domain `andon/events.ts`: line_targets.changed, line_target.reason_given): `GET|PUT /production/boards/targets` (a line's hourly targets of a day: production.boards.view | targets; today or later, a past day is history (409 PAST_DAY); one command, lock `factory.line-targets|tenant|line|day`; removing an hour whose reason was given 409 REASON_GIVEN), `POST /production/boards/targets/:targetId/reason` (office + floor, production.boards.reason: a DOWNTIME code + words once the hour is over and short), `GET /production/boards/hourly` (office + floor: per line the garments out of its last operation per hour = the daily report's standing scans, targets, short, calls, totals so far, pace, shift), `GET /production/boards/heatmap` (office: waiting bundles per line × operation code, levels, pile word), `GET /production/boards/tv` (office preview + signed-in floor) and `GET /production/boards/display` (PUBLIC: a DISPLAY device's token alone, `requireDevice` + `shownLine`; 409 NOT_A_BOARD for other modes): output, stations by colour/shape/word, WIP, DHU, the line's latest handover note of the day. B-W2b-66: `GET /production/boards/heatmap/cell?siteId&lineId&code` (office, production.boards.view: the bundles behind one cell, no lineId = the row of no line); `GET /production/reports/lines` carries each line's `openCalls` and `absent` (people absent today: a number only, null while the site's ATTENDANCE gate is closed or the line is under the company's minimum group).
- Production › dispatch every shift (`src/modules/production/dispatch/`, B-W2b-49; MES-022, -023, -025…-028, -031…-033; events in factory-domain `dispatch/events.ts`: dispatch.assignment_changed, dispatch.handover_written): `world.ts` is THE read of a site's day (cards = placements of DispatchAssignment rows over the published plan). Office board, operators, assign / reassign / remove, moves (`undo` in the answer), print; floor `mine`, `floor/line` (no names), reassign, handover (offline kind `andon.handover.write`). Who is in only with the ATTENDANCE gate open, standing only to `production.dispatch.manage`.
- Production › the messages on my work orders (`src/modules/production/dispatch/mywork-messages.ts`, B-W2b-71; COL-036): floor `GET /production/dispatch/mine/messages` (floor.work): the internal notes marked `Message.forFloor` (B-W2b-F5: the office's writer ticked "For the floor", or the floor wrote them; every other note stays in the office), written in the app, of the open threads of the work orders in the tablet's station queue and the day's assignments of the signed-in person, at most 14 days old and 30 rows, newest first, not their own. The floor role has no grant on threads: the read runs in the tenant's system view after the floor's checks and sends the work order, `from` OFFICE or FLOOR, the text (at most 1,000 characters, `cut`) and the time: never a name, never a file. Who read what is kept nowhere but on the tablet. Nothing reads a note's words, so the tick is the only way an office note reaches a tablet, off by default, and its line says no prices (web `record-panels` `noteMayGoToFloor`).

Readers: a group is ONE step in factory-domain `product/routing/parallel` (`stepBefore`/`stepAfter`), used by scans `previousStep`/`nextStep`/`capAt`, WIP, guards `passedTo`, dispatch `previous` and lane order; the guards' components and dispatch's material flag read the operation's own BOM lines (`componentFacts(…, operationId)`, dispatch `materialFlags(…).of(workOrderId, operationId)`, scope OPERATION | WORK_ORDER).

- Production › work orders (`src/modules/production/work-orders/`, B-W1-38): office routes `/production/work-orders…` (workorders.view | manage | release): a DRAFT from sales order lines (WORK_ORDER series; cells ≤ what is open per line × size, advisory lock per line), cells/revision only while DRAFT, the release check = THE release's check (`facts.ts checklistOf`; rules in factory-domain `production/work-orders`), release = one command (WO version bump → site release advisory lock → Style FOR SHARE → check → frozen ops/materials → BATCH number → RELEASED; 409 with every blocker first), start/complete/close/cancel, spec vs master; made-to-measure lines 409 (B-W1-17/18 deferred). Scans' `siteWip` rows carry style, due date, days late, pieces, finished and line names (SCR-073).
