- Invoicing › SDI (`src/modules/invoicing/sdi/`, `src/adapters/sdi/`, B-W1-50): routes in invoicing's routes.ts (`GET …/:id/sdi` invoices.view; `POST …/:id/sdi/send`, `PUT …/:id/sdi/correction` invoices.issue + step-up); send = start command (SdiSubmission SENDING, REJECTED: corrected XML/PDF named) → ONE gateway call `sdi.send_invoice` (key = send id) → result command; every SDI write locks the Invoice row first and `record.ts syncInvoice` keeps the invoice = what its sends decide (rules: factory-domain `invoicing/sdi/states`); notifications once per SDI MessageId via webhook (INBOUND_PROVIDERS.SDI) or the poll (cron `invoicing.sdiPoll` → `invoice.sdi_poll_due` → handler `invoicing.sdiPoll`); handlers `invoicing.sdiArchive`, `invoicing.sdiCourtesyCopy` (MC: copy on the Invoice thread); FAKE only until Q21 (`adapters/sdi/fake.ts`, markers `[sdi-fake:ns:00423]`…); tests `useSdi(createSdiRuntime({ gateway, fake }))`.
- Invoicing › shipped goods, credit notes, settlement (`src/modules/invoicing/{from-shipment,credit-note}/`, B-W1-51; routes in invoicing/routes.ts): `GET /invoicing/shipped-not-invoiced` (invoices.view: live DDTs of SHIPPED/DELIVERED shipments less issued TD01/TD24 lines per DDT × order line × size, deadlines in words), `POST /invoicing/invoices/from-ddts` (invoices.manage; one command under the DDTs' `factory.invoicing.ddt` locks: TD01 for one DDT in its 12 days else TD24 by the 15th, order prices, VAT by the rules, every issued TD02 of the orders deducted once), `DELETE …/:invoiceId/deductions/:lineId`, `POST …/:invoiceId/credit-note` (TD04 FULL | LINES | AMOUNT, reason on the causale, never above what is creditable), `GET /invoicing/orders/:orderId/settlement`; the issue adds DDT_*/DEDUCTION_*/CREDIT_* checks (from-shipment/checks.ts, in facts.ts reviewIssue) and takes the DDT locks after its row; `settleOrders` (from-shipment/settlement.ts), called at the END of allocations, reversals, deposits received, issues and SDI outcome commands (SDI: `noteOrders` then `settleNoted`), takes the `order` locks last and emits `order.fully_settled` / `order.unsettled` when the settled state differs from the order's last audit row `invoices.orderSettled|orderUnsettled` (once per change; B-W1-25's close command consumes it; `settlementOf` is its port); a TD04 on what is paid emits `invoice.refund_due`.
- Invoicing › every day (B-W2a-54; FIN-058, FIN-059, FIN-060, FIN-065; no new permission or event): charge lines = `PUT …/lines` kind CHARGE + `chargeKind` (FREIGHT | PACKAGING | SAMPLES | FEE | OTHER; no order line, size or step; VAT = the request's goods' one treatment else the buyer's goods rule: factory-domain `invoicing/charges` CHARGE_RULES, UNVERIFIED); `POST …/from-ddts` also takes `lines` [{ddtId, salesOrderLineId, size, quantity}] (a part: shipped/pickPart, each ≤ left) and `freight` (default true: each shipment's `Shipment.freightCharge` as a FREIGHT line naming its DDT, once per shipment; `freight[]` answers what was left out and why); the customer's preset (Party.invoiceTiming via sales/terms `invoiceTypePreset`) is the suggested type whenever the law allows it, each DDT's own deadline follows it (`from-shipment/deadlines.ts ddtDeadlines` → shipped-not-invoiced `deadline`, B-W2a-33's INVOICE_DEADLINE date timers in platform/alerts/evaluators.ts) and a draft TD01/TD24 answers its `deadline`; `GET /invoicing/deferred` (invoices.view: per customer × company × month the DDTs a TD24 takes, value, freight to bill, drafts, the 15th counted down; `counts` OPEN/DUE_SOON/LATE for the tile) and `POST /invoicing/invoices/deferred` {companyId, partyId, month} (invoices.manage: ONE TD24 draft of every DDT of the month still to invoice, `from-shipment/draft.ts draftFromDdtsInCommand`); `POST /invoicing/invoices/from-milestone` {milestoneId} (a schedule step through its deposit request only: the DEPOSIT step and, B-W2a-F2, a BEFORE_SHIPPING step → its TD02 draft; a balance without its request yet, ON_DELIVERY and ON_INVOICE refused in words); a TD02's line names the step whose request it invoices (`milestoneId`) and carries TP03; payment terms (`terms.ts`, Invoice.paymentTerms.source MANUAL | TERMS | ADVANCE): the office's own, else the order's library terms (one order), else the customer's (`invoiceDueOf`, the company IBAN on MP05), made again for every new total and, for TERMS, at the issue date; the issue adds CHARGE_FREIGHT_TWICE | INVOICED | CHANGED (from-shipment/checks.ts: a shipment's freight billed once, at the shipment's figure now); the settlement counts a line closed short (SalesOrderLine.closedShortQuantity) as done (`closedShort` per size).
- Credit notes from returns, refunds, the credit balance (B-W2a-61; FIN-070, FIN-127, FIN-122): `modules/invoicing/credit-note/from-return.ts` (`GET|POST /invoicing/returns/:returnId/credit-notes`, invoices.view | manage; ONE command, locks 'invoice' then 'return-line' (payments/locks.ts), one TD04 DRAFT per invoice that billed the RMA lines, NET basis, the pieces at the decided credit per piece, the billed line's VAT, InvoiceLine.returnLineId; worker handler `invoicing.creditNoteFromReturn` on return.credit_due (system; a line on a credit note is skipped: repeats draft nothing); a person issues it; `credit-note/checks.ts` RETURN_OF / RETURN_OVER / RETURN_CREDIT_OVER at issue via from-shipment/checks.ts; PUT lines and an SDI line correction of such a TD04 are refused in words); `modules/payments/refunds.ts` (`/payments/refunds…`: list, `/open` = "Refunds to make", record DUE or PAID of a credit note (capped by its invoice's overpayment less live refunds) or of a payment's unplaced part, PATCH while DUE, `/paid`, `/cancel` (payments.refund), `/void` (payments.void); version required; HARD period lock in words; settleOrders after a credit note's refund is paid or voided); `modules/payments/credit-balance.ts` (`GET /payments/credit-balance`, `POST /payments/release-overpayment` = carry a credited invoice's excess to its payments' unplaced part, newest allocations first, reversal + placed again); open-items answers `stepKind`, `suggestedAmount` (paymentId | amount, oldest first), `left`, `creditBalance`, `noInvoiceDepositAmount`; payments answer `refunded` and `unallocated` less live refunds (allocation and void count them); invoice `refundDueAmount` and the settlement count PAID refunds. Events refund.recorded|changed|paid|cancelled|voided, payment.overpayment_released, invoice.return_credit_drafted.
- Payables, payments and receivables follow-ups (B-W2b-21; W2a gaps Z8:01–Z8:10): `POST /payments/deposit-requests/:id/carry` {invoiceId?, note?} (payments.record; `payments/deposit-carry.ts`: a PAID deposit request with fiscal document NONE carried onto an issued TD01/TD24 of its order in ONE command — per live allocation a reversal + an allocation of the same payment to the invoice with `carriedFromId`; locks payments → request → invoices; the deferred `PaymentAllocation_carry_due` is mapped to 409; event payment.deposit_carried; open items `depositToCarry` / `carriedDepositAmount`); refunds take `reasonCode` (RETURN | PRICE_ADJUSTMENT | DEPOSIT_BACK | OVERPAYMENT | OTHER; absent: factory-domain payments/refunds `defaultRefundReason` from the source), PATCH while DUE; `GET|PUT /payables/settings` (payables.view | payables.settings; `payables/settings.ts`: company row wins value by value, tenant row (companyId null), else the W2a rule; the match (`matching.ts matchSettingsFor`) and approval (`sdiRuleRefusals`: LISTED, NOT_THE_BUYER) read them; accepterMayNotApprove false is refused: B-W2a-F7's four eyes stay); a VOIDED typed invoice frees its number and date (`duplicateProblem`, the SDI check); an SDI file of several invoices is recorded whole (`sdi.ts recordDelivery`: SupplierInvoiceFile + XML on it + each body with sdiFileId/sdiBodyIndex and the file's XML); what the pickup cannot record lands in the SDI inbox (`upsertInboxItem`, codes UNREADABLE … OTHER; RECORDED in the recording command; `GET /payables/sdi/inbox`, `POST …/:itemId/dismiss|reopen` payables.manage; a DISMISSED delivery is not fetched again; `GET /payables/sdi` `lastPickup.waiting` = OPEN inbox items + `inboxOpen`); settle-claim names the claim on the credit note (claimId/claimSiteId; a claim CLOSED before is matched only, event supplier_invoice.claim_matched) and the claim's `credit.matched` lists its credit notes; receivables: `GET|POST /receivables/interest-rates`, `PATCH …/:rateId` (half-year reference rates; STATUTORY interest from factory-domain receivables/interest, no row → no amount), ladder coverage per instalment, an invoice with an uncarried NONE deposit held (DEPOSIT_TO_CARRY). A HARD lock on payments answers 409 in words (B-W2a-F8's `honourPeriodLocks`; tested again here).
- Payment methods (B-W2b-F7): PaymentMethod BANK_TRANSFER | CARD | SEPA_DEBIT (factory-domain `payments/payments` PAYMENT_METHODS, the names of PaymentIntentMethod); `GET /payments?method=` keeps one; the office types bank transfers only: `method` on `POST /payments` and `/payments/deposit-received` is BANK_TRANSFER (absent: the same), CARD / SEPA_DEBIT → 400 in words (`body/method/PROVIDER_ONLY`, PAYMENT_REFUSALS) in checkPayment, before anything is written; `insertPayment` writes the checked method (audit, payment.recorded with its method): B-W2b-185 records a succeeded intent's payment through it, in the system scope. Refunds stay bank transfers.
- Paying online (B-W2b-185; `modules/payments/online/`, `adapters/{payment-fake,stripe}/`; FIN-118, FIN-123): a payment port (factory-domain `payments/online/port`) behind the gateway (`payment.create_intent | retrieve_intent | cancel_intent`; runtime `processPayments()`/`usePayments`): FAKE accounts → the fake (Stripe's words, no network), LIVE/SANDBOX STRIPE → Stripe's REST API; a LIVE Stripe account is refused until GATE-SPEND (`LIVE_CARD_PAYMENTS_ALLOWED`). Start (`start.ts`, link or portal, as the tenant's system): one live intent per payer taken up again, another payer's under way → 409 `payment/IN_PROGRESS`, CREATED → provider → WAITING (or FAILED, 409 `payment/PROVIDER`); the client secret only in the answer. Webhooks via INBOUND_PROVIDERS PAYMENT_FAKE / STRIPE (`/inbound/payment-fake|stripe/…`) → `onlinePaymentProcessor`: SUCCEEDED records ONE Payment (CARD / SEPA_DEBIT, the provider's id as externalRef) through `insertPayment` + `allocateInCommand` (what is still open), a repeated event changes nothing, a success after FAILED is still recorded once. Worker `W2B_185_HANDLERS` (`follow-up.ts`): `payments.payInvoiceTask` (one PAY_INVOICE PartnerTask per open TD01/TD24, source `PayInvoice:`), `payments.onlineFinished` (TD02 when the rule requires it, receipt mail, cancel a FAILED intent at the provider). Routes: office `POST /payments/invoices/:id/link` (PAYMENT link), `GET /payments/intents`; link `GET /payments/link/pay`, `POST /payments/link/pay/intents`, `GET …/intents/:id` (PAYMENT and DEPOSIT_INFO); portal `GET /portal/payments/open-items|statement|statement/pdf`, `POST /portal/payments/intents`, `GET …/intents/:id` (portal.payments.view | pay: customer Finance, Admin); public `POST /payment-fake/confirm` (the fake's own fields: a scenario, never card data; 404 outside local/test). Tests: `online.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
- Sales › quotes (`src/modules/sales/quotes/`, B-W1-21): office routes `/sales/quotes…` (quotes.view | manage | approve): create (number from the company's QUOTE series), header/terms, size-grid lines (`…/lines`, MADE_TO_MEASURE → 409 until W2a), `…/recompute`, `…/approval` (ask), `…/approval/approve|reject` (never the requester; a MARGIN_FLOOR request needs money.margin; decided only while the version's fingerprint is unchanged), `…/reopen`, `…/cancel`; every write is ONE command on the Quote (its version = CAS) that ends in `pricing.ts refreshVersion` (VAT re-decided from the party now, lines costed from costing's sheet only when they change or on recompute); math, rules and issues in factory-domain `sales/quotes` (`priceVersion`, `quoteIssues`, `sendBlockers` for B-W1-24); rules through the `rules.ts` seam (`quoteRulesFor`: the company's SalesSettings row, else the tenant's (id = company or tenant id), else QUOTE_RULE_DEFAULTS; `GET|PUT /sales/settings`, quotes.view | sales.settings.manage); the margin AMOUNT is dropped for a caller without the cost grain (`views.ts forCaller`).
- Finance screens' reads (`src/modules/invoicing/{related,settings}.ts`, `src/modules/payments/open-items.ts`; SCR-080 … 083): the invoice list rows also answer `paidAmount` `creditedAmount` `openAmount` (balanceOf) `dueDate` `creditOfId` `salesOrderId`; `GET /invoicing/invoices/:id/related` (invoices.view: DDTs, deducted TD02s, credited invoice, credit notes, payments placed with reversals, orders and their deposit requests); `GET /invoicing/settings` (invoices.view: per company the seller data and its problems, the SDI account, the archive count, each fiscal series' next number — finance holds neither companies.view nor integrations.view); `GET /payments/open-items?companyId&partyId` (payments.view: OPEN deposit requests + issued TD01/TD24 still open, oldest first); payments (list and record) answer `placedOn` (net per target with its number), each allocation's `label`, and `people` (names).
- Sales › send and accept (`src/modules/sales/{quotes,deposits}/`, B-W1-24): office `GET …/send-check`, `POST …/send` (quotes.send: checks (factory-domain `sales/quotes/send`) → CustomerQuote → QUOTE PDF put (FISCAL_ARCHIVE) + token minted outside the tx → ONE command: StoredFile, older SENT → SUPERSEDED, version SENT with billTo/shipTo/documentSha256/sentAt/sentById/termsVersion, QUOTE_ACCEPT link (hash only), thread mail WITHOUT the link (`mail.ts queueLinkMail`: sendEmail with email.queued withheld) → after commit `deliverLinkMail` in-process with the link + PDF), `POST …/send-link` (new link, old ones revoked), `POST …/revise` (new DRAFT copying lines/sizes/costs), `GET …/versions` (PDFs, links, evidence pack, deposit), cancel after send (reason, links revoked; firm offer refused); link `GET /link/quote`, `POST /link/quote/accept|decline|new-link` (QUOTE_ACCEPT; a link answers the version sent at or before its createdAt; accept = ONE partner command: useLink + rules `sales/quotes/acceptance` + ACCEPTED + QuoteAcceptance + quote.accepted, then system `after-acceptance.ts` (deposit request OPEN, key `accepted-<versionId>`; B-W1-25's order goes there) and the confirmation mail; worker handler `sales.afterAcceptance` is the backstop); `GET|PUT /companies/:companyId/bank-details` (companies.bank.view | manage + step-up; `companyBankDetails(tx, companyId)` for documents and the invoicing XML).
- Sales › the quote screens' reads (`src/modules/sales/quotes/{approvals,preview}.ts`, B-W1-21 part 2): `GET /sales/quote-approvals?view=waiting|mine|decided|all&quoteId=` (quotes.view; per row `mayDecide` + `held` OWN_REQUEST | NO_RIGHT | NEEDS_MARGIN | NOT_WAITING, asker/decider names, tile counts), `GET /sales/quote-numbers/next?companyId=` (quotes.manage; the QUOTE series' next number as printed, a preview), `GET /sales/quotes/:id/preview?language=` (quotes.view; the current version's PDF with the sample banner, inline, stored nowhere; 409 names what it needs), `GET /link/quote/document` (link, links.open: the sent version's stored PDF bytes for the accept page; a QUOTE_ACCEPT session reads no files), `…/versions` answers `people` (names).
- Sales › proofs signed with the quote (`src/modules/sales/quote-proofs/`, B-W2b-165; SAL-199): a proof is a StoredFile of record type `QuoteVersion` (owned.ts: only this module writes them; category PROOF; a PDF is a DOCUMENT, a JPEG/PNG/WebP a PHOTO; at most 12 per version), added (`POST /sales/quotes/:id/proofs` → PUT → `…/proofs/:fileId/complete`), given a new version (`…/proofs/:fileId/versions`, B-W2b-160's series), removed (`DELETE …/proofs/:fileId`, the whole series) or carried over from an earlier version (`POST …/proofs/carry`, copies naming their source) only while the version is unsent (quotes.manage + files.upload; the version's row `FOR UPDATE` first); `GET …/proofs` (quotes.view) per version with what the acceptance named. quotes/send.ts calls `freeze.ts`: `proofSendChecks` (PROOF_NOT_READY blocks) and, inside the send's command, `freezeProofsInCommand` (each current proof shared OFFICE/PARTNER/LINK with the party, kept ten years, audit `quotes.freezeProofs` on the version). quotes/accept.ts calls `link.ts`: `GET /link/quote` lists `quote.proofs`, the accept takes `proofFileIds` (a changed set is a 409) and writes ONE audit row `quotes.acceptProofs` (record QuoteVersion: name, SHA-256, version, size, type of each — the evidence; no schema column), `GET /link/quote/proofs/:fileId` serves one proof's bytes (a picture only as its stripped copy). An order and its work orders read the accepted proofs, never copied: `GET /sales/orders/:orderId/proofs` (orders.view), `GET /production/work-orders/:workOrderId/proofs` (workorders.view). Event `quote.proofs_changed`.
- Sales › orders (`src/modules/sales/orders/`, B-W1-25): made in quotes `afterAccept` (same command and key; `make.ts makeOrderInCommand`: SALES_ORDER series (none: the order waits, office `POST /sales/orders` {quoteVersionId}), lines/sizes/prices/costs/dates/addresses as accepted, the deposit request named); office `/sales/orders…` list, `/board` (counts by state + tiles), record (per-size progress, chain, settlement, actions), `PUT …/promise` (reason; originalPromiseDate kept), `…/ready` (reason), `…/cancel` (CONFIRMED, no released or draft work order; OPEN deposits cancelled), `…/close` (orders.view to reach; settled: manage or close; not settled: reason + orders.close); every move locks the row first (`store.ts lockOrder`, FOR UPDATE: the trigger refuses any write to a final order); rules factory-domain `sales/orders`; worker handlers (`handlers.ts`, background.ts) follow workorder.released, shipment.status_changed and order.fully_settled (a trigger only) through `moves.ts followOrder`; close guard = `settlement.ts` port: the money module's `settlementOf` read under the order's lock (`useOrderSettlement` swaps it in tests); SalesOrder has a thread (platform/threads/records.ts). For the order screens (SCR-038/039) the record also answers each deposit request's `paidAmount` (allocations less reversals, price) and latest DEPOSIT_INFO `link` {sentAt, to, expiresAt, state}, each chain invoice's `depositRequestId` (a TD02), `marginPct` (margin grain) and `people` (names of who changed a promise / made it).
- Sales › payment terms and price lists (`src/modules/sales/{terms,pricelists}/`, `modules/parties/commercial-terms.ts`, B-W2a-19; FIN-075, SAL-165, SAL-012): office `/sales/payment-terms…` (paymentterms.view | manage; rules factory-domain `sales/terms`: instalments ORDER | BEFORE_SHIPPING | DELIVERY | INVOICE + days + endOfMonth totalling 100, at most one ORDER = the deposit and its due days; archive refused while a customer uses them; method/instalments/early discount fixed once a quote version or order names them), `/sales/price-lists…` (pricelists.view | manage; rules factory-domain `sales/pricelists`: list → DRAFT/ACTIVE dated versions, one draft at a time, activate = in ONE command end the version in force the day before then ACTIVE (never back in time, never cutting a later one), prices only on a draft, one row per style + colourway, one ACTIVE BASE list per company + currency; every write locks the list row FOR UPDATE then CASes the version by hand), `GET|PATCH /parties/:partyId/commercial-terms` (parties.view | manage; a price list given also needs pricelists.manage; minimum order money.price); a new quote copies the customer's terms (printed name in its language, deposit: the customer's, else the terms' ORDER share, else the settings') and keeps its list version (`QuoteVersion.priceListVersionId`); quote lines read the list price through `pricelists/store priceChainOf` (kept version → customer list → company BASE list, then baseListId fallbacks); the deposit request and the PDF clause take the terms' ORDER days (`terms/store depositDueDaysFor`, else 7); the order made at acceptance keeps the quote's `paymentTermsId`.
- Sales › price lists in depth (`src/modules/sales/pricing-depth/`, `src/modules/sales/line-checks/`, B-W2b-325; SAL-166, SAL-167, SAL-170, SAL-178, SAL-180, SAL-183; rules factory-domain `sales/pricing-depth/`; no new permission: pricelists.view | manage): a DRAFT version's quantity breaks (`PUT /sales/price-lists/:listId/versions/:versionId/breaks`), size surcharges (`…/size-surcharges`, every style or one) and dated prices (`…/dated-prices`, keyed by style, colourway and first day; the days of one style never overlap; W2a's `…/entries` refuses a style that has dated prices), `GET …/rules` (the Breaks & surcharges tab), a style's own surcharges (`/sales/styles/:styleId/size-surcharges`), the list's `PUT /sales/price-lists/:listId/discount-display` (SAL-170: frozen.ts `customerQuoteOf({ showDiscount })` prints net only), the minimums `/sales/minimums…` (one ACTIVE rule per company, list, subject and count; BLOCK | WARN | SURCHARGE). A quote's dated price is the one that holds on its version's day (pricelists/store `priceChainOf({ entryDay })`, quotes/list-price.ts); "Make next version" copies days, breaks and surcharges (`copyVersionParts`). `GET /sales/quotes/:quoteId/list-prices` answers each line's list price, break, size surcharges and the waterfall's first steps (B-W2b-330 writes them); the line rules (`line-checks/index.ts` LINE_CHECKS, a merge point; `documents.ts` for quotes and orders) run at the quote's send checks (BELOW_MINIMUM) and an office order's plan, and at `GET /sales/{quotes/:quoteId,orders/:orderId}/line-checks`. Import `sales.priceLists` (into each list's draft) and `GET …/versions/:versionId/export?format=csv|xlsx` in its columns (price grain); export source `sales.price-lists`. Events `price_list.rules_changed`, `style.size_surcharges_changed`, `minimum_order.changed` (factory-domain `sales-pricing/events.ts`).
- Sales › the price of a line (`src/modules/sales/price-waterfall/`, B-W2b-330; SAL-173, SAL-174, SAL-175, SAL-181, SAL-189; rules factory-domain `sales/price-waterfall/rules.ts`; no new permission, no new event): `GET /sales/quotes/:quoteId/pricing` (each line's waterfall as stored and in words, "why this price?", what its list gives now, the price its cost suggests at the floor the version is held to, its margin) and `PUT /sales/quotes/:quoteId/lines/:lineId/price` (a DRAFT goods line priced LIST | SUGGESTED | MANUAL → the list's break → the options chosen → a discount → a manual net with an ACTIVE PRICE_ADJUSTMENT reason (one that needs detail: a note) → the list's size surcharges → NET, in ONE update of unitPrice, listUnitPrice, priceSteps and adjustmentReason; then W1's refreshVersion). Any other writer of a price leaves the steps as they were and the database drops them. Fees: `POST /sales/quotes/:quoteId/fees`, `PATCH …/fees/:lineId` (FEE lines: own price and times, cost 0, no waterfall; W1's DELETE line removes one; W1's money counts a sizeless FEE line by its quantity); an order's FEE line is never shipped (`orderProgress({ fees })`, shipping's backorder skips it, the settlement counts it delivered) and goes once, as a SERVICE line naming it, on its order's first invoice of shipped goods (`fee-invoicing.ts` from the DDT draft; the issue refuses a fee another issued invoice bills; lock 'factory.sales.fee' after the DDTs'); its own VAT and a credit against the bulk order wait for the accountant (`FEE_CREDIT_AGAINST_BULK`, off). `GET /sales/orders/:orderId/pricing` (the waterfalls make-order copies, the fee's invoice). The margin floor of the person quoting: `withPersonFloor` (floors.ts; called by quotes/pricing refreshVersion and quotes/views readFacts): the lowest floor of the version maker's live office roles listed in SalesSettings.marginFloorByRole (the company row's, else the tenant's), else W1's floor; `GET|PUT /sales/settings/margin-floors` (lowering one asks a step-up). Revise copies the waterfall.

No new permission (orders.manage / quotes.manage). The record adds `dailyActions`, `amendments`, `locked`, `paymentTermsId`, per line `closedShort`, per progress `closedShort`; ORDER_ACTIONS unchanged (the web checks it).
- Sales › an advance before shipping (B-W2a-F2; DPR 633/1972 art. 6 c.4: a payment received before the goods leave is invoiced then, for the amount paid): a BEFORE_SHIPPING step of the payment schedule has a deposit request of its own (factory-domain `sales/orders/schedule` `stepRequestFor`: an ACCONTO of the step's amount, due on the step's day, the deposit's fiscal rule `sales/deposits` `depositFiscalOf`), made wherever steps are written (`orders/schedule.ts insertSteps`: at acceptance, at manual entry, when an amendment makes the steps again — an unpaid balance's request is cancelled with its step, a part-paid one stays; the amendment takes the requests' money locks before the order's row, `lockStepRequests`); a step without one (made before this step, or no day) is opened by the office: `POST /sales/orders/:orderId/schedule/:milestoneId/request` {version, dueDate?} (orders.manage; refused in words for another kind, a settled step, a step with its request, goods that left, no day; event `sales_order.schedule_changed` REQUESTED). Its payment goes through W1's deposit path unchanged (`POST /payments/deposit-received` → request PAID → TD02 → handler `sales.ordersFollowDeposits` marks the step PAID); the TD02's line names the step; `POST /invoicing/invoices/from-milestone` routes it like the deposit; the goods' invoice deducts every TD02 of the order once (from-shipment/draft.ts, unchanged). Every "is the deposit paid?" read takes the deposit's requests only (`sales/deposits/deposits.ts ORDER_DEPOSIT_ONLY`: the release gate, the orders list's deposit state and filter, Today's flow); the order record's `chain.depositRequests[].stepKind` says which step a request is for.

- B-W2b-F15 (the check's gaps): each address decision (by hand or by an accept) also emits `delivery_address.checked` {addressId, partyId, decision}: only the person who asked (requestedByUserId, with portal.home.view + portal.addresses.view) is told, in their language, the address in short and a refusal's reason (`platform/portal/notifications/rules/addresses.ts`, keys `portal.address.approved|refused`, topic ORDERS; a party of every company: the company of the asker's portal session); the office party record (`GET /parties/:id`) shows each address's `approval` (status, who asked and when, who decided and when, the reason; null = ours) through `parties/store.ts addressCheckOf`, shared with the check list; the portal never names the decider. Purchasing's answer panel (`GET /purchasing/orders/:id/answer`) says `words` {it, en} and, WAITING, `waitingOn` LINK | PORTAL (portal on and the CONFIRM_PO request open) | PORTAL_OR_LINK; Today's SUPPLIER_CHANGE names a website change.
- Sales follow-ups (B-W2b-16): a return's stock outcome posts a CUSTOMER_RETURN move naming `returnLineId` + `returnPartyId` (inventory `postMoves` MoveDraft; W2a's ADJUSTMENTs keep being read by their `return-line:<line>:` key); the RMA record answers `closeWarnings` (factory-domain `closeWarningsOf`: Replace without its order, unchanged goods without a return DDT, Repair lines with fewer live repair orders than garments) and the close answers the same; back into repair after REPAIRED (`reworkRoundOf`), production's repair work order make/options let a new one follow a COMPLETED/CLOSED one (it becomes RepairOrder.workOrderId; the repair record lists `earlierWorkOrders` from the make's audit rows); orders: `POST /sales/orders/:id/schedule/:milestoneId/waive` and `…/deposit-requests/:requestId/waive` (orders.waive, stepUp risk, owner only; the request's money lock before the order's row; a part-paid request is refused), close-lines takes `reasonCode` (an active CANCEL code) and reopen clears it, typed orders' deposit requests emit `deposit_request.created_for_order`, their DEPOSIT_INFO page goes on the order's thread to the INVOICES (else ORDERS) contact (`payments/deposit-link.ts`); `PUT /sales/settings` asks a step-up when it raises a limit (`quotes/settings.ts raisesApprovalLimit`); every write of Party.priceListId keeps `PartyPriceListAssignment` (`pricelists/assignments.ts givePriceListInCommand`: the open row ends the day before, one change per customer per day, a W2a-given list's missing row written from its audit day first).
- Sales › change and cancel requests from the portal (`src/modules/sales/change-requests/`, B-W2b-183; SAL-077, SAL-078; rules factory-domain `sales/change-requests/rules`, events `sales-change-requests/events`; partner permission `portal.salesOrders.ask` (customer Buyer; the Admin follows), reads ride on `portal.salesOrders.view`, the office answers with orders.manage): partner `GET /portal/sales-orders/:orderId/change-requests/rules` (what may change or be cancelled per line and size BEFORE the click, in words: cut-offs from SalesSettings — the company's row, else the tenant's — read in the tenant scope as the person, a line whose leather was cut (CutLog not reversed) or a bundle made is held, one OPEN request per order, SAL-304's no-withdrawal words), `POST …/change-requests` (ONE partner-scope command: the rules checked inside the work, an advisory lock per order, the request OPEN + its cells, audit rows on the request and the order's timeline; a cut-off refusal is a 409 with `body/<CODE>`), `GET …/change-requests`, `POST …/:requestId/withdraw`; office `GET /sales/change-requests` (OPEN oldest first), `GET /sales/change-requests/:requestId` (the rules now + the preview: W2a's amendment diff by grain, the cancel impact, the lines a partial cancel closes), `POST …/accept` (request FOR UPDATE, then a change through `orders/amend amendInCommand` at the order's version + the new `shipTo` (refused while a DRAFT/PACKED shipment exists), a whole cancel through `orders/moves cancelOrderInCommand`, a cancel of lines through `orders/close-lines closeLinesInCommand` with the request's CANCEL code — a line a request names is never removed), `POST …/refuse` (words), `GET|PUT /sales/change-requests/settings` (the three cut-off columns of an existing SalesSettings row; sales.settings.manage). Today kind ORDER_CHANGE_REQUEST (`modules/insights/sources/portal-change-requests.ts`, APPROVAL, OPENs the order); the answer mails the customer through `platform/portal/notifications/rules/change-requests.ts` (topic ORDERS). PG tests: `change-requests.pg.vitest.test.ts`. B-W2b-F14 (SalesSettings.cancelFeePct, wave 23): the cancel fee rides with the cut-offs (`GET|PUT /sales/change-requests/settings`, `cancelFeePct` 0–100, 4 decimals, null free, left out unchanged; refused in words at `body/cancelFeePct`), factory-domain `sales/change-requests/fee` (line net × percent, rounded once at the minor unit by core/money `toMinorUnits`); shown — never charged, never stored on the request (no column): the rules (`lines[].cancelFee`, `cancelFee.wholeOrderFee`), a waiting cancel's view (`cancelFee`, null once answered) and the accept's answer (`cancelFee`, also on its audit rows); amounts are money.price.
- Sales › sample rounds (`src/modules/sales/samples/`, B-W2b-246; SAL-311, SAL-316, MES-018; factory-domain `sales/samples/{rules,contracts,permissions}`, events `sales-samples/events.ts`; perms sales.samples.view | manage): `GET|POST /sales/samples/rounds` (tiles OPEN, DUE_THIS_WEEK, LATE, WAITING_DECISION, REJECTED_THIS_MONTH, ALL + the board's lanes; numbered SAMPLE_ROUND, revision pinned (default the newest not obsolete), roundNo default next of the kind, cells checked as the DB does, charge money.price both-or-neither and never on an internal round), `GET|PATCH …/:roundId` (record with work orders, shipments, `moves`, `actions`; revision only while REQUESTED), `POST …/:roundId/move` (the four manual stages; DECIDED only by B-W2b-252's decision), `…/cancel` (why), `GET …/:roundId/work-order-options` + `POST …/:roundId/work-orders` (W1's `createWorkOrder` with its `sample` option: kind SAMPLE + sampleRoundId, the round's revision, a command on the round; also workorders.manage; REQUESTED → IN_MAKING), shipments `POST …/:roundId/shipments`, `PATCH|DELETE /sales/samples/shipments/:shipmentId` (sent/received once each; an outbound one sent → SENT, received → UNDER_REVIEW). Thread record type SampleRound. W1 refuses a SAMPLE work order's revision change and split in words; its record shows `sampleRound`. Every DB refusal of the module is a 409 (`store.ts databaseRefusal`).
- Sales › sample decisions (`src/modules/sales/sample-decisions/`, B-W2b-252; SAL-312, SAL-314, PRD-070, PRD-158; factory-domain `sales/sample-decisions/{rules,contracts,permissions}`, events `sales-sample-decisions/events.ts`; perms sales.samples.decide | measure, the CE result compliance.certificates.manage): `GET …/rounds/:roundId/approval` (decision as recorded, the brand's latest request, swatches, CE test sample, change requests, `actions` with `needs`), `POST …/:roundId/decision` (office: not REQUESTED, not while an OPEN request waits), `…/next-round` (after REJECTED: B-W2b-246's create with the same kind, brand, cells), `…/test-result` (CERTIFICATION; result and a certificate covering the style, each once); `GET|PUT …/:roundId/fit` (the pinned revision's LIVE spec, DRAFT or APPROVED, × the round's sizes; nominal/tolerances frozen when a cell is first written; the point checked against the spec: SampleMeasurement.pointOfMeasureId has no FK), comments `POST …/:roundId/comments`, `PATCH|DELETE /sales/samples/comments/:commentId` (photos = files of record type SampleComment); swatches `GET|POST /sales/samples/swatches`, `PATCH|DELETE …/:swatchId`, `POST …/:swatchId/decision`. The brand is asked with POST /approvals-out (SAMPLE on SampleRound; COLOUR | LEATHER on SampleSwatch, its own kind): `approvals.ts` adapters (onAsked names the request; settle, in the system scope, copies the answer: decidedVia PORTAL | LINK, decidedAt = answeredAt, a round DECIDED; a round or swatch decided meanwhile keeps its decision).
- Sales › the brand's samples and tech packs in the portal (`src/modules/sales/portal-samples/`, B-W2b-262; PRD-245; rules factory-domain `sales/portal-samples/{rules,permissions}`; partner permission `portal.samples.view` (customer Buyer, Quality, Designer, Viewer; the Admin follows; not Finance)): `GET /portal/samples` = `waiting` (the party's OPEN approval requests of kind SAMPLE | COLOUR | LEATHER | ARTWORK on SampleRound | SampleSwatch | Style, overdue first; null without portal.approvals.view; answered through B-W2b-184's `/portal/approvals/:id` + `/answer`, settled on the round by B-W2b-252's adapter), `styles` (the party's own private-label styles in development or with a round open: stage, latest round, next date, the BRAND tech pack versions with `scanVerdict` against the party's own), `rounds` (status in the brand's words, the outbound shipment, https tracking only); `POST /portal/samples/tech-packs/:documentId/download {mode}` (a TECH_PACK GeneratedDocument with the BRAND layout, the portal's company, of a style whose brandPartyId is the session's party — anything else 404; ONE partner-scope audit `portal.samples.techPackOpen` on the StoredFile, then presigned 60 s as the tenant's system). Partner reads in the party scope; styles, revisions and versions in the tenant's scope as the person. No money, no instructions, no office person. No event, no task source (the APPROVE task is the request's own). PG tests: `portal-samples.pg.vitest.test.ts`.
- Sales › inquiries (`src/modules/sales/inquiries/`, B-W2b-295; SAL-032, SAL-033, SAL-305; rules factory-domain `sales/inquiries/{rules,contracts,permissions}`, events `sales-inquiries/events.ts` (inquiry.received | changed | closed, live sales.inquiries.view); perms sales.inquiries.view | manage (sales)): `GET|POST /sales/inquiries` (tiles NEW, WAITING_US (open, waiting for us over one working day on the company's calendar, `platform/calendars`), WAITING_CUSTOMER, QUOTED_THIS_WEEK, MINE, OPEN, QUOTED, DECLINED, LOST, ALL; numbered INQUIRY; a customer or a prospect with its lawful basis; lines by style or words, pieces by size), `GET|PATCH …/:inquiryId` (the record: lines, the mail as sent, files and the tech pack in force (each named file as its series' current version), the quote, `actions`; PATCH replaces the lines, matches a prospect to a customer once (its waiting mail then joins the thread), the note in any state), `POST …/:inquiryId/assign` (+ `POST /sales/inquiries/assign` all or none; the assignee an ACTIVE office person who keeps inquiries there, live roles), `…/ask-info` (NEED_INFO), `…/resume`, `…/decline` (+ bulk; OTHER in words), `…/lost`, `…/quote` (ONE command: W1's `duplicateInto` from `{ inquiryId }` with `emptyAllowed`, action quotes.fromInquiry; lines with style + colourway + sizes at the list price or 0, the others `leftOut` in words; the wanted date as promise date; QUOTED + quoteId), `GET|POST /sales/inquiries/from-mail/:inboundEmailId` (email.triage too; the sender's party from email in's suggestion, else a prospect from From; one per mail; a customer's waiting mail joins the inquiry's thread MATCHED/MANUAL in the same command, `join.ts`; attachments not copied, named as the tech pack as they are), `GET /sales/inquiries/source?quoteId|salesOrderId` (the inquiry a quote or order came from, its tech pack in force), `GET /sales/inquiries/assignees` (by name, nobody counted). Thread and label record type Inquiry; search indexer `platform/search/indexers/inquiry.ts` (never the request words or the prospect's person); the list's export source `export-source.ts` is registered with the page (B-W2b-322). Every DB refusal a 409 (`store.ts databaseRefusal`).
- Sales › credit limits (`src/modules/sales/credit/`, B-W2b-304; SAL-148, SAL-237, SAL-241, SAL-244; rules factory-domain `sales/credit/{credit,permissions}`, events `sales-credit/events.ts`): the limit is the live PartyCompanyTerms row's creditLimitAmount (none: no check, as before). Every check is ONE CreditCheck row (append-only) written in the step's own command: the quote sent (`quoteCreditInCommand`, never holds), an order made (`planOrderCredit` BEFORE the row: a HOLD makes it PENDING_APPROVAL, then `recordOrderCredit`; makeManualOrderInCommand's 4th argument is the step ORDER/PORTAL/API, null for a replacement), a work order's release and a shipment leaving (`creditGateInCommand`: PASS/WARN written, a HOLD throws CreditHeldError 409 and `withCreditHolds` writes it with its request after the rollback). HOLD only where the company's CREDIT_OVERRIDE approval rules (switched on, condition on the check's facts) hold; otherwise WARN. The override is the CREDIT_OVERRIDE kind (`platform/approvals/kinds/credit-override.ts`, subject = the HOLD check): its yes writes the override check and a waiting order goes on; `POST …/records/:type/:id/check` checks again, `POST …/checks/:id/ask` asks, `PUT …/limits/:party/:company` changes a limit (held orders checked again); a `payment.allocated` handler re-checks waiting orders. Exposure = open orders not invoiced (gross − invoiced lines − paid deposits) + open invoices (receivables' openInvoices), in the limit's currency.
- Insights › Today (`src/modules/insights/`, B-W1-52; ANA-037, SCR-029/031): office `GET /home/today` (ranked needs-attention rows: why mine, what waits, due, figures by grain; tiles per kind; `kind`, `companyId`, `siteId`, rank-key `cursor`, `limit`), `GET /home/flow` (the role's lenses: records per stage, late, oldest) and `GET /home/waiting-on-others` (my approval requests, POs and quotes not answered) (home.view; read only, nothing stored: computed from the records at read time); one source per kind in `sources/` reads only its view permission's rows (scopeWhere) through the owning modules' reads (work orders `checklistOf`, scans `readCorrectionRequests`/`readRefusals`, invoicing `shippedNotInvoiced`/`readCopies`/`filesPending`, register `readEntries`/`traceOf`, privacy `privacyOverview`, product compliance `styleCompliance`); factory-domain `insights` decides who a row is for (`actingPermission`: view + an act permission over the record, four eyes, the decision's grain), ranks (severity, due, age, key), counts, words, and `live` events per kind; a new kind = its rule in `insights/attention` KIND_RULES + one source. Actions on an ALERT row (B-W1-52 actions; `actions.ts`, storage only in `actions-store.ts`): `POST /home/today/rows/:key/assign|resolve|acknowledge` {condition, version, assigneeId | note} (the row is re-read from its kind's source FOR the person: not on their Today = 404; an approval or a mail = 409 `action/NOT_AN_ALERT`; checks under the AttentionAction row lock inside ONE command: 409 `action/CHANGED|ALREADY_RESOLVED|ALREADY_ACKNOWLEDGED|ALREADY_ASSIGNED|NOT_ASSIGNED`, version CAS; an assignee must be ACTIVE, hold home.view and pass actingPermission with their live roles; a resolve note 3-1000 chars), `GET …/assignees` (who may act, responsible first), `GET /home/today/handled` (my verbs today + quote approvals I decided), `GET|PUT /home/today/layout` (Arrange Today, own person only); a resolution holds for the row's CONDITION (factory-domain `conditionOf`: kind, state, due, since, the source's `condition` codes): the row leaves Today for everyone until the condition changes; GET /home/today lays the stored actions over the rows (`condition`, `actions`, `handled`); events attention.assigned|resolved|acknowledged (live: home.view).
  W2a (B-W2a-20): `POST /home/today/rows/:key/snooze` {condition, version, until | null} (an ALERT row, for everyone, until a time or (null) until its condition changes; the AttentionAction snooze and its SNOOZED log row in ONE command; Handled today leaves SNOOZED out; event attention.snoozed; rows carry `actions.snoozable`), `GET /home/today?include=TASKS,MENTIONS` (the person's own kinds TASK_DUE (my open tasks due today or overdue, not snoozed) and MENTION (my MENTION notifications still open; key `MENTION:<record>:<notification>`), categories TASK/MENTION, extra tiles DUE_TODAY · OVERDUE · MENTION, `due=TODAY|OVERDUE`; without `include` the answer is W1's, so screens built before stay valid), `POST /home/waiting-on-others/:key/nudge` (an APPROVAL_REQUEST I asked for, once a business day per record: a SYSTEM note on the record's thread naming me + audit `insights.nudge` (what once-a-day reads) + attention.nudged → the fan-out tells who may decide; a PO or a quote waits on a real party: 409 `nudge/OUTSIDE`; items carry `nudge {allowed, refusal}`), `GET /home/coming-up` (my next five working days by the site/company calendar (platform/calendars; none: Mon–Fri): tasks due, promise dates of open orders I act on, PO deliveries I act on, certificates I keep that expire). Assigning a row makes the assignee follow its record (ASSIGNEE) and attention.assigned tells them (notifications).

## Documents and printing (`src/platform/{documents,print}/`, `src/adapters/print-relay/`, plan A1 §3.9; GAP-007, GAP-008)
- Documents: the issuing module builds its frozen document (`@nexus/factory-domain/documents`: QUOTE, PURCHASE_ORDER,
  DDT, INVOICE_COPY; it, en) and calls `storeDocumentPdf(processFiles(), caller, { companyId, recordType, recordId,
  document })` after its own permission check: the company's c.c. art. 2250 data comes from its Company row, never a
  request; `composeDocument` refuses an incomplete document (legal data, DPR 472/1996 for a DDT, totals that do not add
  up); `pdf.ts` paints it with pdfkit's standard fonts (same input, same bytes); `files.storeGeneratedFile` puts it in
  the store (gateway op files.put) and records it AVAILABLE in one command `documents.render` — FISCAL_ARCHIVE (ten
  years) for DDTs and invoice copies, DOCUMENT otherwise. The invoice PDF is a courtesy copy and says so (the legal
  invoice is the FatturaPA XML). `GET /documents/sample` (documents.view) previews each layout with a company's data.
- Labels: `@nexus/factory-domain/labels` composes HIDE, LOT, BUNDLE and CARTON labels (the scannable id must be the
  tenant's, of the label's kind) and makes ZPL (`^BC` Code 128 + the id as text); `documents/label-pdf.ts` draws the same
  label as a PDF with Code 128 bars (`POST /print/labels/pdf`, the browser's fallback, stored nowhere).
- Station label printer (MES-184; `stations.ts`): `Station.labelPrinterId` names a LABEL_ZPL printer of its site (not archived; a trigger holds it), where its floor devices' bundle tickets print when they name none; `GET /print/stations` (office+floor, print.view), `PUT /print/stations/:stationId/label-printer` {printerId|null, version} (print.manage, audited).
- Printing: `Printer` (site, kind LABEL_ZPL | A4_PDF, relay, relay target, label stock, ACTIVE | PAUSED | ARCHIVED) and
  `PrintJob` (print.prisma). `POST /print/jobs/label|document` → `print.queue` (QUEUED + event print.job_queued) → the
  worker's `printDispatchHandler()` (in background.ts HANDLERS) hands it to the printer's relay → SENT
  (`print.markSent`); a refused try is counted (`print.recordAttempt`) and retried with backoff; FAILED after 5 tries or
  a final refusal (`print.fail`); a relay's report → PRINTED or FAILED (`applyRelayReport`); `POST
  /print/jobs/:jobId/retry` queues a FAILED job again. A floor device prints and lists only at its own site (row-level
  security; an idempotency key is required).
- Relays: `adapters/print-relay/port.ts` (submit is idempotent by job id). Only FAKE exists (`fake.ts`: records, sends
  nothing; local and test only): which relay a site gets is the Owner's question (A1 §9 q. 5). Without a relay the
  browser prints the PDF. Tests: `usePrintRelays(printRelays({ FAKE: fakePrintRelay({ environment: 'test' }) }))`.
- Permissions: documents.view; print.view, print.labels, print.documents, print.manage (printers).

## Email out (`src/platform/email-out/`, `src/adapters/ses/`, plan A1 §3.3, §3.7; PLT-139, PLT-149, PLT-230, COL-024, GAP-005)
- Tables (email.prisma): EmailIdentity (a company's verified SES domain: kind CONVERSATION for record mail, NOTIFICATION
  for sign-in mail; From-line policy COMPANY | PERSON_VIA_COMPANY | PERSON_ADDRESS; reply domain; its SES account; the
  company's default per kind), EmailOutbound (who, from which identity, subject, Message-ID, In-Reply-To/References,
  status QUEUED → SENDING → SENT → DELIVERED / BOUNCED / COMPLAINED, or FAILED; NO body), Suppression (tenant-wide).
  The …_email_guards trigger keeps what a mail sends fixed and its status moving forward; nothing is deleted.
- Send from a module's command: `sendEmail(tools, { record, sender, to, cc?, bcc?, subject?, body, attachments?,
  identityId?, locale? })` (outbound.ts): ensureThread → attachmentProblems(PARTNER) and at most 10 MB of attachments
  together (MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES) → suppression check → writeMessage
  (OUTBOUND, EMAIL, `emailMessageId: <outboundId@identity domain>`) → EmailOutbound QUEUED → event `email.queued`, one
  transaction. Route: `POST /threads/:recordType/:recordId/emails` (email.send). `GET …/emails` lists them (threads.read).
- The worker's `emailSendHandler()` (background.ts HANDLERS) runs `deliverOutbound`: claim (QUEUED, or SENDING with a
  lapsed claim → SENDING, one try counted; suppression and identity re-checked) → MIME (factory-domain email/compose +
  mime: the message text, the sender's signature, the attached files (bytes read here, in the worker, through the file
  store: one `files.read` gateway call each; still AVAILABLE and shared with the party), the footer with the c.c. art.
  2250 block as the documents print it (documents/legal.ts on platform/documents `companyLegalOf`: liquidation, single
  shareholder …) and the privacy-notice slot `<FACTORY_WEB_ORIGIN>/privacy/<companyId>`; Reply-To
  `r-<thread replyToken>@<replyDomain>` read here only) → ONE gateway call `mail.send` (non-idempotent; the ledger keeps id, template, recipient count and
  size, never an address) → SENT. A failed try → QUEUED and the job retries (MAX_SEND_ATTEMPTS 5), else FAILED.
- Sign-in mail (`auth-mail.ts`, the default AuthMailer): composed in memory with the link, an EmailOutbound row without
  body (record `User`), sent at once through the same deliverOutbound from the tenant's default NOTIFICATION identity,
  never retried by a job (the person asks again). No thread, no Reply-To, `Auto-Submitted: auto-generated`.
- SES notifications (`notifications.ts`, via platform/inbound): Delivery → DELIVERED; Bounce Permanent → BOUNCED + the
  addresses suppressed; Transient → noted only; Complaint → COMPLAINED + suppressed. A later event only makes it worse.
- Identities: `GET /email/identities` (email.send), `POST /email/identities`, `POST …/:id/check`, `PUT …/:id`
  (email.manage, step-up): SES CreateEmailIdentity / GetEmailIdentity through the gateway (Easy DKIM records shown).
- Delivery log and blocked addresses (`delivery/`, B-W2a-41; PLT-149): `GET /email/delivery` (email.delivery.view, scopeWhere over the mail's company; state WAITING|SENT|DELIVERED|PROBLEM|FAILED, `about=INVOICES|LINKS`, record, from/to, `before` paging; addresses masked unless email.manage over that company; a mail that carried a OneOffLink names its purpose; counts per state over 30 days), `GET /email/blocked` (tenant-wide; BLOCKED or UNBLOCKED), `POST /email/blocked/:id/unblock` {version, reason} (email.delivery.unblock over every active company; command email.unblockAddress: the Suppression row stays, its address moved to `~unblocked:<id>:<address>` (factory-domain email-delivery/rules: no lifted column yet), audit with the reason, event email.address_unblocked). Every send looks addresses up, so an unblocked one receives mail again; a later bounce blocks it in a new row.
- Adapters: `adapters/ses/ses.ts` (SESv2 client; LIVE and SANDBOX; credentials sealed on the account, else the
  environment's), `fake.ts` (FAKE: in-memory mailbox, local and test only; never a table or a file), `sns.ts` (the SNS
  envelope parser and verifier), `testing.ts` (FakeSesTransport: the real client on a scripted transport;
  sesNotificationBody). Tests: `email.pg.vitest.test.ts` on the auth harness (`h.ses`, `h.email`, `h.files`, `h.mailer`).

## Email in (`src/platform/email-in/`, `src/adapters/ses-inbound/`, plan A1 §3.7; COL-020, COL-022, COL-023, COL-025, COL-026)
- Table (email-in.prisma): InboundEmail, office only: what SES said (account, receipt, SES id, S3 bucket and key, envelope,
  SPF/DKIM/DMARC/spam/virus verdicts; never changes), what the mail held (From, To, Cc, subject, Message-ID unique per
  tenant, In-Reply-To, References, size, text, sanitised HTML; written once, when read), its attachments (JSON: kept ones
  with their object and file, skipped ones with why), status RECEIVED → FETCHED → MATCHED | TRIAGE | HELD | REJECTED, or
  FAILED; thread, message, matchedBy (TOKEN, HEADERS, SENDER, MANUAL), held and reject reasons, the sender's party and
  contact (a suggestion while it waits). The …_email_in_guards trigger holds all of it; nothing is deleted.
- Receive (`receive.ts`, inside the inbound ledger's processing command): SES's "Received" notification → ONE row
  RECEIVED + `email.inbound_received` (a virus verdict: REJECTED, never read); the same SES id or Message-ID again adds
  nothing. Other SES notifications go on to email out's processor (`emailInProcessor(next)`, inbound/providers.ts).
- Fetch (`fetch.ts`, worker handler `emailIn.fetch`): the raw mail through the gateway (`mail.fetch_inbound` on the SES
  account: FAKE → `adapters/ses-inbound/fake.ts` FakeMailBucket, in memory; LIVE/SANDBOX → `s3.ts`, GetObject from the
  account's `settings.inboundBucket` only; at most 40 MB) → postal-mime (`parse.ts`) → sanitised HTML and text
  (factory-domain email-in/sanitise) → each attachment an ATTACHMENT file may hold is put in the file store (files.put);
  inline pictures, other types, empty and too many are listed as skipped → command FETCHED + `email.inbound_fetched`.
  A retryable failure is counted and retried (5 tries), a final one (object gone or too large, unreadable, no file store)
  makes the mail FAILED.
- Sort (`sort.ts`, handler `emailIn.sort`, ONE command): the facts (`match.ts`: thread by `r-<token>@<a tenant reply
  domain>`, else In-Reply-To/References against OUTBOUND Message-IDs; the sender known to the thread: a contact of its
  party, a recipient of its mail, or at a contact's company domain; else the one party the sender is a contact of and
  its `Party` thread) → factory-domain `sortMail`: virus or duplicate Message-ID → REJECTED; DMARC FAIL or spam → HELD;
  thread + unknown sender → HELD; closed thread → HELD; thread without party → TRIAGE; else MATCHED: `writeMessage`
  INBOUND EMAIL (sanitised HTML, the mail's Message-ID) with the files on the thread's RECORD shared with its party; a
  known contact without a thread joins its party's thread only with DMARC PASS and a `Party` thread resolver (the parties
  module's; none in W0: TRIAGE with the party suggested). HELD and TRIAGE keep their files on the mail (office only).
  Event `email.inbound_sorted` (live: email.triage).
- "Mail to sort" (A2 SCR-019; email.triage over the mail's company; `triage.ts`): `GET /email/inbound` (TRIAGE, HELD,
  FAILED, or `?status=`), `GET /email/inbound/:id`, `POST …/:id/assign` (record with a party; threads.post over it),
  `POST …/:id/release` (HELD: into the thread it named, also a closed one, else TRIAGE), `POST …/:id/reject`
  (MARKED_SPAM | DISCARDED). Version-guarded commands; assign and release copy the mail's files onto the record (files.read
  + files.put before the command: a file's record never changes). Answers never hold the raw mail, its S3 place, a reply
  token or a storage key.
- Tests: `email-in.pg.vitest.test.ts` on the auth harness (`createEmailInRuntime({ gateway, fake: FakeMailBucket, s3 })`,
  `testing.ts`: `receivedNotificationBody`, `rawMail`), `parse.vitest.test.ts`, `adapters/ses-inbound` (FakeS3Transport).

## Rules
- Configuration only through `src/env.ts`. It reads ONLY `apps/factory-api/.env`, by explicit path, and ONLY
  `FACTORY_` variables: never the root `.env` (production credentials on the Owner's Mac), never `DATABASE_URL` or
  `REDIS_URL`. `scripts/factory/check-env-loader.mjs` enforces it.
- `FACTORY_DATABASE_URL` is the runtime login `factory_app`; the boot audits it and refuses a superuser or owner.
- `FACTORY_ENV=local` or `test`: the database and Redis must be loopback, or the API refuses to start.
  `staging` and `production` start only on Railway (`RAILWAY_ENVIRONMENT`, set by the platform, never by the file),
  with an https `FACTORY_WEB_ORIGIN` and a real `FACTORY_COOKIE_SECRET` (32+ characters, not the local placeholder).
- A document number comes only from `allocateNumber(tools, { companyId, kind, code? }, { type, id, date? })` (`src/platform/numbering/allocate.ts`), called in the issuing command's work: it locks the series row, so numbers have no gap and never repeat. A floor command takes numbers of the floor's document kinds only (factory-domain `FLOOR_DOCUMENT_KINDS`: GOODS_RECEIPT, LOT, INSPECTION, RELEASE, and since W2b HOLD and NCR — the inspection bench numbers its hold and NCR in the tablet's own command, B-W2b-17), of its own site's company, from the same series (403 in words otherwise; the ledger's allocator is the tablet, never the worker); the database holds the same wall (the floor's policies see only those kinds' series and numbers, `onlyValues` in ownership/numbering.json; the …_numbering_floor_documents_guards triggers refuse a fiscal series, another company, a skipped number, a series change other than a new year). A number unique in the tenant (lot, inspection, release) also calls `assertNumberUniqueInTenant` (409 in words when another company's series printed it).
- A new year needs no new series (B-W2b-F16): `allocateNumber` makes a YEARLY series' row of the year from its newest one, from 1, on the first number dated in it (office, system and tablet alike); `platform/numbering/new-year.pg.vitest.test.ts` proves it 2026-12-31 → 2027-01-01 for every series the numbering seed makes. Approval requests (`platform/approvals-out`) send codes (kind, status, dueAt) and the record's label as `words` {it, en} (an adapter's `ApprovalRecord.label` is both languages); no kindName/stateWords/dueWords.
- A scan id (BUNDLE `BN-…`, HIDE `HD-…`, GARMENT_SERIAL `SN-…`) comes only from the tenant's ONE counter per kind (`src/platform/numbering/scan-ids.ts`, NumberBlock ledger; never a company series): online `allocateScanIds(tools, kind, count)` (office, system and floor commands; gaps allowed); a tablet's offline labels from its own DEVICE blocks (`GET /numbering/floor/blocks`, `POST /numbering/floor/blocks/top-up`; office `/numbering/blocks…` reserve, close) recorded with `useScanIds(tools, kind, [{ code, record }])` inside the command that stores them (409 `scanIds/<i>/<CODE>` when outside the tablet's open blocks or used before).
- `testAuthenticator()` is the only way to give a test a session; `buildApp` refuses it unless FACTORY_ENV=test.
- Tests refuse to start unless `FACTORY_DATABASE_URL` is loopback and names `factory_test…` or this worktree's
  `factory_<branch>` (`src/testing/global-setup.ts`), even tests that never connect.
- Local stack: PostgreSQL 17 on 127.0.0.1:5442 and Redis on 127.0.0.1:6399 (`infra/factory/docker-compose.yml`,
  started once from a fixed folder, never from a worktree). Never Commerce's 5432/6379 or Commerce's databases.
- The worker and scheduler run only against this worktree's own loopback database: with FACTORY_ENV local or test,
  `background.ts` refuses any other database name before it connects, and env.ts refuses a missing
  FACTORY_QUEUE_PREFIX (the local Redis is shared; Railway defaults it to `factory`).
- Every event is declared before it is emitted (`check-event-contract`); every schedule is a `defineCron` job
  (`check-cron-clustered`: node-cron only in core/cron.ts, no other scheduler library, no BullMQ repeatable job).
- The Prisma CLI runs only from `packages/factory-db`.
- Vitest runs `src/**/*.vitest.test.ts`; `*.pg.vitest.test.ts` need the local stack.
